`README.md`:

```md
# Unmida

Unmida is a Windows command-line unpacker for x86 and x64 executables and DLLs protected with Themida.

Unmida runs the target under a debugger and waits for the protector to unpack it in memory. For native targets it locates the original entry point (OEP), reconstructs the import address table (IAT), and writes a new Portable Executable (PE) file. Use it on binaries you are authorized to analyze. Run untrusted targets in an isolated Windows VM.

## Build from source

Install [Go 1.25.4 or newer](https://go.dev/doc/install) and run the following from the project root in PowerShell.

```powershell
.\build.ps1
```

The script produces `Unmida32.exe` (`windows/386`) and `Unmida64.exe` (`windows/amd64`) in the project root with CGO disabled. The instruction decoder comes from `golang.org/x/arch`, pinned in `go.mod`.

## ScyllaHide setup

ScyllaHide helps the debugger handle Themida's anti-debugging checks. Keep the supplied [`scylla_hide.ini`](scylla_hide.ini) beside the Unmida executable.

For **x64**, download the compatible ScyllaHide bundle from the upstream release asset.

```powershell
curl.exe --fail --location --header 'Accept: application/octet-stream' `
  --output scyllahide-x64.zip https://api.github.com/repositories/441734472/releases/assets/420483178
```

Extract `InjectorCLIx64.exe` and `HookLibraryx64.dll` from the archive and place them next to `Unmida64.exe`. This injector meets the minimum PE timestamp of `0x6484FEF9`.

For **x86**, Unmida has built-in anti-debugging workarounds. To use ScyllaHide, copy `InjectorCLIx86.exe` and `HookLibraryx86.dll` from the [ScyllaHide releases](https://github.com/x64dbg/ScyllaHide/releases) next to `Unmida32.exe`.

## Unpack a file

Choose the Unmida executable that matches the target's architecture. Pass the target path directly or prefix it with `/unpack`.

```powershell
.\Unmida32.exe 'C:\samples\target32.exe'
.\Unmida64.exe /unpack 'C:\samples\target64.dll'
.\Unmida32.exe 'C:\samples\target32.exe' --no-data --parameters '--mode demo'
.\Unmida64.exe --help
```

- `--parameters <command line>` passes one string of arguments to the target EXE.
- `--no-data` skips the automatic `.rdata` and `.data` reconstruction attempted for eligible native PE32 dumps.

The dump normally goes beside the input with `U` inserted before the extension, for example `target.exe` becomes `targetU.exe` and `target.dll` becomes `targetU.dll`. If the output path is denied, Unmida tries the current working directory. An existing file at the chosen output path can be overwritten.

Exit codes are `0` for success or requested help, `1` for an operation failure, and `2` for invalid arguments or an invocation without arguments. Progress goes to standard output and errors go to standard error.

## Post-processing PE32 dumps

| Command | Effect |
| --- | --- |
| `/mkdata <file>` | Reconstructs `.rdata` and `.data` for eligible PE32 images, including certain MSVC builds. Updates the file in place. |
| `/shrink <file>` | Removes sections that the shrink heuristics consider unused. Updates the file in place. Use a copy because the operation can break non-MSVC images or code that still relies on virtualized sections. |
| `/dump <PID> <file>` | Copies the code region of a running process's main module into a copy of the supplied PE32 dump. The process must match the supplied image. For `targetU.exe`, the output is `targetU.novm.exe`. |

```powershell
.\Unmida32.exe /mkdata 'C:\samples\targetU.exe'
.\Unmida32.exe /dump 1234 'C:\samples\targetU.exe'
.\Unmida32.exe /shrink 'C:\samples\targetU.exe'
```

## Limitations

- Results depend on the Themida version, protection settings, target compiler, and Windows environment. Verify the dump against your target.
- Virtualized code and VM anti-dump protections can leave a dump unable to run.
- DLL dumps may lack relocation information and fail to load if their preferred base address is unavailable.
- Windows compatibility shims can interfere with tracing. Turn off compatibility mode for Unmida and the target.

## License

Unmida is distributed under the [GNU General Public License, version 3](LICENSE).

```

`build.ps1`:

```ps1
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$previousGoOs = $env:GOOS
$previousGoArch = $env:GOARCH
$previousCgo = $env:CGO_ENABLED
Push-Location $PSScriptRoot
try {
    $env:GOOS = 'windows'
    $env:CGO_ENABLED = '0'
    $env:GOARCH = '386'
    go build -trimpath -o Unmida32.exe .
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Unmida32 build failed' }
    $env:GOARCH = 'amd64'
    go build -trimpath -o Unmida64.exe .
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Unmida64 build failed' }
} finally {
    $env:GOOS = $previousGoOs
    $env:GOARCH = $previousGoArch
    $env:CGO_ENABLED = $previousCgo
    Pop-Location
}

`pkg/antidump/antidump.go`:

```go
package antidump

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"unmida/pkg/log"
	"unmida/pkg/winapi"
	"syscall"
)

type AntiDumpFixer struct {
	hProcess  syscall.Handle
	imageBase uintptr
}

func NewAntiDumpFixer(hProcess syscall.Handle, imageBase uintptr) *AntiDumpFixer {
	return &AntiDumpFixer{
		hProcess:  hProcess,
		imageBase: imageBase,
	}
}

func (a *AntiDumpFixer) RedirectOEP(oep, iat uintptr) error {
	var displBuf [4]byte
	if _, err := winapi.ReadProcessMemory(a.hProcess, oep+1, displBuf[:]); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("AntiDumpFixer: failed to read displacement from OEP+1: %v", err)
	}
	displ := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(displBuf[:])

	k32, err := winapi.GetModuleHandle("kernel32.dll")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("AntiDumpFixer: failed to get kernel32 handle: %v", err)
	}
	vprotectAddr, err := winapi.GetProcAddress(k32, "VirtualProtect")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("AntiDumpFixer: failed to find VirtualProtect: %v", err)
	}

	var iatData [512 * 4]byte
	if _, err := winapi.ReadProcessMemory(a.hProcess, iat, iatData[:]); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("AntiDumpFixer: failed to read IAT: %v", err)
	}

	var vprotectIAT uint32
	for i := 0; i < 512; i++ {
		addr := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(iatData[i*4 : (i+1)*4])
		if uintptr(addr) == vprotectAddr {
			vprotectIAT = uint32(iat + uintptr(i*4))
			break
		}
	}

	if vprotectIAT == 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("VirtualProtect not found in IAT")
	}

	var lfaNewBuf [4]byte
	if _, err := winapi.ReadProcessMemory(a.hProcess, a.imageBase+0x3C, lfaNewBuf[:]); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("ReadProcessMemory failed for lfaNew: %v", err)
	}
	lfaNew := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(lfaNewBuf[:])

	var epBuf [4]byte
	if _, err := winapi.ReadProcessMemory(a.hProcess, a.imageBase+uintptr(lfaNew)+0x28, epBuf[:]); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("ReadProcessMemory failed for AddressOfEntryPoint: %v", err)
	}
	entrypoint := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(epBuf[:])
	optHdrEntrypoint := uint32(a.imageBase + uintptr(lfaNew) + 0x28)

	pushArgs1 := []byte{0x6A, 0x00, 0x54, 0x6A, 0x04, 0x68, 0x00, 0x04, 0x00, 0x00, 0x68}
	pushArgs2 := []byte{0x54, 0xFF, 0x74, 0x24, 0x04, 0x68, 0x00, 0x04, 0x00, 0x00, 0x68}

	// Calculate size of new code
	// pushArgs1 (11) + pushImgBase1 (4) + callInstr1 (2) + vpAddr1 (4)
	// + movInstr (2) + optHdrEP (4) + entrypoint (4)
	// + pushArgs2 (11) + pushImgBase2 (4) + callInstr2 (2) + vpAddr2 (4) + pop (1)
	// + jmp (1) + jmpDispl (4)
	// Total = 11+4+2+4 + 2+4+4 + 11+4+2+4+1 + 1+4 = 58 bytes
	codeLen := uint32(58)
	jmpDispl := displ - (codeLen - 5)

	buf := new(bytes.Buffer)
	buf.Write(pushArgs1)
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, uint32(a.imageBase))
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, uint16(0x15FF))
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, vprotectIAT)

	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, uint16(0x05C7))
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, optHdrEntrypoint)
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, entrypoint)

	buf.Write(pushArgs2)
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, uint32(a.imageBase))
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, uint16(0x15FF))
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, vprotectIAT)
	buf.WriteByte(0x58) // pop eax

	buf.WriteByte(0xE9)
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, jmpDispl)

	newCode := buf.Bytes()
	if len(newCode) != int(codeLen) {
		return fmt.Errorf("anti-dump stub length mismatch")
	}
	old, err := winapi.VirtualProtectEx(a.hProcess, oep, uintptr(len(newCode)), winapi.PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	_, writeErr := winapi.WriteProcessMemory(a.hProcess, oep, newCode)
	_, protectErr := winapi.VirtualProtectEx(a.hProcess, oep, uintptr(len(newCode)), old)
	if writeErr != nil {
		return writeErr
	}
	if protectErr != nil {
		return protectErr
	}
	if err := winapi.FlushInstructionCache(a.hProcess, oep, uintptr(len(newCode))); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	log.Goodf("Installed VM anti-dump (PE header) mitigation at OEP")
	return nil
}

```

`pkg/app/app.go`:

```go
package app

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"unsafe"

	"unmida/pkg/log"
	"unmida/pkg/patcher"
	"unmida/pkg/pe"
	"unmida/pkg/themida"
)

type Options struct {
	File, Parameters string
	CreateData       bool
	PID              uint32
}

func Execute(action string, options Options) error {
	filename, err := filepath.Abs(options.File)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	data, err := os.ReadFile(filename)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	header, err := pe.ParsePEHeader(data)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if header.FileHeader.Machine != 0x14C && header.FileHeader.Machine != 0x8664 {
		return fmt.Errorf("unsupported PE machine: 0x%X", header.FileHeader.Machine)
	}
	if action == "unpack" {
		if header.Is64Bit != (unsafe.Sizeof(uintptr(0)) == 8) {
			return fmt.Errorf("use the %d-bit Unmida executable for this file", map[bool]int{true: 64, false: 32}[header.Is64Bit])
		}
		if header.Is64Bit {
			debugger, err := themida.NewDebugger64(filename, options.Parameters, options.CreateData)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return debugger.RunDebugLoop()
		}
		debugger, err := themida.NewDebugger32(filename, options.Parameters, options.CreateData)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return debugger.RunDebugLoop()
	}
	if header.Is64Bit {
		return fmt.Errorf("%s is available only for PE32 files, as in the original program", action)
	}
	if action == "dump" {
		if options.PID == 0 {
			return fmt.Errorf("PID must be greater than zero")
		}
		return patcher.DumpProcessCode(options.PID, filename)
	}
	patch, err := patcher.NewPatcher(filename)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	switch action {
	case "shrink":
		return patch.ProcessShrink()
	case "mkdata":
		return patch.ProcessMkData()
	default:
		return fmt.Errorf("unknown operation: %s", action)
	}
}

func CLI(args []string) int {
	usage := "Unmida <file> [--no-data] [--parameters <command line>]\nUnmida /unpack <file> [--no-data] [--parameters <command line>]\nUnmida /shrink <file>\nUnmida /mkdata <file>\nUnmida /dump <PID> <file>"
	if len(args) == 0 {
		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, usage)
		return 2
	}
	if args[0] == "--help" || args[0] == "-h" || args[0] == "/?" {
		fmt.Println(usage)
		return 0
	}
	action := strings.TrimLeft(strings.ToLower(args[0]), "/-")
	options := Options{CreateData: true}
	index := 1
	switch action {
	case "unpack", "dump", "shrink", "mkdata":
	default:
		action = "unpack"
		index = 0
	}
	if action == "dump" {
		if index >= len(args) {
			fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, usage)
			return 2
		}
		pid, err := strconv.ParseUint(args[index], 10, 32)
		if err != nil || pid == 0 {
			fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Invalid PID")
			return 2
		}
		options.PID = uint32(pid)
		index++
	}
	if index >= len(args) {
		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, usage)
		return 2
	}
	options.File = args[index]
	index++
	for index < len(args) {
		switch args[index] {
		case "--no-data":
			options.CreateData = false
		case "--parameters":
			index++
			if index >= len(args) {
				fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Missing --parameters value")
				return 2
			}
			options.Parameters = args[index]
		default:
			fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Unknown argument:", args[index])
			return 2
		}
		index++
	}
	switch action {
	case "unpack", "dump", "shrink", "mkdata":
	default:
		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, usage)
		return 2
	}
	if err := Execute(action, options); err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("%v", err)
		return 1
	}
	return 0
}

```

`pkg/debugger/debugger.go`:

```go
package debugger

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"runtime"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"syscall"
	"unsafe"

	"unmida/pkg/log"
	"unmida/pkg/pe"
	"unmida/pkg/utils"
	"unmida/pkg/winapi"
)

type HWBPType int

const (
	HWExecute HWBPType = 0
	HWWrite   HWBPType = 1
	HWAccess  HWBPType = 3
)

type Breakpoint struct {
	Address  uintptr
	BType    HWBPType
	Disabled bool
}

func (bp *Breakpoint) Change(addr uintptr, bType HWBPType) {
	bp.Address = addr
	bp.BType = bType
	bp.Disabled = false
}

func (bp *Breakpoint) IsSet() bool {
	return !bp.Disabled && bp.Address > 0
}

type SoftBPAction int

const (
	SbpKeepContinue SoftBPAction = iota
	SbpClearContinue
	SbpKeepContinueNoStep
)

type DebuggerCallbacks interface {
	OnDebugStart(hFile syscall.Handle, hThread syscall.Handle)
	OnAccessViolation(hThread syscall.Handle, excRec *winapi.EXCEPTION_RECORD) uint32
	OnDLLLoad(fileName string, baseAddress uintptr)
	OnHardwareBreakpoint(hThread syscall.Handle, bpa uintptr, c32 *winapi.CONTEXT32, c64 *winapi.CONTEXT64)
	OnSoftwareBreakpoint(hThread syscall.Handle, bpa uintptr) SoftBPAction
	OnUnsolicitedSoftwareBreakpoint(hThread syscall.Handle, bpa uintptr)
	OnSinglestep(bpa uintptr) uint32
}

type DebuggerCore struct {
	Callbacks DebuggerCallbacks

	Executable    string
	Parameters    string
	AttachPID     uint32
	IsDLL         bool
	Is64Bit       bool
	DLLExecutable string

	ProcessInfo           syscall.ProcessInformation
	CurrentThreadID       uint32
	ImageBase             uintptr
	HideThreadEnd         bool
	HW                    [4]Breakpoint
	Threads               map[uint32]syscall.Handle
	SoftBPs               map[uintptr]byte
	SoftBPReenable        uintptr
	mu                    sync.Mutex
	ShouldTerminate       bool
	Err                   error
	Succeeded             bool
	EventConsumed         bool
	InitialBreakpointSeen bool
	CurrentThreadHnd      syscall.Handle
}

func NewDebuggerCore(executable, parameters string, callbacks DebuggerCallbacks) *DebuggerCore {
	return &DebuggerCore{
		Executable: executable,
		Parameters: parameters,
		Callbacks:  callbacks,
		Threads:    make(map[uint32]syscall.Handle),
		SoftBPs:    make(map[uintptr]byte),
	}
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) RPM(addr uintptr, buf []byte) bool {
	_, err := winapi.ReadProcessMemory(d.ProcessInfo.Process, addr, buf)
	return err == nil
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) WriteByte(addr uintptr, val byte) bool {
	oldProt, err := winapi.VirtualProtectEx(d.ProcessInfo.Process, addr, 1, winapi.PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE)
	if err != nil {
		return false
	}
	_, err = winapi.WriteProcessMemory(d.ProcessInfo.Process, addr, []byte{val})
	if err != nil {
		winapi.VirtualProtectEx(d.ProcessInfo.Process, addr, 1, oldProt)
		return false
	}
	winapi.VirtualProtectEx(d.ProcessInfo.Process, addr, 1, oldProt)
	winapi.FlushInstructionCache(d.ProcessInfo.Process, addr, 1)
	return true
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) SetBreakpoint(addr uintptr, bType HWBPType, refreshContexts bool) error {
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()

	idx := -1
	for i := 0; i < 4; i++ {
		if d.HW[i].Address == addr {
			d.HW[i].Change(addr, bType)
			if refreshContexts {
				for _, thread := range d.Threads {
					d.UpdateDR(thread)
				}
			}
			return nil
		}
		if d.HW[i].Address == 0 {
			idx = i
			break
		}
	}
	if idx == -1 {
		return fmt.Errorf("all hardware breakpoints in use")
	}

	d.HW[idx].Change(addr, bType)

	if refreshContexts {
		for _, hThread := range d.Threads {
			d.UpdateDR(hThread)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) DisableBreakpoint(addr uintptr) bool {
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()

	for i := 0; i < 4; i++ {
		if d.HW[i].Address == addr {
			d.HW[i].Disabled = true
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) EnableBreakpoints() {
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()

	needUpdate := false
	for i := 0; i < 4; i++ {
		if d.HW[i].Disabled {
			d.HW[i].Disabled = false
			needUpdate = true
		}
	}
	if needUpdate {
		for _, hThread := range d.Threads {
			d.UpdateDR(hThread)
		}
	}
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) ResetBreakpoint(addr uintptr) {
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()

	for i := 0; i < 4; i++ {
		if d.HW[i].Address == addr {
			d.HW[i].Address = 0
			d.HW[i].Disabled = false
		}
	}
	for _, hThread := range d.Threads {
		d.UpdateDR(hThread)
	}
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) IsHWBreakpoint(addr uintptr) bool {
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()

	for i := 0; i < 4; i++ {
		if d.HW[i].Address == addr {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) ApplyDebugRegisters64(c *winapi.CONTEXT64) {
	mask := uint32(0)

	c.Dr0 = uint64(d.HW[0].Address)
	if d.HW[0].IsSet() {
		mask |= 1
	}

	c.Dr1 = uint64(d.HW[1].Address)
	if d.HW[1].IsSet() {
		mask |= (1 << 2)
	}

	c.Dr2 = uint64(d.HW[2].Address)
	if d.HW[2].IsSet() {
		mask |= (1 << 4)
	}

	c.Dr3 = uint64(d.HW[3].Address)
	if d.HW[3].IsSet() {
		mask |= (1 << 6)
	}

	c.Dr6 &= 0xFFFFBFFF
	c.Dr7 = uint64(mask | (uint32(d.HW[0].BType) << 16) | (uint32(d.HW[1].BType) << 20) | (uint32(d.HW[2].BType) << 24) | (uint32(d.HW[3].BType) << 28))
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) ApplyDebugRegisters32(c *winapi.CONTEXT32) {
	mask := uint32(0)

	c.Dr0 = uint32(d.HW[0].Address)
	if d.HW[0].IsSet() {
		mask |= 1
	}

	c.Dr1 = uint32(d.HW[1].Address)
	if d.HW[1].IsSet() {
		mask |= (1 << 2)
	}

	c.Dr2 = uint32(d.HW[2].Address)
	if d.HW[2].IsSet() {
		mask |= (1 << 4)
	}

	c.Dr3 = uint32(d.HW[3].Address)
	if d.HW[3].IsSet() {
		mask |= (1 << 6)
	}

	c.Dr6 &= 0xFFFFBFFF
	c.Dr7 = mask | (uint32(d.HW[0].BType) << 16) | (uint32(d.HW[1].BType) << 20) | (uint32(d.HW[2].BType) << 24) | (uint32(d.HW[3].BType) << 28)
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) UpdateDR(hThread syscall.Handle) {
	if d.Is64Bit {
		var c winapi.CONTEXT64
		c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_DEBUG_REGISTERS_64
		if err := winapi.GetThreadContext64(hThread, &c); err == nil {
			d.ApplyDebugRegisters64(&c)
			if err := winapi.SetThreadContext64(hThread, &c); err != nil {
				d.Fail(err)
			}
		} else {
			d.Fail(fmt.Errorf("UpdateDR: GetThreadContext64: %w", err))
		}
	} else {
		var c winapi.CONTEXT32
		c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_DEBUG_REGISTERS_32
		if err := winapi.GetThreadContext32(hThread, &c); err == nil {
			d.ApplyDebugRegisters32(&c)
			if err := winapi.SetThreadContext32(hThread, &c); err != nil {
				d.Fail(err)
			}
		} else {
			d.Fail(fmt.Errorf("UpdateDR: GetThreadContext32: %w", err))
		}
	}
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) SetSoftBP(addr uintptr) error {
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()

	var b [1]byte
	if !d.RPM(addr, b[:]) {
		return fmt.Errorf("read for soft bp at 0x%X failed", addr)
	}

	if orig, ok := d.SoftBPs[addr]; ok {
		if b[0] != 0xCC {
			log.Fatalf("Soft bp inconsistency at 0x%X (stored 0x%X, got 0x%X)", addr, orig, b[0])
		}
		return nil
	}

	d.SoftBPs[addr] = b[0]
	if !d.WriteByte(addr, 0xCC) {
		return fmt.Errorf("write for soft bp at 0x%X failed", addr)
	}
	return nil
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) SoftBPClear() {
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()

	for addr, orig := range d.SoftBPs {
		d.WriteByte(addr, orig)
	}
	d.SoftBPs = make(map[uintptr]byte)
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) PEInspect() error {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(d.Executable)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if len(data) < 0x40 {
		return fmt.Errorf("selected file is too short")
	}
	if data[0] != 'M' || data[1] != 'Z' {
		return fmt.Errorf("selected file is not a valid PE")
	}
	lfanew := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[0x3C:0x40])
	if lfanew > 0xF00 || uint64(lfanew)+24 > uint64(len(data)) {
		return fmt.Errorf("selected file has malformed PE headers")
	}

	sig := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[lfanew : lfanew+4])
	if sig != 0x00004550 {
		return fmt.Errorf("PE file signature mismatch: 0x%08X", sig)
	}

	machine := binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[lfanew+4 : lfanew+6])
	if machine == winapi.IMAGE_FILE_MACHINE_AMD64 {
		d.Is64Bit = true
	} else if machine == winapi.IMAGE_FILE_MACHINE_I386 {
		d.Is64Bit = false
	} else {
		return fmt.Errorf("unsupported machine architecture: 0x%04X", machine)
	}

	characteristics := binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[lfanew+22 : lfanew+24])
	d.IsDLL = (characteristics & winapi.IMAGE_FILE_DLL) != 0

	if d.Is64Bit != (unsafe.Sizeof(uintptr(0)) == 8) {
		return fmt.Errorf("file architecture differs from Unmida; use the %d-bit version", map[bool]int{true: 64, false: 32}[d.Is64Bit])
	}
	return nil
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) MakeDLLExecutable() error {
	d.DLLExecutable = d.Executable + "MM.exe"
	data, err := os.ReadFile(d.Executable)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("reading DLL failed: %w", err)
	}

	lfanew := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[0x3C:0x40])
	// Clear IMAGE_FILE_DLL
	chars := binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[lfanew+22 : lfanew+24])
	chars &^= winapi.IMAGE_FILE_DLL
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint16(data[lfanew+22:lfanew+24], chars)

	peHeader, err := pe.ParsePEHeader(data)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("parsing PE failed: %w", err)
	}

	// Disable "Code Integrity Image" (DllCharacteristics 0x80)
	if (peHeader.DllCharacteristics & 0x80) != 0 {
		peHeader.DllCharacteristics &^= 0x80
		// OptionalHeader DllCharacteristics offset
		dllCharsOffset := lfanew + 24 + 70
		if int(dllCharsOffset)+2 <= len(data) {
			binary.LittleEndian.PutUint16(data[dllCharsOffset:dllCharsOffset+2], peHeader.DllCharacteristics)
		}
	}

	var stub []byte
	if !peHeader.Is64Bit {
		stub = []byte{0x8B, 0x40, 0x08, 0x6A, 0x00, 0x6A, 0x01, 0x50, 0xE8, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00}
	} else {
		stub = []byte{
			0x48, 0x83, 0xEC, 0x28, 0x65, 0x48, 0x8B, 0x04, 0x25, 0x60, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
			0x48, 0x8B, 0x48, 0x10, 0xBA, 0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x45, 0x31, 0xC0,
			0xE8, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
		}
	}

	epSec := peHeader.GetSectionByVA(peHeader.AddressOfEntryPoint)
	if epSec == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("cannot find section for entrypoint")
	}

	stubOffset := int(epSec.Header.PointerToRawData+epSec.Header.SizeOfRawData) - len(stub)
	if stubOffset < 0 || stubOffset+len(stub) > len(data) {
		return fmt.Errorf("not enough room in EP section")
	}

	for i := stubOffset; i < stubOffset+len(stub); i++ {
		if data[i] != 0 {
			log.Fatalf("Not enough room in EP section (non-zero bytes present)")
			return fmt.Errorf("EP section not padded with zeroes")
		}
	}

	newEP := epSec.Header.VirtualAddress + epSec.Header.SizeOfRawData - uint32(len(stub))
	rel := int32(peHeader.AddressOfEntryPoint - (newEP + uint32(len(stub))))

	copy(data[stubOffset:], stub)
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(data[stubOffset+len(stub)-4:stubOffset+len(stub)], uint32(rel))

	// Update AddressOfEntryPoint
	peHeader.AddressOfEntryPoint = newEP
	optEPPos := lfanew + 24 + 16
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(data[optEPPos:optEPPos+4], newEP)

	if err := os.WriteFile(d.DLLExecutable, data, 0755); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("writing modified DLL failed: %w", err)
	}

	// Switch SxS Manifest type
	f, err := os.OpenFile(d.DLLExecutable, os.O_RDWR, 0755)
	if err == nil {
		SwitchSxSManifestType(f, peHeader, 2, 1)
		f.Close()
	}

	return nil
}

func SwitchSxSManifestType(s io.ReadWriteSeeker, peHeader *pe.PEHeader, switchFrom, switchTo int) {
	rsrcDir := peHeader.DataDirectory[winapi.IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_RESOURCE]
	if rsrcDir.VirtualAddress == 0 || rsrcDir.Size == 0 {
		return
	}
	rsrcSec := peHeader.GetSectionByVA(rsrcDir.VirtualAddress)
	if rsrcSec == nil {
		return
	}

	if _, err := s.Seek(int64(rsrcSec.Header.PointerToRawData+rsrcDir.VirtualAddress-rsrcSec.Header.VirtualAddress), io.SeekStart); err != nil {
		return
	}

	rsrcData := make([]byte, rsrcDir.Size)
	if _, err := io.ReadFull(s, rsrcData); err != nil {
		return
	}

	if len(rsrcData) < 16 {
		return
	}

	numNamed := binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(rsrcData[0x0C:0x0E])
	numID := binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(rsrcData[0x0E:0x10])
	if numID == 0 {
		return
	}

	offset := 0x10 + int(numNamed)*8
	manifestSubDir := uint32(0)
	for i := 0; i < int(numID); i++ {
		entryOffset := offset + i*8
		if entryOffset+8 > len(rsrcData) {
			break
		}
		typeID := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(rsrcData[entryOffset : entryOffset+4])
		if typeID == 24 { // RT_MANIFEST
			manifestSubDir = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(rsrcData[entryOffset+4 : entryOffset+8])
			break
		}
	}

	if manifestSubDir == 0 || (manifestSubDir>>31) != 1 {
		return
	}
	manifestSubDir &= 0x7FFFFFFF

	if int(manifestSubDir)+16 > len(rsrcData) {
		return
	}

	idOffset := int(manifestSubDir) + 0x10
	if idOffset+4 > len(rsrcData) {
		return
	}

	currID := int(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(rsrcData[idOffset : idOffset+4]))
	if currID != switchFrom {
		return
	}

	if _, err := s.Seek(int64(rsrcSec.Header.PointerToRawData+rsrcDir.VirtualAddress-rsrcSec.Header.VirtualAddress)+int64(idOffset), io.SeekStart); err == nil {
		var buf [4]byte
		binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(buf[:], uint32(switchTo))
		s.Write(buf[:])
	}
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) PEExecute() error {
	if d.AttachPID != 0 {
		return winapi.DebugActiveProcess(d.AttachPID)
	}

	if err := d.PEInspect(); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	targetDir := filepath.Dir(d.Executable)

	var cmdLine string
	if d.IsDLL {
		if err := d.MakeDLLExecutable(); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		log.Infof("Debugging modified DLL: %s", d.DLLExecutable)
		cmdLine = fmt.Sprintf(`"%s"`, d.DLLExecutable)
	} else {
		cmdLine = fmt.Sprintf(`"%s" %s`, d.Executable, strings.TrimRight(d.Parameters, " "))
	}

	var si syscall.StartupInfo
	si.Cb = uint32(unsafe.Sizeof(si))
	si.Flags = winapi.STARTF_USESHOWWINDOW
	si.ShowWindow = winapi.SW_SHOW

	flags := uint32(winapi.CREATE_DEFAULT_ERROR_MODE | winapi.CREATE_NEW_CONSOLE | winapi.NORMAL_PRIORITY_CLASS | winapi.DEBUG_PROCESS | winapi.DEBUG_ONLY_THIS_PROCESS)

	workDir := targetDir

	return winapi.CreateProcess("", cmdLine, workDir, flags, &si, &d.ProcessInfo)
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) RunDebugLoop() error {
	runtime.LockOSThread()
	defer runtime.UnlockOSThread()
	defer d.cleanup()
	if err := d.PEExecute(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("creating process failed: %w", err)
	}

	status := uint32(winapi.DBG_CONTINUE)
	var ev winapi.DEBUG_EVENT

	for !d.ShouldTerminate {
		if err := winapi.WaitForDebugEvent(&ev, 0xFFFFFFFF); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("WaitForDebugEvent failed: %w", err)
		}

		d.CurrentThreadID = ev.DwThreadId
		hThread := d.Threads[ev.DwThreadId]
		d.CurrentThreadHnd = hThread

		switch ev.DwDebugEventCode {
		case winapi.EXCEPTION_DEBUG_EVENT:
			status = d.handleException(&ev)

		case winapi.CREATE_THREAD_DEBUG_EVENT:
			info := ev.CreateThread()
			log.Infof("[%.4d] Thread started (%p).", ev.DwThreadId, info.LpStartAddress)
			d.Threads[ev.DwThreadId] = info.HThread
			d.UpdateDR(info.HThread)
			status = winapi.DBG_CONTINUE

		case winapi.CREATE_PROCESS_DEBUG_EVENT:
			info := ev.CreateProcessInfo()
			log.Infof("Running on Windows build %d", utils.GetWindowsBuildNumber())
			log.Infof("Debug session launched (PID: %d, TID: %d)", ev.DwProcessId, ev.DwThreadId)

			d.ProcessInfo.Process = info.HProcess
			d.ProcessInfo.ProcessId = ev.DwProcessId
			d.ProcessInfo.ThreadId = ev.DwThreadId
			d.ImageBase = info.LpBaseOfImage
			d.Threads[ev.DwThreadId] = info.HThread

			var pbi winapi.PROCESS_BASIC_INFORMATION
			if err := winapi.NtQueryInformationProcess(info.HProcess, 0, unsafe.Pointer(&pbi), uint32(unsafe.Sizeof(pbi)), nil); err != nil {
				winapi.CloseHandle(info.HFile)
				return err
			}
			log.Infof("PEB: %p", unsafe.Pointer(pbi.PebBaseAddress))

			// Patch PEB.BeingDebugged (offset 2)
			var beingDebugged [1]byte
			if d.RPM(pbi.PebBaseAddress+2, beingDebugged[:]) {
				if beingDebugged[0] == 1 {
					log.Goodf("Patching PEB.BeingDebugged")
					d.WriteByte(pbi.PebBaseAddress+2, 0)
				}
			}

			// Read ImageBase from PEB: +8 on 32-bit, +16 on 64-bit
			offsetImageBase := uintptr(8)
			offsetShimData := uintptr(0x1E8)
			if d.Is64Bit {
				offsetImageBase = 16
				offsetShimData = 0x2D8
			}

			var imgBaseBuf [8]byte
			readLen := 4
			if d.Is64Bit {
				readLen = 8
			}
			if d.RPM(pbi.PebBaseAddress+offsetImageBase, imgBaseBuf[:readLen]) {
				if d.Is64Bit {
					d.ImageBase = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(imgBaseBuf[:8]))
				} else {
					d.ImageBase = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(imgBaseBuf[:4]))
				}
				log.Infof("Process Image Base: 0x%X", d.ImageBase)
			} else {
				return fmt.Errorf("reading process image base failed")
			}

			// Clear PEB.pShimData
			var shimBuf [8]byte
			if d.RPM(pbi.PebBaseAddress+offsetShimData, shimBuf[:readLen]) {
				hasShim := false
				for i := 0; i < readLen; i++ {
					if shimBuf[i] != 0 {
						hasShim = true
						break
					}
				}
				if hasShim {
					zeroes := make([]byte, readLen)
					winapi.WriteProcessMemory(info.HProcess, pbi.PebBaseAddress+offsetShimData, zeroes)
					log.Infof("Cleared PEB.pShimData to prevent apphelp hooks")
				}
			}

			if d.Callbacks != nil {
				d.Callbacks.OnDebugStart(info.HFile, info.HThread)
			}
			winapi.CloseHandle(info.HFile)
			status = winapi.DBG_CONTINUE

		case winapi.EXIT_THREAD_DEBUG_EVENT:
			info := ev.ExitThread()
			if !d.HideThreadEnd {
				log.Infof("[%.4d] Thread ended (code %d).", ev.DwThreadId, info.DwExitCode)
			}
			winapi.CloseHandle(d.Threads[ev.DwThreadId])
			delete(d.Threads, ev.DwThreadId)
			status = winapi.DBG_CONTINUE

		case winapi.EXIT_PROCESS_DEBUG_EVENT:
			info := ev.ExitProcess()
			log.Infof("Process ended (code %d).", info.DwExitCode)
			winapi.ContinueDebugEvent(ev.DwProcessId, ev.DwThreadId, winapi.DBG_CONTINUE)
			if !d.Succeeded {
				return fmt.Errorf("target exited before unpacking completed (code %d)", info.DwExitCode)
			}
			return d.Err

		case winapi.LOAD_DLL_DEBUG_EVENT:
			info := ev.LoadDll()
			dllName := winapi.DebugModuleFileName(info.HFile, d.ProcessInfo.Process, info.LpBaseOfDll)
			var lpImageName uintptr
			var pBuf [8]byte
			ptrSize := 4
			if d.Is64Bit {
				ptrSize = 8
			}
			if dllName == "" && d.RPM(info.LpImageName, pBuf[:ptrSize]) {
				if d.Is64Bit {
					lpImageName = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(pBuf[:8]))
				} else {
					lpImageName = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(pBuf[:4]))
				}
				if lpImageName != 0 {
					var wideBuf [520]byte
					if d.RPM(lpImageName, wideBuf[:]) {
						var runes []uint16
						for i := 0; i < len(wideBuf); i += 2 {
							r := binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(wideBuf[i : i+2])
							if r == 0 {
								break
							}
							runes = append(runes, r)
						}
						dllName = syscall.UTF16ToString(runes)
					}
				}
			}
			if dllName == "" {
				dllName = "?"
			}
			log.Infof("[0x%08X] Loaded %s", info.LpBaseOfDll, dllName)
			if strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(dllName), "aclayers.dll") {
				log.Fatalf("Compatibility mode screws up the unpacking process.")
				return fmt.Errorf("compatibility mode detected")
			}
			if d.Callbacks != nil {
				d.Callbacks.OnDLLLoad(dllName, info.LpBaseOfDll)
			}
			winapi.CloseHandle(info.HFile)
			status = winapi.DBG_CONTINUE

		case winapi.UNLOAD_DLL_DEBUG_EVENT:
			status = winapi.DBG_CONTINUE

		case winapi.OUTPUT_DEBUG_STRING_EVENT:
			info := ev.DebugString()
			if info.NDebugStringLength > 0 && info.NDebugStringLength < 256 {
				strBuf := make([]byte, info.NDebugStringLength)
				if d.RPM(info.LpDebugStringData, strBuf) {
					log.Infof("[Debug Str] %s", strings.TrimRight(string(strBuf), "\x00"))
				}
			}
			status = winapi.DBG_CONTINUE

		case winapi.RIP_EVENT:
			d.Fail(fmt.Errorf("system error (RIP event)"))
			status = winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
		}

		if d.EventConsumed {
			d.EventConsumed = false
		} else {
			if err := winapi.ContinueDebugEvent(ev.DwProcessId, ev.DwThreadId, status); err != nil && !d.ShouldTerminate {
				return err
			}
		}
	}

	return d.Err
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) handleException(ev *winapi.DEBUG_EVENT) uint32 {
	exc := ev.Exception()
	hThread := d.Threads[ev.DwThreadId]

	switch exc.ExceptionRecord.ExceptionCode {
	case winapi.EXCEPTION_ACCESS_VIOLATION:
		if d.Callbacks != nil {
			return d.Callbacks.OnAccessViolation(hThread, &exc.ExceptionRecord)
		}
		return winapi.DBG_EXCEPTION_NOT_HANDLED

	case winapi.EXCEPTION_BREAKPOINT:
		bpAddr := exc.ExceptionRecord.ExceptionAddress
		if _, ok := d.SoftBPs[bpAddr]; ok {
			return d.handleSoftwareBreakpoint(hThread, bpAddr)
		}
		if d.Callbacks != nil {
			d.Callbacks.OnUnsolicitedSoftwareBreakpoint(hThread, bpAddr)
		}
		if !d.InitialBreakpointSeen {
			d.InitialBreakpointSeen = true
			return winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
		}
		return winapi.DBG_EXCEPTION_NOT_HANDLED

	case winapi.EXCEPTION_SINGLE_STEP:
		return d.handleSingleStep(hThread, exc.ExceptionRecord.ExceptionAddress)

	default:
		if exc.DwFirstChance == 0 {
			log.Fatalf("Non-first-chance exception 0x%08X at 0x%p", exc.ExceptionRecord.ExceptionCode, unsafe.Pointer(exc.ExceptionRecord.ExceptionAddress))
			d.Fail(fmt.Errorf("unhandled exception 0x%08X at 0x%X", exc.ExceptionRecord.ExceptionCode, exc.ExceptionRecord.ExceptionAddress))
			return winapi.DBG_EXCEPTION_NOT_HANDLED
		}
		log.Infof("Code 0x%08X at 0x%p", exc.ExceptionRecord.ExceptionCode, unsafe.Pointer(exc.ExceptionRecord.ExceptionAddress))
		return winapi.DBG_EXCEPTION_NOT_HANDLED
	}
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) handleSoftwareBreakpoint(hThread syscall.Handle, bpAddr uintptr) uint32 {
	// Rewind IP by 1
	if d.Is64Bit {
		var c winapi.CONTEXT64
		c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_64
		winapi.GetThreadContext64(hThread, &c)
		c.Rip--
		winapi.SetThreadContext64(hThread, &c)
	} else {
		var c winapi.CONTEXT32
		c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_32
		winapi.GetThreadContext32(hThread, &c)
		c.Eip--
		winapi.SetThreadContext32(hThread, &c)
	}

	origByte := d.SoftBPs[bpAddr]
	d.WriteByte(bpAddr, origByte)

	action := SbpClearContinue
	if d.Callbacks != nil {
		action = d.Callbacks.OnSoftwareBreakpoint(hThread, bpAddr)
	}

	switch action {
	case SbpClearContinue:
		delete(d.SoftBPs, bpAddr)
	case SbpKeepContinue:
		d.SoftBPReenable = bpAddr
		if d.Is64Bit {
			var c winapi.CONTEXT64
			c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_64
			winapi.GetThreadContext64(hThread, &c)
			c.EFlags |= 0x100
			winapi.SetThreadContext64(hThread, &c)
		} else {
			var c winapi.CONTEXT32
			c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_32
			winapi.GetThreadContext32(hThread, &c)
			c.EFlags |= 0x100
			winapi.SetThreadContext32(hThread, &c)
		}
	case SbpKeepContinueNoStep:
		d.WriteByte(bpAddr, 0xCC)
	}

	return winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) handleSingleStep(hThread syscall.Handle, eip uintptr) uint32 {
	if d.Is64Bit {
		var c winapi.CONTEXT64
		c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_64 | winapi.CONTEXT_INTEGER_64 | winapi.CONTEXT_DEBUG_REGISTERS_64
		winapi.GetThreadContext64(hThread, &c)

		// Check if bit 14 (single step execution mode) is 0 and any HWBP is set
		if ((c.Dr6>>14)&1) == 0 && (d.HW[0].IsSet() || d.HW[1].IsSet() || d.HW[2].IsSet() || d.HW[3].IsSet()) {
			var bp *Breakpoint
			switch c.Dr6 & 0xF {
			case 1:
				copyBP := d.HW[0]
				bp = &copyBP
			case 2:
				copyBP := d.HW[1]
				bp = &copyBP
			case 4:
				copyBP := d.HW[2]
				bp = &copyBP
			case 8:
				copyBP := d.HW[3]
				bp = &copyBP
			default:
				log.Fatalf("Unknown hwbp at 0x%X (Dr6: 0x%08X)", eip, c.Dr6)
				return winapi.DBG_EXCEPTION_NOT_HANDLED
			}

			if d.Callbacks != nil {
				d.Callbacks.OnHardwareBreakpoint(hThread, bp.Address, nil, &c)
			}

			if bp.BType == HWExecute && d.DisableBreakpoint(uintptr(c.Rip)) {
				d.UpdateDR(hThread)
				winapi.GetThreadContext64(hThread, &c)
				c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_64
				c.EFlags |= 0x100
				winapi.SetThreadContext64(hThread, &c)
			}
			return winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
		}
	} else {
		var c winapi.CONTEXT32
		c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_32 | winapi.CONTEXT_INTEGER_32 | winapi.CONTEXT_DEBUG_REGISTERS_32
		winapi.GetThreadContext32(hThread, &c)

		if ((c.Dr6>>14)&1) == 0 && (d.HW[0].IsSet() || d.HW[1].IsSet() || d.HW[2].IsSet() || d.HW[3].IsSet()) {
			var bp *Breakpoint
			switch c.Dr6 & 0xF {
			case 1:
				copyBP := d.HW[0]
				bp = &copyBP
			case 2:
				copyBP := d.HW[1]
				bp = &copyBP
			case 4:
				copyBP := d.HW[2]
				bp = &copyBP
			case 8:
				copyBP := d.HW[3]
				bp = &copyBP
			default:
				log.Fatalf("Unknown hwbp at 0x%X (Dr6: 0x%08X)", eip, c.Dr6)
				return winapi.DBG_EXCEPTION_NOT_HANDLED
			}

			if d.Callbacks != nil {
				d.Callbacks.OnHardwareBreakpoint(hThread, bp.Address, &c, nil)
			}

			if bp.BType == HWExecute && d.DisableBreakpoint(uintptr(c.Eip)) {
				d.UpdateDR(hThread)
				winapi.GetThreadContext32(hThread, &c)
				c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_32
				c.EFlags |= 0x100
				winapi.SetThreadContext32(hThread, &c)
			}
			return winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
		}
	}

	if d.SoftBPReenable != 0 {
		d.WriteByte(d.SoftBPReenable, 0xCC)
		d.SoftBPReenable = 0
		return winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
	}

	if d.Callbacks != nil {
		return d.Callbacks.OnSinglestep(eip)
	}

	d.EnableBreakpoints()
	return winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) Fail(err error) {
	if d.Err == nil {
		d.Err = err
		log.Fatalf("%v", err)
	}
	d.ShouldTerminate = true
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) WriteMemory(addr uintptr, data []byte) error {
	if len(data) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	old, err := winapi.VirtualProtectEx(d.ProcessInfo.Process, addr, uintptr(len(data)), winapi.PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	_, writeErr := winapi.WriteProcessMemory(d.ProcessInfo.Process, addr, data)
	_, protectErr := winapi.VirtualProtectEx(d.ProcessInfo.Process, addr, uintptr(len(data)), old)
	flushErr := winapi.FlushInstructionCache(d.ProcessInfo.Process, addr, uintptr(len(data)))
	if writeErr != nil {
		return writeErr
	}
	if protectErr != nil {
		return protectErr
	}
	return flushErr
}

func (d *DebuggerCore) cleanup() {
	if d.ProcessInfo.Process != 0 {
		if d.AttachPID == 0 {
			winapi.TerminateProcess(d.ProcessInfo.Process, 0)
		} else {
			winapi.DebugActiveProcessStop(d.AttachPID)
		}
	}
	closed := make(map[syscall.Handle]bool)
	for _, thread := range d.Threads {
		if thread != 0 && !closed[thread] {
			winapi.CloseHandle(thread)
			closed[thread] = true
		}
	}
	if d.ProcessInfo.Thread != 0 && !closed[d.ProcessInfo.Thread] {
		winapi.CloseHandle(d.ProcessInfo.Thread)
	}
	if d.ProcessInfo.Process != 0 {
		winapi.CloseHandle(d.ProcessInfo.Process)
	}
	if d.DLLExecutable != "" {
		os.Remove(d.DLLExecutable)
	}
}

```

`pkg/disasm/disasm.go`:

```go
package disasm

import (
	"fmt"
	"golang.org/x/arch/x86/x86asm"
)

type Instruction struct {
	Length                      int
	Opcode, Opcode2             byte
	IsTwoByte                   bool
	HasModRM                    bool
	ModRM, Mod, Reg, RM         byte
	HasSIB                      bool
	SIB                         byte
	HasDisplacement             bool
	Displacement                int64
	HasImmediate                bool
	Immediate                   uint64
	ImmSize                     int
	IsCall, IsJmp, IsRet        bool
	RIPRelative, AbsoluteMemory bool
	AddrValue, VirtualAddr      uint64
	Decoded                     x86asm.Inst
}

func DecodeInstruction(data []byte, is64Bit bool, virtAddr uint64) (*Instruction, error) {
	mode := 32
	if is64Bit {
		mode = 64
	}
	decoded, err := x86asm.Decode(data, mode)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if decoded.Len == 0 || decoded.Op == 0 {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid instruction at 0x%X", virtAddr)
	}
	ins := &Instruction{Length: decoded.Len, VirtualAddr: virtAddr, Decoded: decoded}
	pos := 0
	for pos < decoded.Len {
		b := data[pos]
		if b == 0x66 || b == 0x67 || b == 0xF0 || b == 0xF2 || b == 0xF3 ||
			b == 0x2E || b == 0x36 || b == 0x3E || b == 0x26 || b == 0x64 || b == 0x65 ||
			(is64Bit && b >= 0x40 && b <= 0x4F) {
			pos++
			continue
		}
		break
	}
	if pos >= decoded.Len {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("instruction contains only prefixes")
	}
	ins.Opcode = data[pos]
	pos++
	if ins.Opcode == 0x0F && pos < decoded.Len {
		ins.IsTwoByte = true
		ins.Opcode2 = data[pos]
		pos++
	}
	if pos < decoded.Len {
		ins.ModRM = data[pos]
		ins.Mod, ins.Reg, ins.RM = ins.ModRM>>6, (ins.ModRM>>3)&7, ins.ModRM&7
	}
	ins.IsCall = decoded.Op == x86asm.CALL || decoded.Op == x86asm.LCALL
	ins.IsRet = decoded.Op == x86asm.RET || decoded.Op == x86asm.LRET
	ins.IsJmp = decoded.Op == x86asm.JMP || decoded.Op == x86asm.LJMP
	for _, arg := range decoded.Args {
		switch value := arg.(type) {
		case x86asm.Mem:
			ins.HasModRM = ins.Opcode != 0xA0 && ins.Opcode != 0xA1 && ins.Opcode != 0xA2 && ins.Opcode != 0xA3
			ins.HasDisplacement = true
			ins.Displacement = value.Disp
			ins.RIPRelative = value.Base == x86asm.RIP || value.Base == x86asm.EIP
			ins.AbsoluteMemory = value.Base == 0 && value.Index == 0
			ins.HasSIB = ins.HasModRM && ins.Mod != 3 && ins.RM == 4 && decoded.AddrSize != 16
			if ins.HasSIB && pos+1 < decoded.Len {
				ins.SIB = data[pos+1]
			}
		case x86asm.Imm:
			ins.HasImmediate = true
			ins.Immediate = uint64(value)
		case x86asm.Rel:
			ins.HasImmediate = true
			ins.Immediate = uint64(value)
			ins.ImmSize = decoded.PCRel
			ins.AddrValue = uint64(int64(virtAddr) + int64(decoded.Len) + int64(value))
			if !is64Bit {
				ins.AddrValue = uint64(uint32(ins.AddrValue))
			}
			if !ins.IsCall {
				ins.IsJmp = true
			}
		}
	}
	return ins, nil
}

```

`pkg/dumper/dumper.go`:

```go
package dumper

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"syscall"

	"unmida/pkg/debugger"
	"unmida/pkg/log"
	"unmida/pkg/onecoreuap"
	"unmida/pkg/pe"
	"unmida/pkg/winapi"
)

const maxIATSize = 5120 * 8 // up to 5K imports

var forwardPreferences = []string{
	"kernel32.dll", // prioritize over kernelbase/ntdll
	"ole32.dll",    // prioritize over combase
	"advapi32.dll", // prioritize over cryptbase
	"netapi32.dll", // prioritize over netutils
	"comdlg32.dll", // prioritize over shlwapi
	"crypt32.dll",  // prioritize over dpapi
	"gdi32.dll",    // prioritize over gdi32full
	"dbghelp.dll",  // prioritize over dbgcore
	"setupapi.dll", // prioritize over cfgmgr32
}

func preferenceScore(name string) int {
	score := 0
	lower := strings.ToLower(name)
	for _, p := range forwardPreferences {
		if lower == p {
			score++
		}
	}
	return score
}

type Forward struct {
	Key   string
	Value uintptr
}

type RemoteModule struct {
	Base         uintptr
	EndOff       uintptr
	Name         string
	ExportTbl    map[uintptr]string
	ExportByName map[string]uintptr
	Forwards     []Forward
}

type ForwardOrigin struct {
	SourceModule  *RemoteModule
	SourceAddress uintptr
}

type ImportThunk struct {
	Module    *RemoteModule
	Name      string
	Addresses []int // indices into IAT slots
}

type OriginalImport struct {
	DLLName  string
	FuncName string
}

type ResolutionCandidate struct {
	Address uintptr
	Module  *RemoteModule
}

type IATSlot struct {
	OriginalValue   uint64
	Candidates      []ResolutionCandidate
	ChosenCandidate int
	IsZero          bool
}

type Dumper struct {
	OutputFile      string
	ProcessHandle   syscall.Handle
	ProcessID       uint32
	OriginalFile    string
	ImageBase       uintptr
	OEP             uintptr
	IAT             uintptr
	Is64Bit         bool
	PtrSize         int
	AllModules      []*RemoteModule
	Forwards        map[uintptr][]ForwardOrigin
	IATImage        []byte
	OriginalImports []OriginalImport
}

func NewDumper(hProcess syscall.Handle, pid uint32, origFile string, imageBase, oep uintptr, is64Bit bool) *Dumper {
	ptrSize := 4
	if is64Bit {
		ptrSize = 8
	}
	d := &Dumper{
		ProcessHandle: hProcess,
		ProcessID:     pid,
		OriginalFile:  origFile,
		ImageBase:     imageBase,
		OEP:           oep,
		Is64Bit:       is64Bit,
		PtrSize:       ptrSize,
		Forwards:      make(map[uintptr][]ForwardOrigin),
	}
	d.OriginalImports = d.getOriginalImports(origFile)
	return d
}

func (d *Dumper) RPM(addr uintptr, buf []byte) bool {
	_, err := winapi.ReadProcessMemory(d.ProcessHandle, addr, buf)
	return err == nil
}

func (d *Dumper) IsAPIAddress(addr uintptr) bool {
	if d.AllModules == nil {
		if err := d.TakeModuleSnapshot(); err != nil {
			log.Fatalf("TakeModuleSnapshot error: %v", err)
			return false
		}
	}
	for _, rm := range d.AllModules {
		if addr >= rm.Base && addr < rm.EndOff {
			if _, ok := rm.ExportTbl[addr]; ok {
				return true
			}
		}
	}
	return false
}

func (d *Dumper) GetRemoteModule(name string) *RemoteModule {
	lower := strings.ToLower(name)
	for _, rm := range d.AllModules {
		if rm.Name == lower {
			return rm
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func (d *Dumper) GetRemoteModuleByBase(base uintptr) *RemoteModule {
	for _, rm := range d.AllModules {
		if rm.Base == base {
			return rm
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func (d *Dumper) TakeModuleSnapshot() error {
	d.AllModules = nil
	d.Forwards = make(map[uintptr][]ForwardOrigin)

	flags := uint32(winapi.TH32CS_SNAPMODULE | winapi.TH32CS_SNAPMODULE32)
	hSnap, err := winapi.CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(flags, d.ProcessID)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("CreateToolhelp32Snapshot failed: %w", err)
	}
	defer winapi.CloseHandle(hSnap)

	var me winapi.MODULEENTRY32W
	if err := winapi.Module32First(hSnap, &me); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("Module32First failed: %w", err)
	}

	for {
		if me.ModBaseAddr != d.ImageBase {
			modName := syscall.UTF16ToString(me.SzModule[:])
			rm := &RemoteModule{
				Base:         me.ModBaseAddr,
				EndOff:       me.ModBaseAddr + uintptr(me.ModBaseSize),
				Name:         strings.ToLower(modName),
				ExportTbl:    make(map[uintptr]string),
				ExportByName: make(map[string]uintptr),
			}
			d.gatherModuleExportsFromRemoteProcess(rm)
			if !d.Is64Bit && rm.Name == "user32.dll" {
				d.collectUser32Forwards(rm, syscall.UTF16ToString(me.SzExePath[:]))
			}
			d.AllModules = append(d.AllModules, rm)
		}
		if err := winapi.Module32Next(hSnap, &me); err != nil {
			break
		}
	}

	for _, rm := range d.AllModules {
		d.resolveForwards(rm)
	}

	return nil
}

func (d *Dumper) gatherModuleExportsFromRemoteProcess(rm *RemoteModule) {
	var head [0x1000]byte
	if !d.RPM(rm.Base, head[:]) {
		return
	}
	if head[0] != 'M' || head[1] != 'Z' {
		return
	}
	lfanew := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(head[0x3C:0x40])
	if int(lfanew)+0x88 > len(head) {
		return
	}

	sig := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(head[lfanew : lfanew+4])
	if sig != 0x00004550 {
		return
	}

	magic := binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(head[lfanew+24 : lfanew+26])
	var expRVA, expSize uint32
	if magic == 0x20B { // PE64
		// Export table data directory is at OptionalHeader + 112 (offset lfanew + 24 + 112 = lfanew + 136)
		expOffset := lfanew + 24 + 112
		if int(expOffset)+8 <= len(head) {
			expRVA = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(head[expOffset : expOffset+4])
			expSize = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(head[expOffset+4 : expOffset+8])
		}
	} else if magic == 0x10B { // PE32
		expOffset := lfanew + 24 + 96
		if int(expOffset)+8 <= len(head) {
			expRVA = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(head[expOffset : expOffset+4])
			expSize = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(head[expOffset+4 : expOffset+8])
		}
	}

	if expRVA == 0 || expSize < 40 || uint64(expRVA)+uint64(expSize) > uint64(rm.EndOff-rm.Base) {
		return
	}

	expBuf := make([]byte, expSize)
	if !d.RPM(rm.Base+uintptr(expRVA), expBuf) {
		return
	}

	numFuncs := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(expBuf[0x14:0x18])
	numNames := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(expBuf[0x18:0x1C])
	addrOfFuncsRVA := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(expBuf[0x1C:0x20])
	addrOfNamesRVA := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(expBuf[0x20:0x24])
	addrOfOrdinalsRVA := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(expBuf[0x24:0x28])
	baseOrdinal := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(expBuf[0x10:0x14])

	if uint64(numFuncs)*4 > uint64(len(expBuf)) || uint64(numNames)*4 > uint64(len(expBuf)) {
		return
	}
	named := make([]bool, numFuncs)

	for i := uint32(0); i < numNames; i++ {
		ordRelOff := (addrOfOrdinalsRVA - expRVA) + i*2
		nameRelOff := (addrOfNamesRVA - expRVA) + i*4

		if uint64(ordRelOff)+2 > uint64(len(expBuf)) || uint64(nameRelOff)+4 > uint64(len(expBuf)) {
			break
		}

		funcIndex := binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(expBuf[ordRelOff : ordRelOff+2])
		if uint32(funcIndex) >= numFuncs {
			continue
		}
		nameRVA := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(expBuf[nameRelOff : nameRelOff+4])

		if int(funcIndex) < len(named) {
			named[funcIndex] = true
		}

		nameStrOff := nameRVA - expRVA
		var nameBytes []byte
		if uint64(nameStrOff) < uint64(len(expBuf)) {
			for j := int(nameStrOff); j < len(expBuf); j++ {
				if expBuf[j] == 0 {
					break
				}
				nameBytes = append(nameBytes, expBuf[j])
			}
		}

		funcRelOff := (addrOfFuncsRVA - expRVA) + uint32(funcIndex)*4
		if uint64(funcRelOff)+4 <= uint64(len(expBuf)) {
			funcRVA := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(expBuf[funcRelOff : funcRelOff+4])
			if len(nameBytes) > 0 && funcRVA != 0 {
				rm.ExportTbl[rm.Base+uintptr(funcRVA)] = string(nameBytes)
				rm.ExportByName[string(nameBytes)] = rm.Base + uintptr(funcRVA)
			}
		}
	}

	for i := uint32(0); i < numFuncs; i++ {
		funcRelOff := (addrOfFuncsRVA - expRVA) + i*4
		if uint64(funcRelOff)+4 > uint64(len(expBuf)) {
			break
		}
		funcRVA := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(expBuf[funcRelOff : funcRelOff+4])
		if funcRVA == 0 {
			continue
		}
		funcAddr := rm.Base + uintptr(funcRVA)

		if !named[i] {
			rm.ExportTbl[funcAddr] = "#" + strconv.Itoa(int(baseOrdinal+i))
		}
		rm.ExportByName["#"+strconv.Itoa(int(baseOrdinal+i))] = funcAddr

		// Check if forwarder
		if funcRVA > expRVA && funcRVA < expRVA+expSize {
			fwdOff := funcRVA - expRVA
			var fwdBytes []byte
			for j := int(fwdOff); j < len(expBuf); j++ {
				if expBuf[j] == 0 {
					break
				}
				fwdBytes = append(fwdBytes, expBuf[j])
			}
			fwdStr := string(fwdBytes)
			if fwdStr != "" {
				rm.Forwards = append(rm.Forwards, Forward{Key: fwdStr, Value: funcAddr})
			}
		}
	}
}

func (d *Dumper) forwardModule(name string) *RemoteModule {
	if !strings.HasSuffix(strings.ToLower(name), ".dll") {
		name += ".dll"
	}
	if module := d.GetRemoteModule(name); module != nil {
		return module
	}
	lower := strings.ToLower(name)
	if !strings.HasPrefix(lower, "api-ms-win-") && !strings.HasPrefix(lower, "ext-ms-win-") {
		return nil
	}
	handle, err := winapi.GetModuleHandle(name)
	if err != nil {
		handle, err = winapi.LoadLibrary(name)
		if err != nil {
			return nil
		}
		defer winapi.FreeLibrary(handle)
	}
	return d.GetRemoteModuleByBase(uintptr(handle))
}

func (d *Dumper) finalExport(module *RemoteModule, name string, depth int) uintptr {
	if module == nil || depth > 16 {
		return 0
	}
	address := module.ExportByName[name]
	if address == 0 {
		return 0
	}
	for _, forward := range module.Forwards {
		if forward.Value == address {
			split := strings.LastIndexByte(forward.Key, '.')
			if split < 1 {
				return 0
			}
			return d.finalExport(d.forwardModule(forward.Key[:split]), forward.Key[split+1:], depth+1)
		}
	}
	return address
}

func (d *Dumper) resolveForwards(module *RemoteModule) {
	for _, forward := range module.Forwards {
		split := strings.LastIndexByte(forward.Key, '.')
		if split < 1 {
			continue
		}
		address := d.finalExport(d.forwardModule(forward.Key[:split]), forward.Key[split+1:], 0)
		if handle, err := winapi.GetModuleHandle(module.Name); err == nil && uintptr(handle) == module.Base {
			symbol := module.ExportTbl[forward.Value]
			if !strings.HasPrefix(symbol, "#") {
				if resolved, err := winapi.GetProcAddress(handle, symbol); err == nil {
					address = resolved
				}
			}
		}
		if address == 0 {
			continue
		}
		source := forward.Value
		if !d.Is64Bit && module.Name == "user32.dll" {
			source = address
		}
		d.Forwards[address] = append(d.Forwards[address], ForwardOrigin{SourceModule: module, SourceAddress: source})
	}
}

func (d *Dumper) DetermineIATSize(iatBuf []byte) uint32 {
	lastValidOffset := uint32(0)
	ptrSize := d.PtrSize
	limit := 5120 * d.PtrSize
	if len(iatBuf) < limit {
		limit = len(iatBuf)
	}

	for i := 0; i < limit; i += ptrSize {
		if lastValidOffset != 0 && uint32(i) >= lastValidOffset+0x100 {
			break
		}
		var val uintptr
		if d.Is64Bit {
			val = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(iatBuf[i : i+8]))
		} else {
			val = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(iatBuf[i : i+4]))
		}

		if d.IsAPIAddress(val) {
			lastValidOffset = uint32(i)
		}
	}

	return lastValidOffset + uint32(ptrSize)
}

func (d *Dumper) Process() (*pe.PEHeader, error) {
	if d.IAT == 0 {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("must set IAT before calling Process()")
	}

	// Read header from memory
	var secBuf [0x1000]byte
	if !d.RPM(d.ImageBase, secBuf[:]) {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading PE header failed")
	}
	peHeader, err := pe.ParsePEHeader(secBuf[:])
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing PE header failed: %w", err)
	}
	peHeader.Sanitize()

	iatData := make([]byte, 5120*d.PtrSize)
	if !d.RPM(d.IAT, iatData) {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading IAT failed")
	}

	iatSize := d.DetermineIATSize(iatData)
	log.Infof("Determined IAT size: 0x%X", iatSize)

	peHeader.DataDirectory[winapi.IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_IAT].VirtualAddress = uint32(d.IAT - d.ImageBase)
	peHeader.DataDirectory[winapi.IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_IAT].Size = iatSize + uint32(d.PtrSize)

	if d.AllModules == nil {
		if err := d.TakeModuleSnapshot(); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}

	allowApiSets := false
	for _, oi := range d.OriginalImports {
		if strings.HasPrefix(oi.DLLName, "api-ms-win") {
			allowApiSets = true
			break
		}
	}

	ptrSize := d.PtrSize
	slotCount := int(iatSize) / ptrSize
	slots := make([]IATSlot, slotCount)

	// PASS 1: Collect candidates
	for i := 0; i < slotCount; i++ {
		slots[i].ChosenCandidate = -1
		var slotAddr uintptr
		if d.Is64Bit {
			slotAddr = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(iatData[i*ptrSize : (i+1)*ptrSize]))
		} else {
			slotAddr = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(iatData[i*ptrSize : (i+1)*ptrSize]))
		}

		slots[i].OriginalValue = uint64(slotAddr)
		if slotAddr == 0 {
			slots[i].IsZero = true
			continue
		}

		for _, rm := range d.AllModules {
			if _, ok := rm.ExportTbl[slotAddr]; ok {
				slots[i].Candidates = append(slots[i].Candidates, ResolutionCandidate{Address: slotAddr, Module: rm})
			}
		}

		// Variant B: forwards
		if origins, ok := d.Forwards[slotAddr]; ok {
			for _, o := range origins {
				slots[i].Candidates = append(slots[i].Candidates, ResolutionCandidate{
					Address: o.SourceAddress,
					Module:  o.SourceModule,
				})
			}
		}

		if len(slots[i].Candidates) == 0 {
			log.Infof("IAT slot 0x%08X -> 0x%08X unresolvable", d.IAT+uintptr(i*ptrSize), slotAddr)
		}
	}

	// PASS 2: Grouping and voting
	var thunks []*ImportThunk

	for i := 0; i < slotCount; {
		if slots[i].IsZero {
			i++
			continue
		}

		groupStart := i
		groupEnd := i
		for groupEnd+1 < slotCount && !slots[groupEnd+1].IsZero {
			groupEnd++
		}

		ordinalMask := uint64(winapi.IMAGE_ORDINAL_FLAG32)
		if d.Is64Bit {
			ordinalMask = winapi.IMAGE_ORDINAL_FLAG64
		}
		ordinalOnly := true
		for j := groupStart; j <= groupEnd; j++ {
			value := slots[j].OriginalValue
			if len(slots[j].Candidates) != 0 || value&ordinalMask == 0 || value&^(ordinalMask|0xFFFF) != 0 {
				ordinalOnly = false
				break
			}
		}
		if ordinalOnly {
			log.Infof("Preserving unbound ordinal-only IAT group at 0x%X (%d slots); its DLL cannot be inferred from addresses", d.IAT+uintptr(groupStart*ptrSize), groupEnd-groupStart+1)
			i = groupEnd + 1
			continue
		}

		moduleVotes := make(map[string]int)
		for j := groupStart; j <= groupEnd; j++ {
			for _, cand := range slots[j].Candidates {
				moduleVotes[cand.Module.Name]++
			}
		}

		winnerName := ""
		winnerVotes := -1
		moduleNames := make([]string, 0, len(moduleVotes))
		for name := range moduleVotes {
			moduleNames = append(moduleNames, name)
		}
		sort.Strings(moduleNames)
		for _, modName := range moduleNames {
			votes := moduleVotes[modName]
			if votes > winnerVotes || (votes == winnerVotes && preferenceScore(modName) > preferenceScore(winnerName)) {
				winnerVotes = votes
				winnerName = modName
			}
		}

		var winnerRM *RemoteModule
		for j := groupStart; j <= groupEnd; j++ {
			for k, cand := range slots[j].Candidates {
				if cand.Module.Name == winnerName {
					slots[j].ChosenCandidate = k
					if winnerRM == nil {
						winnerRM = cand.Module
					}
					break
				}
			}
		}

		apiSetName := ""
		if allowApiSets && winnerRM != nil {
			for j := groupStart; j <= groupEnd; j++ {
				if slots[j].ChosenCandidate < 0 && len(slots[j].Candidates) == 0 {
					continue
				}
				var funcName string
				if slots[j].ChosenCandidate < 0 {
					funcName = slots[j].Candidates[0].Module.ExportTbl[slots[j].Candidates[0].Address]
				} else {
					funcName = winnerRM.ExportTbl[slots[j].Candidates[slots[j].ChosenCandidate].Address]
				}

				if strings.HasPrefix(funcName, "RtlQueryPerformance") {
					funcName = strings.TrimPrefix(funcName, "Rtl")
				}

				mapped := onecoreuap.GetOneCoreUAPModuleByAPI(funcName)
				if j == groupStart {
					apiSetName = mapped
				} else if apiSetName != "" && mapped != apiSetName {
					apiSetName = ""
				}
			}
		}

		if apiSetName != "" && winnerRM != nil {
			for j := groupStart; j <= groupEnd; j++ {
				if slots[j].ChosenCandidate < 0 && len(slots[j].Candidates) > 0 {
					funcName := slots[j].Candidates[0].Module.ExportTbl[slots[j].Candidates[0].Address]
					for funcAddr, name := range winnerRM.ExportTbl {
						if name == funcName {
							slots[j].Candidates[0].Address = funcAddr
							slots[j].ChosenCandidate = 0
							log.Infof("IAT slot 0x%X: remapped apiset member %s", d.IAT+uintptr(j*ptrSize), funcName)
							break
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		var thunk *ImportThunk
		for j := groupStart; j <= groupEnd; j++ {
			if slots[j].ChosenCandidate < 0 {
				details := make([]string, 0, len(slots[j].Candidates))
				for _, candidate := range slots[j].Candidates {
					details = append(details, candidate.Module.Name+"!"+candidate.Module.ExportTbl[candidate.Address])
				}
				if os.Getenv("UNMIDA_DEBUG_IMPORTS") != "" {
					os.WriteFile(".dependencies/runtime-iat.bin", iatData, 0600)
					os.WriteFile(".dependencies/runtime-header.bin", secBuf[:], 0600)
					for offset := groupStart; offset <= groupEnd; offset++ {
						value := uint64(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(iatData[offset*ptrSize:]))
						if d.Is64Bit {
							value = binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(iatData[offset*ptrSize:])
						}
						names := []string{}
						for _, candidate := range slots[offset].Candidates {
							names = append(names, candidate.Module.Name+"!"+candidate.Module.ExportTbl[candidate.Address])
						}
						log.Infof("Import diagnostic 0x%X -> 0x%X: %v", d.IAT+uintptr(offset*ptrSize), value, names)
					}
				}
				return nil, fmt.Errorf("IAT slot 0x%X has no candidate for module %s; candidates: %v", d.IAT+uintptr(j*ptrSize), winnerName, details)
			}

			if thunk == nil {
				thunk = &ImportThunk{
					Module: winnerRM,
					Name:   winnerName,
				}
				thunks = append(thunks, thunk)
			}

			cand := slots[j].Candidates[slots[j].ChosenCandidate]
			if d.Is64Bit {
				binary.LittleEndian.PutUint64(iatData[j*ptrSize:(j+1)*ptrSize], uint64(cand.Address))
			} else {
				binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(iatData[j*ptrSize:(j+1)*ptrSize], uint32(cand.Address))
			}
			thunk.Addresses = append(thunk.Addresses, j)
		}

		if thunk != nil && apiSetName != "" && winnerRM != nil && len(thunk.Addresses) > 0 {
			firstSlot := thunk.Addresses[0]
			var firstAddr uintptr
			if d.Is64Bit {
				firstAddr = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(iatData[firstSlot*ptrSize : (firstSlot+1)*ptrSize]))
			} else {
				firstAddr = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(iatData[firstSlot*ptrSize : (firstSlot+1)*ptrSize]))
			}
			if !d.hasOriginalImport(thunk.Name, winnerRM.ExportTbl[firstAddr]) {
				log.Infof("OneCoreUAP: Using %s instead of %s", apiSetName, winnerName)
				thunk.Name = apiSetName
			}
		}

		i = groupEnd + 1
	}

	// Create .import section
	importSec := peHeader.CreateSection(".import", 0x1000)

	descSize := 20
	totalDescs := len(thunks) + 1 // null terminator
	strOffset := totalDescs * descSize

	secData := bytes.NewBuffer(make([]byte, 0, 0x1000))
	descBytes := make([]byte, totalDescs*descSize)
	secData.Write(descBytes)

	for thunkIdx, thunk := range thunks {
		firstSlot := thunk.Addresses[0]
		firstThunkRVA := uint32(d.IAT - d.ImageBase + uintptr(firstSlot*ptrSize))

		// Write DLL name
		nameRVA := importSec.Header.VirtualAddress + uint32(secData.Len())
		secData.WriteString(thunk.Name)
		secData.WriteByte(0)

		// Update descriptor
		descStart := thunkIdx * descSize
		binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(secData.Bytes()[descStart+12:descStart+16], nameRVA)
		binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(secData.Bytes()[descStart+16:descStart+20], firstThunkRVA)

		rm := thunk.Module
		var firstAddr uintptr
		if d.Is64Bit {
			firstAddr = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(iatData[firstSlot*ptrSize : (firstSlot+1)*ptrSize]))
		} else {
			firstAddr = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(iatData[firstSlot*ptrSize : (firstSlot+1)*ptrSize]))
		}
		log.Infof("Thunk %s - first import: %s", thunk.Name, rm.ExportTbl[firstAddr])

		for _, slotIdx := range thunk.Addresses {
			var funcAddr uintptr
			if d.Is64Bit {
				funcAddr = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(iatData[slotIdx*ptrSize : (slotIdx+1)*ptrSize]))
			} else {
				funcAddr = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(iatData[slotIdx*ptrSize : (slotIdx+1)*ptrSize]))
			}
			funcName := rm.ExportTbl[funcAddr]

			if strings.HasPrefix(funcName, "#") {
				ord, _ := strconv.Atoi(funcName[1:])
				if d.Is64Bit {
					binary.LittleEndian.PutUint64(iatData[slotIdx*ptrSize:(slotIdx+1)*ptrSize], winapi.IMAGE_ORDINAL_FLAG64|uint64(ord))
				} else {
					binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(iatData[slotIdx*ptrSize:(slotIdx+1)*ptrSize], winapi.IMAGE_ORDINAL_FLAG32|uint32(ord))
				}
				continue
			}

			if strings.HasPrefix(funcName, "RtlQueryPerformance") && strings.HasPrefix(thunk.Name, "api-ms-win") {
				funcName = strings.TrimPrefix(funcName, "Rtl")
			}

			// IMAGE_IMPORT_BY_NAME: Hint (2 bytes) + Name + \0
			if secData.Len()%2 != 0 {
				secData.WriteByte(0)
			}
			currRVA := importSec.Header.VirtualAddress + uint32(secData.Len())
			if d.Is64Bit {
				binary.LittleEndian.PutUint64(iatData[slotIdx*ptrSize:(slotIdx+1)*ptrSize], uint64(currRVA))
			} else {
				binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(iatData[slotIdx*ptrSize:(slotIdx+1)*ptrSize], currRVA)
			}

			secData.WriteByte(0) // Hint lo
			secData.WriteByte(0) // Hint hi
			secData.WriteString(funcName)
			secData.WriteByte(0)
		}
	}

	_ = strOffset

	// Align import section data
	for secData.Len()%0x1000 != 0 {
		secData.WriteByte(0)
	}
	importSec.Data = secData.Bytes()
	importSec.Header.SizeOfRawData = uint32(len(importSec.Data))
	importSec.Header.VirtualSize = importSec.Header.SizeOfRawData
	peHeader.SizeOfImage = importSec.Header.VirtualAddress + importSec.Header.VirtualSize
	peHeader.SectionAlign(&peHeader.SizeOfImage)

	peHeader.DataDirectory[winapi.IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_IMPORT].VirtualAddress = importSec.Header.VirtualAddress
	peHeader.DataDirectory[winapi.IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_IMPORT].Size = uint32(len(thunks) * descSize)

	if uint64(iatSize)+uint64(ptrSize) > uint64(len(iatData)) {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("IAT exceeds maximum size")
	}
	clear(iatData[iatSize : iatSize+uint32(ptrSize)])
	d.IATImage = iatData[:iatSize+uint32(ptrSize)]

	return peHeader, nil
}

func (d *Dumper) DumpToFile(fileName string, peHeader *pe.PEHeader, isDLL bool) error {
	f, actualPath, err := createDumpFile(fileName)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	d.OutputFile = actualPath

	dumpSize := peHeader.DumpSize
	buf := make([]byte, dumpSize)
	d.makeMemoryReadable(d.ImageBase, uintptr(dumpSize))

	if !d.RPM(d.ImageBase, buf) {
		return fmt.Errorf("DumpToFile RPM failed (base: 0x%X, size: 0x%X)", d.ImageBase, dumpSize)
	}

	iatRawOffset := uint32(d.IAT - d.ImageBase)
	delta := peHeader.TrimHugeSections(buf, &iatRawOffset)
	dumpSize -= delta

	if _, err := f.Write(buf[:dumpSize]); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	origSecCount := int(peHeader.FileHeader.NumberOfSections)
	for i := origSecCount; i < len(peHeader.Sections); i++ {
		sec := peHeader.Sections[i]
		if len(sec.Data) > 0 {
			if _, err := f.Seek(int64(sec.Header.PointerToRawData), io.SeekStart); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if _, err := f.Write(sec.Data); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		} else {
			zeroes := make([]byte, sec.Header.SizeOfRawData)
			if _, err := f.Seek(int64(sec.Header.PointerToRawData), io.SeekStart); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if _, err := f.Write(zeroes); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	}

	peHeader.FileHeader.NumberOfSections = uint16(len(peHeader.Sections))
	peHeader.AddressOfEntryPoint = uint32(d.OEP - d.ImageBase)

	if isDLL {
		peHeader.FileHeader.Characteristics |= winapi.IMAGE_FILE_DLL
		debugger.SwitchSxSManifestType(f, peHeader, 1, 2)
	}

	if (peHeader.DllCharacteristics & 0x40) != 0 {
		log.Infof("Executable is ASLR-aware - disabling the flag in the dump")
		peHeader.DllCharacteristics &^= 0x40
	}

	if err := peHeader.SaveToStream(f); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if _, err := f.Seek(int64(iatRawOffset), io.SeekStart); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if _, err := f.Write(d.IATImage); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	return nil
}

func (d *Dumper) makeMemoryReadable(base uintptr, size uintptr) {
	addr := base
	end := base + size
	var mbi winapi.MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION

	for addr < end {
		ret, err := winapi.VirtualQueryEx(d.ProcessHandle, addr, &mbi)
		if err != nil || ret == 0 {
			break
		}
		if mbi.State == winapi.MEM_COMMIT && mbi.Protect == winapi.PAGE_NOACCESS {
			winapi.VirtualProtectEx(d.ProcessHandle, mbi.BaseAddress, mbi.RegionSize, winapi.PAGE_READONLY)
		}
		next := mbi.BaseAddress + mbi.RegionSize
		if next <= addr {
			break
		}
		addr = next
	}
}

func (d *Dumper) getOriginalImports(filename string) []OriginalImport {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(filename)
	if err != nil {
		return nil
	}
	header, err := pe.ParsePEHeader(data)
	if err != nil {
		return nil
	}
	directory := header.DataDirectory[winapi.IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_IMPORT]
	if directory.VirtualAddress == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	ptrSize := uint32(4)
	ordinalMask := uint64(winapi.IMAGE_ORDINAL_FLAG32)
	if header.Is64Bit {
		ptrSize = 8
		ordinalMask = winapi.IMAGE_ORDINAL_FLAG64
	}
	var imports []OriginalImport
	for offset := uint32(0); uint64(offset)+20 <= uint64(directory.Size); offset += 20 {
		descriptor := fileRVA(header, data, directory.VirtualAddress+offset, 20)
		if descriptor == nil {
			break
		}
		nameRVA := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(descriptor[12:16])
		if nameRVA == 0 {
			break
		}
		name := strings.ToLower(fileString(header, data, nameRVA))
		thunk := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(descriptor[0:4])
		if thunk == 0 {
			thunk = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(descriptor[16:20])
		}
		if thunk == 0 {
			continue
		}
		for index := uint32(0); index < 5120; index++ {
			entry := fileRVA(header, data, thunk+index*ptrSize, ptrSize)
			if entry == nil {
				break
			}
			value := uint64(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(entry))
			if ptrSize == 8 {
				value = binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(entry)
			}
			if value == 0 {
				break
			}
			var function string
			if value&ordinalMask != 0 {
				function = "#" + strconv.Itoa(int(value&0xFFFF))
			} else if value <= 0xFFFFFFFD {
				function = fileString(header, data, uint32(value)+2)
			}
			if name != "" && function != "" {
				imports = append(imports, OriginalImport{DLLName: name, FuncName: function})
			}
		}
	}
	return imports
}

func (d *Dumper) hasOriginalImport(dll, funcName string) bool {
	lowerDLL := strings.ToLower(dll)
	for _, oi := range d.OriginalImports {
		if oi.DLLName == lowerDLL && oi.FuncName == funcName {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

// DumperDotnet dumps Themida .NET processes
type DumperDotnet struct {
	OutputFile    string
	ProcessHandle syscall.Handle
	ImageBase     uintptr
}

func NewDumperDotnet(hProcess syscall.Handle, imageBase uintptr) *DumperDotnet {
	return &DumperDotnet{
		ProcessHandle: hProcess,
		ImageBase:     imageBase,
	}
}

func (dd *DumperDotnet) DumpToFile(fileName string) error {
	var header [0x1000]byte
	if _, err := winapi.ReadProcessMemory(dd.ProcessHandle, dd.ImageBase, header[:]); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("DumpToFile header RPM failed: %w", err)
	}

	peHeader, err := pe.ParsePEHeader(header[:])
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("ParsePEHeader failed: %w", err)
	}

	lastSec := peHeader.Sections[len(peHeader.Sections)-1]
	size := lastSec.Header.VirtualAddress + lastSec.Header.VirtualSize

	f, actualPath, err := createDumpFile(fileName)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	dd.OutputFile = actualPath

	buf := make([]byte, size)
	physicalSize := size
	peHeader.FileAlign(&physicalSize)
	imageSize := physicalSize
	peHeader.SectionAlign(&imageSize)
	peHeader.SizeOfImage = imageSize

	if len(peHeader.Sections) > 0 {
		peHeader.Sections[0].Rename(".text")
	}

	log.Infof("Output has %d sections, determined size to be 0x%X", len(peHeader.Sections), size)

	var done uint32
	ptr := dd.ImageBase
	var mbi winapi.MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION
	mbi.RegionSize = 0x1000

	for done < size {
		ret, err := winapi.VirtualQueryEx(dd.ProcessHandle, ptr, &mbi)
		if err != nil || ret == 0 {
			return fmt.Errorf("VirtualQueryEx failed at 0x%X", ptr)
		}
		if mbi.RegionSize == 0 {
			return fmt.Errorf("VirtualQueryEx returned a zero region at 0x%X", ptr)
		}

		remaining := mbi.BaseAddress + mbi.RegionSize - ptr
		chunk := uint32(min(remaining, uintptr(size-done)))
		if size-done < chunk {
			chunk = size - done
		}

		if mbi.State == winapi.MEM_COMMIT {
			if _, err := winapi.ReadProcessMemory(dd.ProcessHandle, ptr, buf[done:done+chunk]); err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("DumpToFile RPM failed at 0x%X: %w", ptr, err)
			}
		} else if mbi.State == winapi.MEM_RESERVE {
			// zeroed
		} else {
			return fmt.Errorf("unexpected region state 0x%X at 0x%X", mbi.State, ptr)
		}

		done += chunk
		ptr += uintptr(chunk)
	}

	if _, err := f.Write(buf); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if size < physicalSize {
		pad := make([]byte, physicalSize-size)
		if _, err := f.Write(pad); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	return peHeader.SaveToStream(f)
}

func ChangeFileExt(path, newExt string) string {
	ext := filepath.Ext(path)
	return strings.TrimSuffix(path, ext) + newExt
}

func createDumpFile(path string) (*os.File, string, error) {
	file, err := os.Create(path)
	if err == nil {
		return file, path, nil
	}
	if !os.IsPermission(err) {
		return nil, path, err
	}
	fallback, resolveErr := filepath.Abs(filepath.Base(path))
	if resolveErr != nil {
		return nil, path, resolveErr
	}
	if filepath.Clean(fallback) == filepath.Clean(path) {
		return nil, path, err
	}
	file, err = os.Create(fallback)
	if err == nil {
		log.Infof("Output directory is not writable; saving dump to %s", fallback)
	}
	return file, fallback, err
}

`pkg/dumper/file_imports.go`:

```go
package dumper

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"unmida/pkg/pe"
	"unmida/pkg/winapi"
	"os"
	"strings"
)

func fileRVA(header *pe.PEHeader, data []byte, rva uint32, size uint32) []byte {
	var offset uint64
	if rva < header.SizeOfHeaders {
		offset = uint64(rva)
	} else {
		found := false
		for _, section := range header.Sections {
			h := section.Header
			if rva >= h.VirtualAddress && uint64(rva)-uint64(h.VirtualAddress)+uint64(size) <= uint64(h.SizeOfRawData) {
				offset = uint64(h.PointerToRawData) + uint64(rva) - uint64(h.VirtualAddress)
				found = true
				break
			}
		}
		if !found {
			return nil
		}
	}
	if offset+uint64(size) > uint64(len(data)) {
		return nil
	}
	return data[offset : offset+uint64(size)]
}

func fileString(header *pe.PEHeader, data []byte, rva uint32) string {
	bytes := make([]byte, 0, 64)
	for i := uint32(0); i < 4096; i++ {
		if rva+i < rva {
			return ""
		}
		current := fileRVA(header, data, rva+i, 1)
		if current == nil {
			return ""
		}
		if current[0] == 0 {
			return string(bytes)
		}
		bytes = append(bytes, current[0])
	}
	return ""
}

func (d *Dumper) collectUser32Forwards(module *RemoteModule, path string) {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return
	}
	header, err := pe.ParsePEHeader(data)
	if err != nil {
		return
	}
	directory := header.DataDirectory[winapi.IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_EXPORT]
	if directory.VirtualAddress == 0 {
		return
	}
	exports := fileRVA(header, data, directory.VirtualAddress, 40)
	if exports == nil {
		return
	}
	count := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(exports[20:24])
	table := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(exports[28:32])
	if uint64(count)*4 > uint64(len(data)) {
		return
	}
	for i := uint32(0); i < count; i++ {
		entry := fileRVA(header, data, table+i*4, 4)
		if entry == nil {
			return
		}
		address := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(entry)
		if address > directory.VirtualAddress && uint64(address) < uint64(directory.VirtualAddress)+uint64(directory.Size) {
			name := fileString(header, data, address)
			if name != "" && !strings.Contains(name, ".#") {
				module.Forwards = append(module.Forwards, Forward{Key: name})
			}
		}
	}
}

`pkg/log/log.go`:

```go
package log

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"sync"
)

type MsgType int

const (
	Info MsgType = iota
	Good
	Fatal
)

var output struct {
	sync.RWMutex
	handler func(MsgType, string)
}

func SetHandler(handler func(MsgType, string)) func() {
	output.Lock()
	previous := output.handler
	output.handler = handler
	output.Unlock()
	return func() { output.Lock(); output.handler = previous; output.Unlock() }
}

func Log(kind MsgType, format string, args ...interface{}) {
	message := fmt.Sprintf(format, args...)
	output.RLock()
	handler := output.handler
	output.RUnlock()
	if handler != nil {
		handler(kind, message)
		return
	}
	names := [...]string{"Info", "Good", "Fatal"}
	name := "Log"
	if kind >= Info && kind <= Fatal {
		name = names[kind]
	}
	stream := os.Stdout
	if kind == Fatal {
		stream = os.Stderr
	}
	fmt.Fprintf(stream, "[%s] %s\n", name, message)
}

func Infof(format string, args ...interface{})  { Log(Info, format, args...) }
func Goodf(format string, args ...interface{})  { Log(Good, format, args...) }
func Fatalf(format string, args ...interface{}) { Log(Fatal, format, args...) }

```

`pkg/onecoreuap/onecoreuap.go`:

package onecoreuap

var apiSetLookup = map[string]string{
	"CreateNewAdvertisingId":                           "api-ms-win-appmodel-advertisingid-l1-1-0.dll",
	"EnableAdvertisingId":                              "api-ms-win-appmodel-advertisingid-l1-1-0.dll",
	"EnableAdvertisingIdForUser":                       "api-ms-win-appmodel-advertisingid-l1-1-0.dll",
	"QueryAdvertisingId":                               "api-ms-win-appmodel-advertisingid-l1-1-0.dll",
	"QueryAdvertisingIdStatus":                         "api-ms-win-appmodel-advertisingid-l1-1-0.dll",
	"QueryAdvertisingIdStatusForUser":                  "api-ms-win-appmodel-advertisingid-l1-1-0.dll",
	"ClosePackageInfo":                                 "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",
	"GetApplicationUserModelId":                        "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",
	"GetCurrentApplicationUserModelId":                 "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",
	"GetCurrentPackageFamilyName":                      "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",
	"GetCurrentPackageFullName":                        "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",
	"GetCurrentPackageId":                              "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",
	"GetCurrentPackageInfo":                            "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",
	"GetCurrentPackagePath":                            "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",
	"GetPackageFamilyName":                             "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",
	"GetPackageFullName":                               "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",
	"GetPackageId":                                     "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",
	"GetPackageInfo":                                   "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",
	"GetPackagePath":                                   "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",
	"GetPackagesByPackageFamily":                       "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",
	"OpenPackageInfoByFullName":                        "api-ms-win-appmodel-runtime-l1-1-0.dll",

func GetOneCoreUAPModuleByAPI(api string) string {
	return apiSetLookup[api]
