
`pkg/patcher/patcher.go`:

```go
package patcher

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"

	"unmida/pkg/dumper"
	"unmida/pkg/log"
	"unmida/pkg/pe"
	"unmida/pkg/utils"
	"unmida/pkg/winapi"
)

type Patcher struct {
	FileName string
	Data     []byte
	PE       *pe.PEHeader
}

func NewPatcher(fileName string) (*Patcher, error) {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(fileName)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	peHeader, err := pe.ParsePEHeader(data)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &Patcher{
		FileName: fileName,
		Data:     data,
		PE:       peHeader,
	}, nil
}

func (p *Patcher) ProcessShrink() error {
	if err := p.shrinkPE(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	p.shrinkExportSect()

	return p.save()
}

func (p *Patcher) shrinkPE() error {
	firstRaw := p.PE.Sections[0].Header.PointerToRawData
	if uint64(firstRaw) > uint64(len(p.Data)) {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid first section offset")
	}
	data := bytes.NewBuffer(append([]byte(nil), p.Data[:firstRaw]...))
	sections := make([]*pe.PESection, 0, len(p.PE.Sections))
	for i, section := range p.PE.Sections {
		referenced := false
		for directoryIndex, directory := range p.PE.DataDirectory {
			if directoryIndex == winapi.IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_SECURITY || directory.VirtualAddress == 0 || directory.Size == 0 {
				continue
			}
			if uint64(directory.VirtualAddress) >= uint64(section.Header.VirtualAddress) && uint64(directory.VirtualAddress)+uint64(directory.Size) <= uint64(section.Header.VirtualAddress)+uint64(section.Header.VirtualSize) {
				referenced = true
				break
			}
		}
		keep := i == 0 || referenced || section.Header.SectionName() == ".data" || section.Header.SectionName() == ".rdata"
		if !keep {
			if len(sections) != 0 {
				previous := sections[len(sections)-1]
				end := uint64(section.Header.VirtualAddress) + uint64(section.Header.VirtualSize)
				if end > uint64(previous.Header.VirtualAddress) && end <= 0xFFFFFFFF {
					previous.Header.VirtualSize = uint32(end) - previous.Header.VirtualAddress
					p.PE.SectionAlign(&previous.Header.VirtualSize)
				}
			}
			continue
		}
		start := uint64(section.Header.PointerToRawData)
		end := start + uint64(section.Header.SizeOfRawData)
		if i+1 < len(p.PE.Sections) && uint64(p.PE.Sections[i+1].Header.PointerToRawData) >= end {
			end = uint64(p.PE.Sections[i+1].Header.PointerToRawData)
		}
		if i == len(p.PE.Sections)-1 {
			end = uint64(len(p.Data))
		}
		if start > end || end > uint64(len(p.Data)) {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid section data for %s", section.Header.SectionName())
		}
		section.Header.PointerToRawData = uint32(data.Len())
		data.Write(p.Data[start:end])
		sections = append(sections, section)
	}
	p.PE.Sections = sections
	p.PE.FileHeader.NumberOfSections = uint16(len(sections))
	p.PE.DataDirectory[winapi.IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_SECURITY] = pe.ImageDataDirectory{}
	p.Data = data.Bytes()
	return nil
}

func (p *Patcher) shrinkExportSect() {
	exportDir := &p.PE.DataDirectory[winapi.IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_EXPORT]
	if exportDir.VirtualAddress == 0 {
		return
	}
	ehSec := p.PE.GetSectionByVA(exportDir.VirtualAddress)
	if ehSec == nil {
		return
	}
	eh := &ehSec.Header

	pBase := int(eh.PointerToRawData)
	pExp := pBase + int(exportDir.VirtualAddress-eh.VirtualAddress)

	if exportDir.Size < 40 || exportDir.Size > 0x1000 || eh.SizeOfRawData < 0x1000 || pExp < 0 || uint64(pExp)+uint64(exportDir.Size) > uint64(len(p.Data)) || uint64(pBase)+uint64(eh.SizeOfRawData) > uint64(len(p.Data)) {
		return
	}

	copy(p.Data[pBase:pBase+int(exportDir.Size)], p.Data[pExp:pExp+int(exportDir.Size)])
	if pBase+0x1000 <= len(p.Data) {
		for j := pBase + int(exportDir.Size); j < pBase+0x1000; j++ {
			p.Data[j] = 0
		}
	}
	exportDir.VirtualAddress = eh.VirtualAddress

	diff := uint32(pExp - pBase)
	// Update export directory struct in place
	// Name: offset 0x0C
	// AddressOfFunctions: offset 0x1C
	// AddressOfNames: offset 0x20
	// AddressOfNameOrdinals: offset 0x24
	nameRVA := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(p.Data[pBase+0x0C : pBase+0x10])
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(p.Data[pBase+0x0C:pBase+0x10], nameRVA-diff)

	funcsRVA := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(p.Data[pBase+0x1C : pBase+0x20])
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(p.Data[pBase+0x1C:pBase+0x20], funcsRVA-diff)

	namesRVA := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(p.Data[pBase+0x20 : pBase+0x24])
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(p.Data[pBase+0x20:pBase+0x24], namesRVA-diff)

	ordsRVA := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(p.Data[pBase+0x24 : pBase+0x28])
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(p.Data[pBase+0x24:pBase+0x28], ordsRVA-diff)

	numNames := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(p.Data[pBase+0x18 : pBase+0x1C])
	namesTableOff := pBase + int(namesRVA-diff-eh.VirtualAddress)

	for i := uint32(0); i < numNames; i++ {
		off := namesTableOff + int(i*4)
		if off >= pBase && off+4 <= pBase+int(exportDir.Size) {
			nRVA := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(p.Data[off : off+4])
			binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(p.Data[off:off+4], nRVA-diff)
		}
	}

	count := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(p.Data[pBase+20 : pBase+24])
	functionOffset := pBase + int(funcsRVA-diff-eh.VirtualAddress)
	oldExportStart := exportDir.VirtualAddress + diff
	for i := uint32(0); i < count; i++ {
		offset := functionOffset + int(i)*4
		if offset < pBase || offset+4 > pBase+int(exportDir.Size) {
			break
		}
		value := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(p.Data[offset : offset+4])
		if value >= oldExportStart && uint64(value) < uint64(oldExportStart)+uint64(exportDir.Size) {
			binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(p.Data[offset:offset+4], value-diff)
		}
	}

	sizeDiff := eh.SizeOfRawData - 0x1000
	eh.SizeOfRawData -= sizeDiff

	for i := 0; i < len(p.PE.Sections); i++ {
		if p.PE.Sections[i].Header.VirtualAddress > eh.VirtualAddress {
			p.PE.Sections[i].Header.PointerToRawData -= sizeDiff
		}
	}

	ehSec.Rename(".export")
	eh.Characteristics &^= (winapi.IMAGE_SCN_MEM_WRITE | winapi.IMAGE_SCN_MEM_EXECUTE)

	// Truncate stream
	newData := new(bytes.Buffer)
	newData.Write(p.Data[:eh.PointerToRawData+0x1000])
	remainderStart := int(eh.PointerToRawData + 0x1000 + sizeDiff)
	if remainderStart <= len(p.Data) {
		newData.Write(p.Data[remainderStart:])
	}
	p.Data = newData.Bytes()
}

func (p *Patcher) ProcessMkData() error {
	lower := strings.ToLower(p.FileName)
	posMS := strings.LastIndex(lower, "maplestory")
	if posMS >= 0 && strings.Index(lower[posMS:], ".exe") < 20 {
		if err := p.mapleCreateDataSections(); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	} else if p.PE.MajorLinkerVersion == 14 { // MSVC 2015+
		if err := p.msvcCreateDataSections(); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	} else {
		log.Infof("Data section creation not available for this compiler.")
		return nil
	}

	return p.save()
}

func (p *Patcher) mapleCreateDataSections() error {
	if len(p.PE.Sections) < 2 {
		return fmt.Errorf("data section creation requires at least two sections")
	}
	mem := p.Data
	dataStart := -1

	if len(mem) > 0x2000000 {
		off := utils.FindStatic("10000000200000004000000060000000", mem[0x2000000:])
		if off >= 0 {
			dataStart = 0x2000000 + off
		}
	}

	if dataStart == -1 {
		if p.PE.MajorLinkerVersion == 6 {
			dataStart = p.findDataStartMSVC6()
			if dataStart == -1 {
				return fmt.Errorf("data section not found")
			}
		} else {
			baseSearch := 0xB00000 - 0x400000
			if len(mem) > baseSearch {
				d1 := utils.FindStatic("2E3F41565F636F6D5F6572726F724040", mem[baseSearch:])
				dTW := utils.FindStatic("2E3F41563F245A4C69737440554D4150494E464F4043416374696F6E4672616D6540404040", mem[baseSearch:])
				if dTW >= 0 && (d1 < 0 || dTW < d1) {
					d1 = dTW
				}
				if d1 < 0 {
					return fmt.Errorf("data section not found")
				}
				dataStart = d1 + (baseSearch - 8)
				if (dataStart&0xFFF) == 0xB4 || (dataStart&0xFFF) == 0xF8 || (dataStart&0xFFF) == 0xFC {
					dataStart &= ^0xFFF
				}
				log.Infof("Old executable")
			}
		}
	}

	if dataStart == -1 || (dataStart&0xFFF) != 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("data section bytes found, but not aligned: 0x%X", dataStart)
	}

	log.Goodf(".data section at 0x%08X (VA 0x%08X)", dataStart, dataStart+0x400000)

	// Add 2 sections: .rdata and .data
	p.PE.Sections = append(p.PE.Sections, &pe.PESection{}, &pe.PESection{})
	for i := len(p.PE.Sections) - 1; i >= 3; i-- {
		p.PE.Sections[i] = p.PE.Sections[i-2]
	}

	zEnd := 0
	zSize := 0
	lock := false
	limit := int(p.PE.Sections[3].Header.PointerToRawData)
	if limit < dataStart || limit > len(mem) {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid data section range")
	}

	for a := limit - 1; a >= dataStart; a-- {
		if mem[a] == 0 {
			if zSize == 0 {
				zEnd = a + 1
			}
			zSize++
			if zSize > 0x2000 {
				lock = true
			}
		} else {
			if lock {
				break
			}
			zSize = 0
		}
	}

	if zSize == 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("data section doesn't contain zeroes")
	}

	if (zEnd & 0xFFF) == 1 {
		zEnd--
		zSize--
	}
	if (zEnd & 0xFFF) != 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("real .data section end not found (got 0x%X with size 0x%X)", zEnd, zSize)
	}

	gfidsSize := limit - zEnd

	// .data at index 2
	dataSize := uint32(limit - dataStart - gfidsSize)
	secData := &pe.PESection{}
	secData.Rename(".data")
	secData.Header.VirtualSize = dataSize
	secData.Header.VirtualAddress = uint32(dataStart)
	secData.Header.PointerToRawData = uint32(dataStart)
	secData.Header.SizeOfRawData = dataSize - uint32(zSize)
	secData.Header.Characteristics = winapi.IMAGE_SCN_MEM_READ | winapi.IMAGE_SCN_MEM_WRITE | winapi.IMAGE_SCN_CNT_INITIALIZED_DATA
	p.PE.Sections[2] = secData

	// .rdata at index 1
	rdataStart := p.PE.BaseOfData
	rdataSize := uint32(dataStart) - rdataStart
	secRData := &pe.PESection{}
	secRData.Rename(".rdata")
	secRData.Header.VirtualSize = rdataSize
	secRData.Header.VirtualAddress = rdataStart
	secRData.Header.PointerToRawData = rdataStart
	secRData.Header.SizeOfRawData = rdataSize
	secRData.Header.Characteristics = winapi.IMAGE_SCN_MEM_READ | winapi.IMAGE_SCN_CNT_INITIALIZED_DATA
	p.PE.Sections[1] = secRData

	// .gfids / .vmp
	if gfidsSize != 0 {
		p.PE.Sections = append(p.PE.Sections, &pe.PESection{})
		for i := len(p.PE.Sections) - 1; i >= 4; i-- {
			p.PE.Sections[i] = p.PE.Sections[i-1]
		}
		secGfids := &pe.PESection{}
		if p.PE.MajorLinkerVersion >= 14 {
			secGfids.Rename(".gfids")
		} else {
			secGfids.Rename(".vmp")
		}
		secGfids.Header.VirtualSize = uint32(gfidsSize)
		secGfids.Header.VirtualAddress = uint32(zEnd)
		secGfids.Header.PointerToRawData = uint32(zEnd)
		secGfids.Header.SizeOfRawData = uint32(gfidsSize)
		secGfids.Header.Characteristics = winapi.IMAGE_SCN_MEM_READ | winapi.IMAGE_SCN_MEM_WRITE | winapi.IMAGE_SCN_CNT_INITIALIZED_DATA
		p.PE.Sections[3] = secGfids

		p.PE.FileHeader.NumberOfSections += 3
	} else {
		if p.PE.MajorLinkerVersion >= 14 {
			log.Fatalf(".gfids not found")
		}
		p.PE.FileHeader.NumberOfSections += 2
	}

	p.PE.Sections[0].Header.VirtualSize -= (rdataSize + dataSize + uint32(gfidsSize))
	p.PE.Sections[0].Header.SizeOfRawData -= (rdataSize + dataSize + uint32(gfidsSize))
	p.PE.Sections[0].Rename(".text")
	p.PE.Sections[0].Header.Characteristics &^= winapi.IMAGE_SCN_MEM_WRITE

	return nil
}

func (p *Patcher) findDataStartMSVC6() int {
	if len(p.Data) <= 0x100000 {
		return -1
	}
	off := utils.FindDynamic("68????????68??????00E8????????83C410C3", p.Data[0x100000:])
	if off < 0 {
		return -1
	}
	cinitCode := 0x100000 + off
	res := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(p.Data[cinitCode+6 : cinitCode+10])
	if (res & 0xFFF) != 0 {
		return -1
	}
	return int(res - uint32(p.PE.ImageBase))
}

func (p *Patcher) findDynTLSMSVC14() (uint32, bool) {
	if len(p.Data) <= 0x1000 {
		return 0, false
	}
	off := utils.FindDynamic("8BF033FF393E74??56E8", p.Data[0x1000:])
	if off < 0 {
		log.Infof("DynTLS code sequence not found.")
		return 0, false
	}

	codePtr := 0x1000 + off
	if p.Data[codePtr-5] != 0xE8 {
		log.Infof("DynTLS code sequence mismatch.")
		return 0, false
	}

	relCall := int32(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(p.Data[codePtr-4 : codePtr]))
	getPtrFunc := codePtr + int(relCall)
	if getPtrFunc < 0 || getPtrFunc >= len(p.Data) {
		return 0, false
	}

	if p.Data[getPtrFunc] == 0xE9 {
		if getPtrFunc+5 > len(p.Data) {
			return 0, false
		} // jmp indirection
		relJmp := int32(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(p.Data[getPtrFunc+1 : getPtrFunc+5]))
		getPtrFunc = getPtrFunc + int(relJmp) + 5
	}

	if getPtrFunc < 0 || getPtrFunc+5 > len(p.Data) || p.Data[getPtrFunc] != 0xB8 {
		log.Infof("DynTLS call analysis failed.")
		return 0, false
	}

	val := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(p.Data[getPtrFunc+1 : getPtrFunc+5])
	dynTLSInit := val - uint32(p.PE.ImageBase)

	if uint64(dynTLSInit)+4 <= uint64(len(p.Data)) {
		targetVal := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(p.Data[dynTLSInit : dynTLSInit+4])
		log.Infof("[MSVC] dyn_tls_init at 0x%08X", targetVal)
		if targetVal == 0 {
			dynTLSInit = 0
		}
	}

	return dynTLSInit, true
}

func (p *Patcher) msvcCreateDataSections() error {
	baseOfData := p.PE.BaseOfData
	firstSec := p.PE.Sections[0]
	if len(p.PE.Sections) < 2 {
		return fmt.Errorf("data section creation requires at least two sections")
	}

	if baseOfData > firstSec.Header.VirtualAddress &&
		baseOfData < firstSec.Header.VirtualAddress+firstSec.Header.VirtualSize &&
		(baseOfData&0xFFF) == 0 {

		dynTLS, ok := p.findDynTLSMSVC14()
		if !ok {
			return nil
		}

		var dataStart uint32
		if dynTLS != 0 {
			dataStart = (dynTLS + 0x1000) & ^uint32(0xFFF)
		} else {
			dataStart = baseOfData + 0x1000
			log.Infof("Setting .rdata size to just 1000 (no reference point for actual size)")
		}

		if dataStart >= p.PE.Sections[1].Header.PointerToRawData {
			return fmt.Errorf("data section start exceeds merged section")
		}
		p.PE.Sections = append(p.PE.Sections, &pe.PESection{}, &pe.PESection{})
		for i := len(p.PE.Sections) - 1; i >= 3; i-- {
			p.PE.Sections[i] = p.PE.Sections[i-2]
		}
		p.PE.FileHeader.NumberOfSections += 2

		// .data at index 2
		dataSize := p.PE.Sections[3].Header.PointerToRawData - dataStart
		secData := &pe.PESection{}
		secData.Rename(".data")
		secData.Header.VirtualSize = dataSize
		secData.Header.VirtualAddress = dataStart
		secData.Header.PointerToRawData = dataStart
		secData.Header.SizeOfRawData = dataSize
		secData.Header.Characteristics = winapi.IMAGE_SCN_MEM_READ | winapi.IMAGE_SCN_MEM_WRITE | winapi.IMAGE_SCN_CNT_INITIALIZED_DATA
		p.PE.Sections[2] = secData

		// .rdata at index 1
		rdataStart := baseOfData
		rdataSize := dataStart - rdataStart
		secRData := &pe.PESection{}
		secRData.Rename(".rdata")
		secRData.Header.VirtualSize = rdataSize
		secRData.Header.VirtualAddress = rdataStart
		secRData.Header.PointerToRawData = rdataStart
		secRData.Header.SizeOfRawData = rdataSize
		secRData.Header.Characteristics = winapi.IMAGE_SCN_MEM_READ | winapi.IMAGE_SCN_CNT_INITIALIZED_DATA
		p.PE.Sections[1] = secRData

		p.PE.Sections[0].Header.VirtualSize -= (rdataSize + dataSize)
		p.PE.Sections[0].Header.SizeOfRawData -= (rdataSize + dataSize)

		log.Infof(".text : 0x%08X ~ 0x%08X", p.PE.Sections[0].Header.VirtualAddress, p.PE.Sections[0].Header.VirtualAddress+p.PE.Sections[0].Header.VirtualSize)
		log.Infof(".rdata: 0x%08X ~ 0x%08X", p.PE.Sections[1].Header.VirtualAddress, p.PE.Sections[1].Header.VirtualAddress+p.PE.Sections[1].Header.VirtualSize)
		log.Infof(".data : 0x%08X ~ 0x%08X", p.PE.Sections[2].Header.VirtualAddress, p.PE.Sections[2].Header.VirtualAddress+p.PE.Sections[2].Header.VirtualSize)
	} else {
		log.Infof("Assuming sections are not merged.")
	}

	p.PE.Sections[0].Rename(".text")
	p.PE.Sections[0].Header.Characteristics &^= winapi.IMAGE_SCN_MEM_WRITE

	return nil
}

func DumpProcessCode(pid uint32, fileName string) error {
	hProcess, err := winapi.OpenProcess(winapi.PROCESS_ALL_ACCESS, false, pid)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("OpenProcess failed for PID %d: %w", pid, err)
	}
	defer winapi.CloseHandle(hProcess)

	patch, err := NewPatcher(fileName)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if patch.PE.Is64Bit {
		return fmt.Errorf("process code dumping requires PE32")
	}
	section := patch.PE.Sections[0].Header
	codeSize := section.VirtualSize
	if patch.PE.BaseOfData > section.VirtualAddress {
		codeSize = patch.PE.BaseOfData - section.VirtualAddress
	}
	if uint64(section.PointerToRawData)+uint64(codeSize) > uint64(len(patch.Data)) {
		return fmt.Errorf("code exceeds file section")
	}
	snapshot, err := winapi.CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(winapi.TH32CS_SNAPMODULE|winapi.TH32CS_SNAPMODULE32, pid)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer winapi.CloseHandle(snapshot)
	var module winapi.MODULEENTRY32W
	if err := winapi.Module32First(snapshot, &module); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	startAddr := module.ModBaseAddr + uintptr(section.VirtualAddress)
	size := uintptr(codeSize)

	buf := make([]byte, size)
	if _, err := winapi.ReadProcessMemory(hProcess, startAddr, buf); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("ReadProcessMemory failed: %w", err)
	}

	rawStart := int(patch.PE.Sections[0].Header.PointerToRawData)
	if rawStart+int(size) > len(patch.Data) {
		return fmt.Errorf("buffer overflow writing process code")
	}

	copy(patch.Data[rawStart:rawStart+int(size)], buf)

	outPath := dumper.ChangeFileExt(fileName, ".novm.exe")
	if err := os.WriteFile(outPath, patch.Data, 0755); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("writing output file failed: %w", err)
	}

	log.Goodf("Dumped 0x%X bytes to %s", size, filepath.Base(outPath))
	return nil
}

func (p *Patcher) save() error {
	file, err := os.CreateTemp(filepath.Dir(p.FileName), ".unmida-*.tmp")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	name := file.Name()
	defer os.Remove(name)
	defer file.Close()
	if _, err = file.Write(p.Data); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err = p.PE.SaveToStream(file); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err = file.Sync(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err = file.Close(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err = os.Rename(name, p.FileName); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	log.Goodf("Saved %s", p.FileName)
	return nil
}

```

`pkg/pe/pe.go`:

```go
package pe

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"unmida/pkg/log"
)

type ImageDataDirectory struct {
	VirtualAddress uint32
	Size           uint32
}

type ImageFileHeader struct {
	Machine              uint16
	NumberOfSections     uint16
	TimeDateStamp        uint32
	PointerToSymbolTable uint32
	NumberOfSymbols      uint32
	SizeOfOptionalHeader uint16
	Characteristics      uint16
}

type ImageSectionHeader struct {
	Name                 [8]byte
	VirtualSize          uint32
	VirtualAddress       uint32
	SizeOfRawData        uint32
	PointerToRawData     uint32
	PointerToRelocations uint32
	PointerToLinenumbers uint32
	NumberOfRelocations  uint16
	NumberOfLinenumbers  uint16
	Characteristics      uint32
}

func (s *ImageSectionHeader) SectionName() string {
	idx := bytes.IndexByte(s.Name[:], 0)
	if idx == -1 {
		return string(s.Name[:])
	}
	return string(s.Name[:idx])
}

type PESection struct {
	Header ImageSectionHeader
	Data   []byte
}

func (s *PESection) Rename(newName string) {
	var b [8]byte
	copy(b[:], []byte(newName))
	s.Header.Name = b
}

type PEHeader struct {
	Is64Bit bool
	LFANew  uint32

	FileHeader ImageFileHeader

	// Optional Header fields (unified)
	Magic                       uint16
	MajorLinkerVersion          byte
	MinorLinkerVersion          byte
	SizeOfCode                  uint32
	SizeOfInitializedData       uint32
	SizeOfUninitializedData     uint32
	AddressOfEntryPoint         uint32
	BaseOfCode                  uint32
	BaseOfData                  uint32 // 32-bit only
	ImageBase                   uint64
	SectionAlignment            uint32
	FileAlignment               uint32
	MajorOperatingSystemVersion uint16
	MinorOperatingSystemVersion uint16
	MajorImageVersion           uint16
	MinorImageVersion           uint16
	MajorSubsystemVersion       uint16
	MinorSubsystemVersion       uint16
	Win32VersionValue           uint32
	SizeOfImage                 uint32
	SizeOfHeaders               uint32
	CheckSum                    uint32
	Subsystem                   uint16
	DllCharacteristics          uint16
	SizeOfStackReserve          uint64
	SizeOfStackCommit           uint64
	SizeOfHeapReserve           uint64
	SizeOfHeapCommit            uint64
	LoaderFlags                 uint32
	NumberOfRvaAndSizes         uint32
	DataDirectory               [16]ImageDataDirectory

	Sections []*PESection
	DumpSize uint32
}

func ParsePEHeader(data []byte) (*PEHeader, error) {
	if len(data) < 0x40 {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("data too short for DOS header")
	}
	if data[0] != 'M' || data[1] != 'Z' {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid DOS signature")
	}

	pe := &PEHeader{}
	pe.LFANew = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[0x3C:0x40])
	if uint64(pe.LFANew)+24 > uint64(len(data)) {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid LFANew: 0x%X", pe.LFANew)
	}

	sig := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[pe.LFANew : pe.LFANew+4])
	if sig != 0x00004550 {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid NT signature: 0x%X", sig)
	}

	fhOffset := pe.LFANew + 4
	fhReader := bytes.NewReader(data[fhOffset : fhOffset+20])
	if err := binary.Read(fhReader, binary.LittleEndian, &pe.FileHeader); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed reading FileHeader: %w", err)
	}

	optOffset := fhOffset + 20
	optEnd := uint64(optOffset) + uint64(pe.FileHeader.SizeOfOptionalHeader)
	if pe.FileHeader.SizeOfOptionalHeader < 2 || optEnd > uint64(len(data)) {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("truncated optional header")
	}
	magic := binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset : optOffset+2])
	pe.Magic = magic
	minimum := uint16(96)
	if magic == 0x20B {
		minimum = 112
	}
	if pe.FileHeader.SizeOfOptionalHeader < minimum {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("optional header is too short")
	}

	if magic == 0x20B { // PE32+ (64-bit)
		pe.Is64Bit = true
		pe.MajorLinkerVersion = data[optOffset+2]
		pe.MinorLinkerVersion = data[optOffset+3]
		pe.SizeOfCode = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+4 : optOffset+8])
		pe.SizeOfInitializedData = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+8 : optOffset+12])
		pe.SizeOfUninitializedData = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+12 : optOffset+16])
		pe.AddressOfEntryPoint = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+16 : optOffset+20])
		pe.BaseOfCode = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+20 : optOffset+24])

		pe.ImageBase = binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(data[optOffset+24 : optOffset+32])
		pe.SectionAlignment = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+32 : optOffset+36])
		pe.FileAlignment = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+36 : optOffset+40])
		pe.MajorOperatingSystemVersion = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+40 : optOffset+42])
		pe.MinorOperatingSystemVersion = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+42 : optOffset+44])
		pe.MajorImageVersion = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+44 : optOffset+46])
		pe.MinorImageVersion = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+46 : optOffset+48])
		pe.MajorSubsystemVersion = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+48 : optOffset+50])
		pe.MinorSubsystemVersion = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+50 : optOffset+52])
		pe.Win32VersionValue = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+52 : optOffset+56])
		pe.SizeOfImage = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+56 : optOffset+60])
		pe.SizeOfHeaders = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+60 : optOffset+64])
		pe.CheckSum = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+64 : optOffset+68])
		pe.Subsystem = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+68 : optOffset+70])
		pe.DllCharacteristics = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+70 : optOffset+72])
		pe.SizeOfStackReserve = binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(data[optOffset+72 : optOffset+80])
		pe.SizeOfStackCommit = binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(data[optOffset+80 : optOffset+88])
		pe.SizeOfHeapReserve = binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(data[optOffset+88 : optOffset+96])
		pe.SizeOfHeapCommit = binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(data[optOffset+96 : optOffset+104])
		pe.LoaderFlags = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+104 : optOffset+108])
		pe.NumberOfRvaAndSizes = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+108 : optOffset+112])

		ddOffset := optOffset + 112
		for i := 0; i < 16 && uint32(i) < pe.NumberOfRvaAndSizes; i++ {
			if uint64(ddOffset)+8 <= optEnd {
				pe.DataDirectory[i].VirtualAddress = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[ddOffset : ddOffset+4])
				pe.DataDirectory[i].Size = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[ddOffset+4 : ddOffset+8])
				ddOffset += 8
			}
		}
	} else if magic == 0x10B { // PE32 (32-bit)
		pe.Is64Bit = false
		pe.MajorLinkerVersion = data[optOffset+2]
		pe.MinorLinkerVersion = data[optOffset+3]
		pe.SizeOfCode = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+4 : optOffset+8])
		pe.SizeOfInitializedData = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+8 : optOffset+12])
		pe.SizeOfUninitializedData = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+12 : optOffset+16])
		pe.AddressOfEntryPoint = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+16 : optOffset+20])
		pe.BaseOfCode = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+20 : optOffset+24])
		pe.BaseOfData = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+24 : optOffset+28])

		pe.ImageBase = uint64(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+28 : optOffset+32]))
		pe.SectionAlignment = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+32 : optOffset+36])
		pe.FileAlignment = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+36 : optOffset+40])
		pe.MajorOperatingSystemVersion = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+40 : optOffset+42])
		pe.MinorOperatingSystemVersion = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+42 : optOffset+44])
		pe.MajorImageVersion = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+44 : optOffset+46])
		pe.MinorImageVersion = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+46 : optOffset+48])
		pe.MajorSubsystemVersion = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+48 : optOffset+50])
		pe.MinorSubsystemVersion = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+50 : optOffset+52])
		pe.Win32VersionValue = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+52 : optOffset+56])
		pe.SizeOfImage = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+56 : optOffset+60])
		pe.SizeOfHeaders = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+60 : optOffset+64])
		pe.CheckSum = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+64 : optOffset+68])
		pe.Subsystem = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+68 : optOffset+70])
		pe.DllCharacteristics = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[optOffset+70 : optOffset+72])
		pe.SizeOfStackReserve = uint64(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+72 : optOffset+76]))
		pe.SizeOfStackCommit = uint64(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+76 : optOffset+80]))
		pe.SizeOfHeapReserve = uint64(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+80 : optOffset+84]))
		pe.SizeOfHeapCommit = uint64(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+84 : optOffset+88]))
		pe.LoaderFlags = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+88 : optOffset+92])
		pe.NumberOfRvaAndSizes = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[optOffset+92 : optOffset+96])

		ddOffset := optOffset + 96
		for i := 0; i < 16 && uint32(i) < pe.NumberOfRvaAndSizes; i++ {
			if uint64(ddOffset)+8 <= optEnd {
				pe.DataDirectory[i].VirtualAddress = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[ddOffset : ddOffset+4])
				pe.DataDirectory[i].Size = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[ddOffset+4 : ddOffset+8])
				ddOffset += 8
			}
		}
	} else {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unsupported OptionalHeader Magic: 0x%X", magic)
	}

	pe.DumpSize = pe.SizeOfImage
	if pe.SectionAlignment == 0 || pe.FileAlignment == 0 || pe.SizeOfImage == 0 {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid PE alignment or image size")
	}
	if pe.FileHeader.NumberOfSections == 0 || optEnd+uint64(pe.FileHeader.NumberOfSections)*40 > uint64(len(data)) {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("missing or truncated section table")
	}

	sectOffset := optOffset + uint32(pe.FileHeader.SizeOfOptionalHeader)
	pe.Sections = make([]*PESection, pe.FileHeader.NumberOfSections)
	for i := 0; i < int(pe.FileHeader.NumberOfSections); i++ {
		sReader := bytes.NewReader(data[sectOffset : sectOffset+40])
		sec := &PESection{}
		if err := binary.Read(sReader, binary.LittleEndian, &sec.Header); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed reading section %d: %w", i, err)
		}
		pe.Sections[i] = sec
		sectOffset += 40
	}

	return pe, nil
}

func (pe *PEHeader) SectionAlign(v *uint32) {
	delta := *v % pe.SectionAlignment
	if delta > 0 {
		*v += pe.SectionAlignment - delta
	}
}

func (pe *PEHeader) FileAlign(v *uint32) {
	delta := *v % pe.FileAlignment
	if delta > 0 {
		*v += pe.FileAlignment - delta
	}
}

func (pe *PEHeader) CreateSection(name string, size uint32) *PESection {
	var prev *ImageSectionHeader
	if len(pe.Sections) > 0 {
		prev = &pe.Sections[len(pe.Sections)-1].Header
	}

	sec := &PESection{}
	sec.Rename(name)

	sec.Header.VirtualSize = size
	if prev != nil {
		sec.Header.VirtualAddress = prev.VirtualAddress + prev.VirtualSize
	} else {
		sec.Header.VirtualAddress = 0x1000
	}
	pe.SectionAlign(&sec.Header.VirtualAddress)
	sec.Header.PointerToRawData = pe.SizeOfImage
	sec.Header.SizeOfRawData = size
	sec.Header.Characteristics = 0x40000000 | 0x00000040 // IMAGE_SCN_MEM_READ | IMAGE_SCN_CNT_INITIALIZED_DATA

	pe.SizeOfImage += size
	pe.Sections = append(pe.Sections, sec)
	return sec
}

func (pe *PEHeader) DeleteSection(idx int) {
	if idx < 0 || idx >= len(pe.Sections) {
		return
	}
	isLast := idx == len(pe.Sections)-1
	var sz uint32
	if isLast {
		sz = pe.SizeOfImage - pe.Sections[idx].Header.SizeOfRawData
	} else {
		sz = pe.Sections[idx+1].Header.PointerToRawData - pe.Sections[idx].Header.PointerToRawData
	}

	for i := len(pe.Sections) - 1; i > idx; i-- {
		pe.Sections[i].Header.PointerToRawData -= sz
	}
	if idx > 0 {
		pe.Sections[idx-1].Header.VirtualSize += pe.Sections[idx].Header.VirtualSize
		pe.SectionAlign(&pe.Sections[idx-1].Header.VirtualSize)
	}

	pe.Sections = append(pe.Sections[:idx], pe.Sections[idx+1:]...)
	pe.FileHeader.NumberOfSections--
}

func (pe *PEHeader) GetSectionByVA(va uint32) *PESection {
	for _, s := range pe.Sections {
		if va >= s.Header.VirtualAddress && uint64(va)-uint64(s.Header.VirtualAddress) < uint64(s.Header.VirtualSize) {
			return s
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func (pe *PEHeader) ConvertOffsetToRVAVector(offset uint64) uint64 {
	for _, s := range pe.Sections {
		rawStart := uint64(s.Header.PointerToRawData)
		rawEnd := rawStart + uint64(s.Header.SizeOfRawData)
		if offset >= rawStart && offset < rawEnd {
			return (offset - rawStart) + uint64(s.Header.VirtualAddress)
		}
	}
	return 0
}

func (pe *PEHeader) Sanitize() {
	for _, s := range pe.Sections {
		s.Header.PointerToRawData = s.Header.VirtualAddress
		s.Header.SizeOfRawData = s.Header.VirtualSize
	}
	if len(pe.Sections) > 0 {
		pe.SizeOfHeaders = pe.Sections[0].Header.PointerToRawData
		// Must have write access in code section (in case .text and .data were merged)
		pe.Sections[0].Header.Characteristics |= 0x80000000 // IMAGE_SCN_MEM_WRITE
	}
}

func (pe *PEHeader) TrimHugeSections(buf []byte, iatRawAddr *uint32) uint32 {
	currentSize := uint32(len(buf))
	var totalDelta uint32
	count := min(int(pe.FileHeader.NumberOfSections), len(pe.Sections))
	for i := 0; i < count; i++ {
		section := pe.Sections[i]
		start := section.Header.PointerToRawData
		rawSize := section.Header.SizeOfRawData
		if uint64(start)+uint64(rawSize) > uint64(currentSize) {
			continue
		}
		zeroStart := rawSize
		for zeroStart >= 4 && binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(buf[start+zeroStart-4:start+zeroStart]) == 0 {
			zeroStart -= 4
		}
		if rawSize-zeroStart <= 1024*1024 {
			continue
		}
		end := currentSize
		if i+1 < len(pe.Sections) {
			end = pe.Sections[i+1].Header.PointerToRawData
		}
		if end < start || end > currentSize {
			continue
		}
		newSize := zeroStart
		pe.FileAlign(&newSize)
		oldSize := end - start
		if newSize >= oldSize {
			continue
		}
		delta := oldSize - newSize
		copy(buf[start+newSize:currentSize-delta], buf[end:currentSize])
		currentSize -= delta
		totalDelta += delta
		section.Header.SizeOfRawData = newSize
		for j := i + 1; j < len(pe.Sections); j++ {
			pe.Sections[j].Header.PointerToRawData -= delta
		}
		if *iatRawAddr >= end {
			*iatRawAddr -= delta
		}
		log.Infof("Reducing section [%s]: 0x%X -> 0x%X", section.Header.SectionName(), oldSize, newSize)
	}
	return totalDelta
}

func (pe *PEHeader) SerializeNTHeaders() ([]byte, error) {
	buf := new(bytes.Buffer)
	// Signature
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, uint32(0x00004550))
	// FileHeader
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.FileHeader)

	// OptionalHeader
	if pe.Is64Bit {
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.Magic)
		buf.WriteByte(pe.MajorLinkerVersion)
		buf.WriteByte(pe.MinorLinkerVersion)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SizeOfCode)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SizeOfInitializedData)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SizeOfUninitializedData)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.AddressOfEntryPoint)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.BaseOfCode)

		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.ImageBase)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SectionAlignment)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.FileAlignment)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.MajorOperatingSystemVersion)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.MinorOperatingSystemVersion)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.MajorImageVersion)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.MinorImageVersion)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.MajorSubsystemVersion)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.MinorSubsystemVersion)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.Win32VersionValue)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SizeOfImage)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SizeOfHeaders)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.CheckSum)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.Subsystem)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.DllCharacteristics)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SizeOfStackReserve)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SizeOfStackCommit)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SizeOfHeapReserve)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SizeOfHeapCommit)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.LoaderFlags)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.NumberOfRvaAndSizes)
		for i := 0; i < 16; i++ {
			binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.DataDirectory[i])
		}
	} else {
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.Magic)
		buf.WriteByte(pe.MajorLinkerVersion)
		buf.WriteByte(pe.MinorLinkerVersion)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SizeOfCode)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SizeOfInitializedData)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SizeOfUninitializedData)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.AddressOfEntryPoint)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.BaseOfCode)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.BaseOfData)

		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, uint32(pe.ImageBase))
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SectionAlignment)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.FileAlignment)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.MajorOperatingSystemVersion)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.MinorOperatingSystemVersion)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.MajorImageVersion)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.MinorImageVersion)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.MajorSubsystemVersion)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.MinorSubsystemVersion)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.Win32VersionValue)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SizeOfImage)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.SizeOfHeaders)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.CheckSum)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.Subsystem)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.DllCharacteristics)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, uint32(pe.SizeOfStackReserve))
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, uint32(pe.SizeOfStackCommit))
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, uint32(pe.SizeOfHeapReserve))
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, uint32(pe.SizeOfHeapCommit))
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.LoaderFlags)
		binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.NumberOfRvaAndSizes)
		for i := 0; i < 16; i++ {
			binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, pe.DataDirectory[i])
		}
	}
	return buf.Bytes(), nil
}

func (pe *PEHeader) SaveToStream(s io.WriteSeeker) error {
	pe.FileHeader.NumberOfSections = uint16(len(pe.Sections))
	pe.FileHeader.SizeOfOptionalHeader = 224
	if pe.Is64Bit {
		pe.FileHeader.SizeOfOptionalHeader = 240
	}
	pe.NumberOfRvaAndSizes = 16
	headerEnd := uint64(pe.LFANew) + 24 + uint64(pe.FileHeader.SizeOfOptionalHeader) + uint64(len(pe.Sections))*40
	firstRaw := uint64(pe.SizeOfHeaders)
	for _, section := range pe.Sections {
		if section.Header.SizeOfRawData != 0 && uint64(section.Header.PointerToRawData) < firstRaw {
			firstRaw = uint64(section.Header.PointerToRawData)
		}
	}
	if headerEnd > firstRaw {
		return fmt.Errorf("section table does not fit in PE headers")
	}
	if _, err := s.Seek(int64(pe.LFANew), io.SeekStart); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	ntBytes, err := pe.SerializeNTHeaders()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if _, err := s.Write(ntBytes); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	for _, sec := range pe.Sections {
		if err := binary.Write(s, binary.LittleEndian, sec.Header); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	// Zero out leftovers
	zeroes := make([]byte, min(uint64(0x200), firstRaw-headerEnd))
	if _, err := s.Write(zeroes); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

```

`pkg/themida/common.go`:

```go
package themida

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"

	"unmida/pkg/debugger"
	"unmida/pkg/disasm"
	"unmida/pkg/dumper"
	"unmida/pkg/log"
	"unmida/pkg/pe"
	"unmida/pkg/tracer"
	"unmida/pkg/utils"
	"unmida/pkg/winapi"
)

type TMCommon struct {
	*debugger.DebuggerCore

	CreateDataSections bool
	BaseOfData         uintptr
	ImageBoundary      uintptr
	PESections         []pe.ImageSectionHeader
	MajorLinkerVersion byte

	TMSect  []byte
	TMSectR utils.MemoryRegion

	ThemidaV3 bool
	IsVMOEP   bool

	GuardAddrs []uintptr

	TracedAPI    uintptr
	SleepAPI     uintptr
	LstrlenAPI   uintptr
	TraceStartSP uintptr
	TraceInVM    bool
}

func (tm *TMCommon) InitPEDetails(header *pe.PEHeader) error {
	tm.PESections = make([]pe.ImageSectionHeader, len(header.Sections))
	for i, s := range header.Sections {
		tm.PESections[i] = s.Header
	}

	if len(tm.PESections) > 0 {
		firstSecEnd := uint32(tm.PESections[0].VirtualAddress + tm.PESections[0].VirtualSize)
		if header.AddressOfEntryPoint < firstSecEnd {
			return fmt.Errorf("the selected binary does not seem to be packed (entrypoint is in .text section)")
		}
	}

	tm.ImageBoundary = tm.ImageBase + uintptr(header.SizeOfImage)
	log.Infof("Image boundary: 0x%X", tm.ImageBoundary)

	tm.MajorLinkerVersion = header.MajorLinkerVersion
	log.Infof("Image linker: %d.%d", header.MajorLinkerVersion, header.MinorLinkerVersion)
	return nil
}

func (tm *TMCommon) CheckVirtualizedOEP(oep uintptr) {
	var code [5]byte
	if !tm.RPM(oep, code[:]) {
		return
	}
	if code[0] != 0xE9 {
		return
	}
	displ := int32(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(code[1:5]))
	target := uintptr(int64(oep) + 5 + int64(displ))

	if !tm.TMSectR.Contains(target) {
		return
	}

	tm.IsVMOEP = true
	log.Infof("OEP is virtualized (!): jmp 0x%X", target)
}

func findDelphiCall(codeDump []byte) uint32 {
	counter := 0
	for i := 0; i < len(codeDump)-6; i++ {
		// 0xFF 0x25: jmp dword ptr [...]
		if codeDump[i] == 0xFF && codeDump[i+1] == 0x25 {
			counter++
			if counter == 3 {
				return uint32(i)
			}
		}
	}
	return 0
}

func findGoBuildIdEnd(codeDump []byte) uint32 {
	for i := 0; i < len(codeDump)-2; i++ {
		// 0x20 0xFF: marks end of Go build ID
		if codeDump[i] == 0x20 && codeDump[i+1] == 0xFF {
			return uint32(i + 2)
		}
	}
	return 0
}

func (tm *TMCommon) DetermineIATAddress(oep uintptr, dmp *dumper.Dumper) (uintptr, error) {
	if len(tm.PESections) == 0 {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("no PE sections found")
	}

	if tm.BaseOfData <= uintptr(tm.PESections[0].VirtualAddress) {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("invalid BaseOfData")
	}
	dataSectionIndex := -1
	for i, s := range tm.PESections {
		if tm.BaseOfData >= uintptr(s.VirtualAddress) && tm.BaseOfData < uintptr(s.VirtualAddress)+uintptr(s.VirtualSize) {
			dataSectionIndex = i
			break
		}
	}

	if dataSectionIndex < 0 {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("BaseOfData is outside sections")
	}
	textBase := tm.ImageBase + uintptr(tm.PESections[0].VirtualAddress)
	codeSize := tm.BaseOfData - uintptr(tm.PESections[0].VirtualAddress)
	dataSec := tm.PESections[dataSectionIndex]
	dataSize := uintptr(dataSec.VirtualSize) - (tm.BaseOfData - uintptr(dataSec.VirtualAddress))

	log.Infof("Text base: 0x%08X, code size: 0x%X, data size: 0x%X", textBase, codeSize, dataSize)

	codeDump := make([]byte, codeSize)
	if !tm.RPM(textBase, codeDump) {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("DetermineIATAddress: RPM code failed")
	}

	numInstr := 0

	var findCallOrJmpPtr func(addr uintptr, ignoreBoundary bool) uintptr
	findCallOrJmpPtr = func(addr uintptr, ignoreBoundary bool) uintptr {
		for (numInstr < 200) || (ignoreBoundary && addr < textBase+codeSize) {
			numInstr++
			relOff := int(addr - textBase)
			if relOff < 0 || relOff >= len(codeDump) {
				return 0
			}

			ins, err := disasm.DecodeInstruction(codeDump[relOff:], tm.Is64Bit, uint64(addr))
			if err != nil || ins == nil {
				addr++
				continue
			}

			// Check call [addr] or jmp [addr] (FF 15 or FF 25)
			if ins.Opcode == 0xFF && (ins.Reg == 2 || ins.Reg == 4) && ins.HasDisplacement && (ins.AbsoluteMemory || ins.RIPRelative) {
				iatPtr := uintptr(uint32(ins.Displacement))
				if ins.RIPRelative {
					// RIP-relative in 64-bit
					iatPtr = uintptr(int64(addr) + int64(ins.Length) + ins.Displacement)
				}
				log.Infof("Found 0x%08X : call/jmp ptr [0x%08X]", ins.VirtualAddr, iatPtr)

				var thePtr uintptr
				var ptrBuf [8]byte
				ptrLen := 4
				if tm.Is64Bit {
					ptrLen = 8
				}
				if !tm.RPM(iatPtr, ptrBuf[:ptrLen]) {
					return iatPtr
				}
				if tm.Is64Bit {
					thePtr = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(ptrBuf[:8]))
				} else {
					thePtr = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(ptrBuf[:4]))
				}
				if thePtr > textBase+codeSize {
					return iatPtr
				}
			}

			// Direct call: E8
			if ins.Opcode == 0xE8 && !ignoreBoundary {
				target := uintptr(ins.AddrValue)
				if target < textBase || target >= textBase+codeSize {
					return 0
				}
				res := findCallOrJmpPtr(target, false)
				if res != 0 {
					return res
				}
			}

			// Return
			if ins.IsRet && !ignoreBoundary {
				return 0
			}

			if ins.Length > 0 {
				addr += uintptr(ins.Length)
			} else {
				addr++
			}
		}
		return 0
	}

	findGoAPICall := func(addr uintptr) uintptr {
		for addr < textBase+codeSize {
			relOff := int(addr - textBase)
			if relOff < 0 || relOff >= len(codeDump) {
				break
			}
			ins, err := disasm.DecodeInstruction(codeDump[relOff:], tm.Is64Bit, uint64(addr))
			if err != nil || ins == nil {
				addr++
				continue
			}

			if tm.Is64Bit {
				// mov rax, [addr] (8B ...) followed by mov [rsp], rax (48 89 04 24)
				if ins.Length == 7 && ins.Opcode == 0x8B && relOff+11 <= len(codeDump) {
					next4 := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(codeDump[relOff+7 : relOff+11])
					if next4 == 0x24048948 {
						iatPtr := uintptr(int64(addr) + 7 + ins.Displacement)
						var thePtrBuf [8]byte
						if tm.RPM(iatPtr, thePtrBuf[:]) {
							thePtr := uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(thePtrBuf[:]))
							if dmp.IsAPIAddress(thePtr) {
								return iatPtr
							}
						}
					}
				}
			} else {
				// mov eax, [addr] (8B ...) followed by mov [esp], eax (89 04 24)
				if ins.Length == 6 && ins.Opcode == 0x8B && relOff+10 <= len(codeDump) {
					next3 := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(codeDump[relOff+6 : relOff+10])
					if (next3 & 0x00FFFFFF) == 0x00240489 {
						iatPtr := uintptr(uint32(ins.Displacement))
						var thePtrBuf [4]byte
						if tm.RPM(iatPtr, thePtrBuf[:]) {
							thePtr := uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(thePtrBuf[:]))
							if dmp.IsAPIAddress(thePtr) {
								return iatPtr
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}

			if ins.Length > 0 {
				addr += uintptr(ins.Length)
			} else {
				addr++
			}
		}
		return 0
	}

	scanData := func(toFind uintptr) (uintptr, error) {
		startOffset := textBase + codeSize
		scanSize := dataSize
		dataSect := make([]byte, scanSize)
		if !tm.RPM(startOffset, dataSect) {
			return 0, fmt.Errorf("scanData RPM failed")
		}

		ptrSize := 4
		if tm.Is64Bit {
			ptrSize = 8
		}

		for i := 0; i+ptrSize <= len(dataSect); i += ptrSize {
			var val uintptr
			if tm.Is64Bit {
				val = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(dataSect[i : i+8]))
			} else {
				val = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(dataSect[i : i+4]))
			}
			if val == toFind {
				return startOffset + uintptr(i), nil
			}
		}

		// Fallback: scan text section as well
		codeScan := make([]byte, codeSize)
		if tm.RPM(textBase, codeScan) {
			for i := 0; i+ptrSize <= len(codeScan); i += ptrSize {
				var val uintptr
				if tm.Is64Bit {
					val = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(codeScan[i : i+8]))
				} else {
					val = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(codeScan[i : i+4]))
				}
				if val == toFind {
					return textBase + uintptr(i), nil
				}
			}
		}

		return 0, fmt.Errorf("unable to find API in section")
	}

	var iatRef uintptr
	if len(codeDump) >= 4 && binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(codeDump[:4]) == 0x6F4720FF { // \xFF Go
		iatRef = findGoAPICall(textBase + uintptr(findGoBuildIdEnd(codeDump)))
	} else if !tm.IsVMOEP {
		iatRef = findCallOrJmpPtr(oep, false)
	} else {
		delphiOffset := 6
		if tm.Is64Bit {
			delphiOffset = 10
		}
		if len(codeDump) >= delphiOffset+4 &&
			(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(codeDump[delphiOffset:delphiOffset+4]) == 0x6C6F6F42 || // 'Bool'
				binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(codeDump[6:10]) == 0x65747942) { // 'Byte'
			iatRef = findCallOrJmpPtr(textBase+uintptr(findDelphiCall(codeDump)), true)
		} else {
			iatRef = findCallOrJmpPtr(textBase, true)
		}
	}

	if iatRef == 0 {
		log.Infof("No IAT reference found via reference search")
		if len(tm.GuardAddrs) > 0 {
			firstGuard := tm.GuardAddrs[0]
			var site [6]byte
			tm.RPM(firstGuard, site[:])
			var target uintptr
			if site[0] == 0xE8 || site[0] == 0xE9 {
				displ := int32(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(site[1:5]))
				target = uintptr(int64(firstGuard) + 5 + int64(displ))
			} else if site[1] == 0xE8 || site[1] == 0xE9 {
				displ := int32(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(site[2:6]))
				target = uintptr(int64(firstGuard) + 6 + int64(displ))
			} else {
				return 0, fmt.Errorf("first guard addr is not call/jmp")
			}
			log.Infof("First guard addr 0x%08X yielded API 0x%08X", firstGuard, target)
			var err error
			iatRef, err = scanData(target)
			if err != nil {
				return 0, err
			}
		} else {
			return 0, fmt.Errorf("found no way to obtain IAT reference")
		}
	}

	log.Goodf("First IAT ref: 0x%08X", iatRef)

	// Seek backwards to find the beginning of the IAT
	ptrSize := 4
	if tm.Is64Bit {
		ptrSize = 8
	}
	iatReadCount := 512
	iatBufSize := iatReadCount * ptrSize
	if iatRef < tm.ImageBase+uintptr(ptrSize) {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("IAT reference is outside image")
	}
	if available := int((iatRef-tm.ImageBase)/uintptr(ptrSize)) + 1; available < iatReadCount {
		iatReadCount = available
		iatBufSize = iatReadCount * ptrSize
	}
	readStart := iatRef - uintptr(iatBufSize-ptrSize)

	iatData := make([]byte, iatBufSize)
	if !tm.RPM(readStart, iatData) {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("reading IAT backwards failed")
	}

	seeker := iatRef
	var startIAT uintptr
	consecutiveZeroes := 0

	for i := iatReadCount - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
		var val uintptr
		if tm.Is64Bit {
			val = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(iatData[i*8 : (i+1)*8]))
		} else {
			val = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(iatData[i*4 : (i+1)*4]))
		}

		if val == 0 {
			consecutiveZeroes++
			if consecutiveZeroes > 64 {
				break
			}
		} else if dmp.IsAPIAddress(val) || (tm.ThemidaV3 && tm.TMSectR.Contains(val)) {
			startIAT = seeker
			consecutiveZeroes = 0
		} else {
			log.Infof("Ending IAT start search at 0x%X because pointer is 0x%X", seeker, val)
			break
		}

		seeker -= uintptr(ptrSize)
	}

	if startIAT == 0 {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("IAT assertion failed")
	}

	return startIAT, nil
}

func (tm *TMCommon) TraceImports(iat uintptr, dmp *dumper.Dumper) error {
	ptrSize := 4
	if tm.Is64Bit {
		ptrSize = 8
	}
	slotCount := 5120
	iatData := make([]byte, slotCount*ptrSize)
	if !tm.RPM(iat, iatData) {
		return fmt.Errorf("TraceImports RPM failed")
	}

	didSetExitProcess := false
	trashCounter := 0

	k32, _ := winapi.GetModuleHandle("kernel32.dll")
	exitProcessAddr, err := winapi.GetProcAddress(k32, "ExitProcess")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	for i := 0; i < slotCount; i++ {
		var slotVal uintptr
		if tm.Is64Bit {
			slotVal = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(iatData[i*8 : (i+1)*8]))
		} else {
			slotVal = uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(iatData[i*4 : (i+1)*4]))
		}

		if tm.TMSectR.Contains(slotVal) {
			log.Infof("Trace: 0x%08X [0x%08X]", slotVal, iat+uintptr(i*ptrSize))
			trashCounter = 0

			hThread := tm.Threads[tm.CurrentThreadID]
			if tm.Is64Bit {
				var c winapi.CONTEXT64
				c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_64
				if err := winapi.GetThreadContext64(hThread, &c); err != nil {
					return err
				}
				tm.TraceStartSP = uintptr(c.Rsp)
			} else {
				var c winapi.CONTEXT32
				c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_32
				if err := winapi.GetThreadContext32(hThread, &c); err != nil {
					return err
				}
				tm.TraceStartSP = uintptr(c.Esp)
			}

			tm.TracedAPI = 0
			tm.TraceInVM = false

			predicate := func(tr *tracer.Tracer, c32 *winapi.CONTEXT32, c64 *winapi.CONTEXT64) bool {
				return tm.TraceIsAtAPI(tr, c32, c64)
			}

			tr := tracer.NewTracer(tm.ProcessInfo.ProcessId, tm.CurrentThreadID, hThread, tm.Is64Bit, predicate)
			if err := tr.Trace(slotVal, 500000); err != nil {
				tm.EventConsumed = !tr.Pending
				return err
			}
			tm.EventConsumed = !tr.Pending

			if tm.TraceInVM {
				if !didSetExitProcess {
					didSetExitProcess = true
					if tm.Is64Bit {
						binary.LittleEndian.PutUint64(iatData[i*8:(i+1)*8], uint64(exitProcessAddr))
					} else {
						binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(iatData[i*4:(i+1)*4], uint32(exitProcessAddr))
					}
					log.Infof("Setting API to ExitProcess")
				} else {
					return fmt.Errorf("unable to determine IAT address for 0x%X", iat+uintptr(i*ptrSize))
				}
			} else if tm.TracedAPI != 0 {
				log.Infof("-> 0x%08X", tm.TracedAPI)
				if tm.TracedAPI < 0x10000 || (tm.TracedAPI >= tm.ImageBase && tm.TracedAPI < tm.ImageBoundary) {
					log.Infof("Discarding result & aborting IAT tracing")
					break
				}
				if tm.Is64Bit {
					binary.LittleEndian.PutUint64(iatData[i*8:(i+1)*8], uint64(tm.TracedAPI))
				} else {
					binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(iatData[i*4:(i+1)*4], uint32(tm.TracedAPI))
				}
			} else {
				return fmt.Errorf("import tracing failed")
			}
		} else if slotVal == 0 || !dmp.IsAPIAddress(slotVal) {
			trashCounter++
			if trashCounter > 64 {
				break
			}
		} else {
			trashCounter = 0
		}
	}

	if err := tm.WriteMemory(iat, iatData); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("writing resolved IAT: %w", err)
	}

	return nil
}

func (tm *TMCommon) TraceIsAtAPI(tr *tracer.Tracer, c32 *winapi.CONTEXT32, c64 *winapi.CONTEXT64) bool {
	var ip, sp uintptr
	if tm.Is64Bit {
		ip = uintptr(c64.Rip)
		sp = uintptr(c64.Rsp)
	} else {
		ip = uintptr(c32.Eip)
		sp = uintptr(c32.Esp)
	}

	if tr.Counter > 100 && tr.Counter < 5000 {
		var insnData [4]byte
		if tm.RPM(ip, insnData[:]) {
			val := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(insnData[:])
			if tm.Is64Bit && val == 0x0CB10FF0 { // lock cmpxchg [rbx+rbp], ecx
				tm.TraceInVM = true
				log.Infof("Trace ran into Themida VM, stopping")
				return true
			} else if !tm.Is64Bit && val == 0x4CB10FF0 { // lock cmpxchg [ebp+ebx+0], ecx
				tm.TraceInVM = true
				log.Infof("Trace ran into Themida VM, stopping")
				return true
			}
		}
	}

	// Fake calls to Sleep / lstrlen
	if sp < tm.TraceStartSP && (ip == tm.SleepAPI || ip == tm.LstrlenAPI) {
		log.Infof("Skipping anti-trace API at 0x%X", ip)
		if tm.Is64Bit {
			var retAddr [8]byte
			if !tm.RPM(sp, retAddr[:]) {
				return false
			}
			c64.Rsp += 8
			c64.Rip = binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(retAddr[:])
		} else {
			var retAddr [4]byte
			if !tm.RPM(sp, retAddr[:]) {
				return false
			}
			c32.Esp += 8
			c32.Eip = binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(retAddr[:])
		}
	}

	if tm.Is64Bit {
		ip = uintptr(c64.Rip)
		sp = uintptr(c64.Rsp)
	} else {
		ip = uintptr(c32.Eip)
		sp = uintptr(c32.Esp)
	}
	res := !tm.TMSectR.Contains(ip)
	if res && sp < tm.TraceStartSP {
		log.Infof("Warning: Might have encountered new fake API at 0x%X", ip)
		res = false
	}

	if res {
		tm.TracedAPI = ip
	}
	return res
}

```

`pkg/themida/iat32.go`:

```go
package themida

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"

	"golang.org/x/arch/x86/x86asm"
	"unmida/pkg/debugger"
	"unmida/pkg/disasm"
	"unmida/pkg/log"
	"unmida/pkg/utils"
	"unmida/pkg/winapi"
)

func (tm *TMDebugger32) find(pattern string, start uintptr) uintptr {
	if start == 0 {
		start = tm.TMSectR.Address
	}
	if start < tm.TMSectR.Address || start-tm.TMSectR.Address >= uintptr(len(tm.TMSect)) {
		return 0
	}
	offset := start - tm.TMSectR.Address
	found := utils.FindDynamic(pattern, tm.TMSect[offset:])
	if found < 0 {
		return 0
	}
	return start + uintptr(found)
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) requirePattern(pattern string, start uintptr) uintptr {
	address := tm.find(pattern, start)
	if address == 0 {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("Themida pattern %s not found", pattern))
	}
	return address
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) sectionBytes(addr uintptr, length int) []byte {
	if addr < tm.TMSectR.Address || length < 0 {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("invalid Themida section address 0x%X", addr))
		return nil
	}
	offset := addr - tm.TMSectR.Address
	if offset > uintptr(len(tm.TMSect)) || uintptr(length) > uintptr(len(tm.TMSect))-offset {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("Themida section read out of bounds at 0x%X", addr))
		return nil
	}
	return tm.TMSect[offset : offset+uintptr(length)]
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) section32(addr uintptr) uint32 {
	data := tm.sectionBytes(addr, 4)
	if data == nil {
		return 0
	}
	return binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data)
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) TMIATFix2() {
	compare := tm.find("74??8B8D????????8B093B8D????????7410", 0)
	if compare == 0 {
		first := tm.requirePattern("3D000001000F83", 0)
		if tm.Err != nil {
			return
		}
		tm.MagicJumpV1 = tm.requirePattern("3B8D????????0F84????0000", first)
		if tm.Err == nil {
			tm.bp(tm.MagicJumpV1, debugger.HWExecute)
		}
	} else {
		tm.CmpImgBase = compare
		tm.bp(compare, debugger.HWExecute)
	}
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) subtraction(start uintptr) uintptr {
	address := tm.find("2BD90F84", start)
	if address == 0 {
		address = tm.find("29CB0F84", start)
	}
	if address == 0 {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("IAT subtraction pattern not found"))
	}
	return address
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) TMIATFix4() {
	tm.requirePattern("83F8500F82", 0)
	compare := tm.requirePattern("3985????????0F84", 0)
	if tm.Err != nil {
		return
	}
	tm.IJumper = compare + 6
	tm.MJ2 = tm.subtraction(compare)
	if tm.Err != nil {
		return
	}
	target := uint32(tm.MJ2+8) + tm.section32(tm.MJ2+4)
	tm.MJ3 = tm.subtraction(tm.MJ2 + 1)
	if tm.Err != nil {
		return
	}
	if uint32(tm.MJ3+8)+tm.section32(tm.MJ3+4) != target {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("old magic jump: targets differ"))
		return
	}
	tm.MJ4 = tm.subtraction(tm.MJ3 + 1)
	if tm.Err != nil {
		return
	}
	for address := tm.MJ2; address >= tm.TMSectR.Address; address-- {
		data := tm.sectionBytes(address, 6)
		if data == nil {
			return
		}
		if data[0] == 0x0F && data[1] == 0x84 && uint32(address+6)+binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[2:]) == target {
			tm.MJ1 = address
			break
		}
		if address == tm.TMSectR.Address {
			break
		}
	}
	if tm.MJ1 == 0 {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("first magic jump not found"))
		return
	}
	tm.NewVer = ((tm.sectionBytes(tm.MJ1-1, 1)[0] == 0x4B && tm.sectionBytes(tm.MJ2, 1)[0] == 0x2B && tm.sectionBytes(tm.MJ3, 1)[0] == 0x2B && tm.sectionBytes(tm.MJ4, 1)[0] == 0x2B) || tm.sectionBytes(tm.MJ2, 1)[0] == 0x29)
	if tm.find("68????????E9??????FF68????????E9??????FF68????????E9??????FF", 0) != 0 {
		tm.NewVer = false
	}
	if tm.find("68????????68????????E9??????FF68????????68????????E9??????FF", 0) != 0 {
		tm.NewVer = true
	}
	log.Infof("Newer Themida IAT variant: %t", tm.NewVer)
	address := tm.find("3BC89CE9", 0)
	if address != 0 {
		for address != 0 {
			if err := tm.SetSoftBP(address + 3); err != nil {
				tm.Fail(err)
				return
			}
			address = tm.find("3BC89CE9", address+3)
		}
	} else {
		start := tm.TMSectR.Address
		for {
			address = tm.find("39??9C", start)
			if address == 0 {
				break
			}
			start = address + 1
			if address > tm.TMSectR.Address && tm.sectionBytes(address-1, 1)[0] == 0x66 {
				continue
			}
			offset := address - tm.TMSectR.Address
			ins, err := disasm.DecodeInstruction(tm.TMSect[offset:], false, uint64(address))
			if err == nil && ins.Length == 2 && address <= tm.MJ1 {
				if err = tm.SetSoftBP(address + 3); err != nil {
					tm.Fail(err)
					return
				}
			}
		}
	}
	tm.InstallCodeSectionGuard(winapi.PAGE_READONLY)
	tm.bp(tm.MJ1, debugger.HWExecute)
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) TMIATFix5() {
	tm.write(tm.IJumper, []byte{0x90, 0xE9})
	nops := bytes.Repeat([]byte{0x90}, 6)
	for _, address := range []uintptr{tm.MJ1, tm.MJ2 + 2, tm.MJ3 + 2, tm.MJ4 + 2} {
		tm.write(address, nops)
	}
	log.Goodf("IAT jumper patched at 0x%X", tm.IJumper)
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) TMIATFixV1(first uintptr) {
	second := tm.requirePattern("3B8D????????0F84????0000", first+12)
	if tm.Err != nil {
		return
	}
	third := tm.requirePattern("3B8D????????0F84????0000", second+12)
	tm.IJumper = tm.requirePattern("3985????????0F84", 0)
	if tm.Err != nil {
		return
	}
	tm.write(tm.IJumper+6, []byte{0x90, 0xE9})
	for _, address := range []uintptr{first + 6, second + 6, third + 6} {
		tm.write(address, bytes.Repeat([]byte{0x90}, 6))
	}
	tm.GuardStart = tm.ImageBase + uintptr(tm.PESections[0].VirtualAddress)
	tm.GuardEnd = tm.ImageBase + 0x100000
	if tm.GuardEnd > tm.ImageBoundary {
		tm.GuardEnd = tm.ImageBoundary
	}
	tm.GuardProtection = winapi.PAGE_NOACCESS
	if _, err := winapi.VirtualProtectEx(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, tm.GuardStart, tm.GuardEnd-tm.GuardStart, tm.GuardProtection); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
	}
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) moduleBases() [3]uint32 {
	var bases [3]uint32
	for i, name := range []string{"kernel32.dll", "user32.dll", "advapi32.dll"} {
		h, err := winapi.GetModuleHandle(name)
		if err != nil {
			h, err = winapi.LoadLibrary(name)
		}
		if err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
			return bases
		}
		bases[i] = uint32(h)
	}
	return bases
}

func jump32(from, to uintptr) []byte {
	b := []byte{0xE9, 0, 0, 0, 0}
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(b[1:], uint32(to)-uint32(from)-5)
	return b
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) installOldIATPatch(ip uintptr, bases [3]uint32) {
	target := uintptr(uint32(ip+5) + tm.section32(ip+1))
	if tm.Err != nil {
		return
	}
	destination, err := winapi.VirtualAllocEx(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, 0, 128, winapi.MEM_COMMIT|winapi.MEM_RESERVE, winapi.PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE)
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return
	}
	data := make([]byte, 41)
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint16(data[0:], 0xF881)
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(data[2:], bases[0])
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(data[6:], 0xF8811574)
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(data[10:], bases[2])
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(data[14:], 0xF8810D74)
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(data[18:], bases[1])
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint16(data[22:], 0x0574)
	copy(data[24:], jump32(destination+24, target))
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint64(data[29:], 0xE9000002872404C7)
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(data[37:], uint32(target)-uint32(destination+36)-5)
	tm.write(destination, data)
	tm.write(ip, jump32(ip, destination))
	log.Goodf("Special IAT patch written at 0x%X", ip)
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) InstallEFLPatch(ip uintptr, c *winapi.CONTEXT32, record *EFLRecord) bool {
	bases := tm.moduleBases()
	if tm.Err != nil {
		return false
	}
	registers := []uint32{c.Eax, c.Ecx, c.Edx, c.Ebx, c.Esp, c.Ebp, c.Esi, c.Edi}
	register := -1
	for _, base := range bases {
		for i, value := range registers {
			if i != 4 && value == base {
				register = i
				break
			}
		}
		if register >= 0 {
			break
		}
	}
	if register < 0 {
		return false
	}
	if !tm.TMSectR.Contains(ip) {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("EFL patch outside Themida section"))
		return false
	}
	offset := ip - tm.TMSectR.Address
	total := 0
	for total < 5 {
		ins, err := disasm.DecodeInstruction(tm.TMSect[offset+uintptr(total):], false, uint64(ip)+uint64(total))
		if err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
			return false
		}
		if ins.IsJmp || ins.IsCall || ins.IsRet {
			tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("EFL patch would relocate control flow at 0x%X", ip+uintptr(total)))
			return false
		}
		for _, arg := range ins.Decoded.Args {
			if _, ok := arg.(x86asm.Rel); ok {
				tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("EFL patch contains relative operand"))
				return false
			}
		}
		total += ins.Length
	}
	if 55+total+5 > 128 {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("EFL patch exceeds allocation"))
		return false
	}
	destination, err := winapi.VirtualAllocEx(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, 0, 128, winapi.MEM_COMMIT|winapi.MEM_RESERVE, winapi.PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE)
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return false
	}
	data := bytes.Repeat([]byte{0x90}, 128)
	for i, base := range bases {
		at := i * 8
		data[at], data[at+1] = 0x81, 0xF8+byte(register)
		binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(data[at+2:], base)
		data[at+6], data[at+7] = 0x74, byte(0x28-i*8)
	}
	data[24], data[25] = 0xEB, 0x1D
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint64(data[48:], 0x90000002462404C7)
	original := tm.sectionBytes(ip, total)
	if original == nil {
		return false
	}
	copy(data[55:], original)
	copy(data[55+total:], jump32(destination+55+uintptr(total), ip+uintptr(total)))
	tm.write(destination, data)
	tm.write(ip, jump32(ip, destination))
	var preceding [6]byte
	if ip >= 9 && tm.RPM(ip-9, preceding[:]) && preceding[0] == 0x0F && (preceding[1] == 0x84 || preceding[1] == 0x85) {
		displacement := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(preceding[2:])
		if displacement >= 4 && displacement <= 7 {
			binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(preceding[2:], uint32(destination+55)+displacement-3-uint32(ip-9)-6)
			tm.write(ip-7, preceding[2:])
		}
	}
	if tm.Err != nil {
		return false
	}
	record.Address = ip
	record.Original = append([]byte(nil), original...)
	log.Goodf("EFL patch at 0x%X", ip)
	return true
}
```
