`pkg/themida/oep32.go`:

```go
package themida

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"path/filepath"
	"sort"
	"syscall"

	"unmida/pkg/antidump"
	"unmida/pkg/dumper"
	"unmida/pkg/log"
	"unmida/pkg/patcher"
	"unmida/pkg/winapi"
)

func (tm *TMDebugger32) IsTMExceptionHandler(addr uintptr) bool {
	var data [64]byte
	if !tm.RPM(addr, data[:]) {
		return false
	}
	return bytes.Contains(data[:], []byte{0x8B, 0x83, 0xB8, 0x00})
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) RestoreStolenOEPForMSVC6(thread syscall.Handle, oep uintptr) uintptr {
	var check [3]byte
	if !tm.RPM(oep, check[:2]) || check[0] != 0x8A || check[1] != 0xD4 {
		return oep
	}
	restore := []byte{0x55, 0x8B, 0xEC, 0x6A, 0xFF, 0x68, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0x68, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0x64, 0xA1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0x50, 0x64, 0x89, 0x25, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0x83, 0xEC, 0x58, 0x53, 0x56, 0x57, 0x89, 0x65, 0xE8, 0xFF, 0x15, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0x33, 0xD2}
	if oep < uintptr(len(restore)+3) || !tm.RPM(oep-uintptr(len(restore))-3, check[:]) || (check[0] != 0xC2 && check[2] != 0xC3) {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("stolen MSVC6 OEP gap mismatch"))
		return oep
	}
	versionAPI := tm.api("kernel32.dll", "GetVersion")
	iat := tm.ImageBase + tm.BaseOfData
	var iatData [512 * 4]byte
	if !tm.RPM(iat, iatData[:]) {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("reading MSVC6 IAT failed"))
		return oep
	}
	found := false
	for i := 0; i < 512; i++ {
		if uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(iatData[i*4:])) == versionAPI {
			binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(restore[len(restore)-6:], uint32(iat+uintptr(i*4)))
			found = true
			break
		}
	}
	if !found {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("GetVersion not found in MSVC6 IAT"))
		return oep
	}
	var c winapi.CONTEXT32
	c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_INTEGER_32 | winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_32
	if err := winapi.GetThreadContext32(thread, &c); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return oep
	}
	if c.Esp != c.Ebp-0x74 {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("MSVC6 stack frame mismatch"))
		return oep
	}
	var stack [8]byte
	if !tm.RPM(uintptr(c.Ebp)-12, stack[:]) {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("reading MSVC6 exception frame failed"))
		return oep
	}
	copy(restore[6:10], stack[4:8])
	copy(restore[11:15], stack[0:4])
	oep -= uintptr(len(restore))
	tm.write(oep, restore)
	log.Goodf("Restored MSVC6 OEP: 0x%X", oep)
	return oep
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) RestoreStolenOEPForMSVC9DLL(oep uintptr) uintptr {
	var first [1]byte
	if !tm.RPM(oep, first[:]) || first[0] != 0xE8 || oep < 11 {
		return oep
	}
	if !tm.RPM(oep-11, first[:]) || first[0] != 0xE9 {
		return oep
	}
	oep -= 11
	tm.write(oep, []byte{0x8B, 0xFF, 0x55, 0x8B, 0xEC, 0x83, 0x7D, 0x0C, 0x01, 0x75, 0x05})
	log.Goodf("Restored MSVC9 DLL OEP: 0x%X", oep)
	return oep
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) TryFindCorrectOEP(oep uintptr) uintptr {
	switch tm.MajorLinkerVersion {
	case 9, 10, 11, 12, 14:
	default:
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("cannot restore stolen OEP for linker %d", tm.MajorLinkerVersion))
		return oep
	}
	start := tm.ImageBase + uintptr(tm.PESections[0].VirtualAddress)
	if tm.BaseOfData <= uintptr(tm.PESections[0].VirtualAddress) {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("invalid text boundary"))
		return oep
	}
	data := make([]byte, tm.BaseOfData-uintptr(tm.PESections[0].VirtualAddress))
	if !tm.RPM(start, data) {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("reading text for OEP search failed"))
		return oep
	}
	for i := 0; i+10 <= len(data); i++ {
		if data[i] == 0xE8 && data[i+5] == 0xE9 && uint32(start)+uint32(i)+5+binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[i+1:]) == uint32(oep) {
			log.Goodf("Found real OEP: 0x%X", start+uintptr(i))
			return start + uintptr(i)
		}
	}
	tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("real OEP not found"))
	return oep
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) FixupAPICallSites(iat uintptr) error {
	if len(tm.GuardAddrs) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	sort.Slice(tm.GuardAddrs, func(i, j int) bool { return tm.GuardAddrs[i] < tm.GuardAddrs[j] })
	sites := []uintptr{tm.GuardAddrs[0]}
	for _, addr := range tm.GuardAddrs[1:] {
		if addr-sites[len(sites)-1] >= 6 {
			sites = append(sites, addr)
		}
	}
	data := make([]byte, 5120*4)
	if !tm.RPM(iat, data) {
		return fmt.Errorf("reading IAT for call site repair failed")
	}
	slots := make(map[uint32]uint32)
	for i := 0; i < len(data); i += 4 {
		slots[binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data[i:])] = uint32(iat) + uint32(i)
	}
	for _, addr := range sites {
		var site [6]byte
		if !tm.RPM(addr, site[:]) {
			return fmt.Errorf("reading call site 0x%X failed", addr)
		}
		opcodeOffset := -1
		if site[0] == 0xE8 || site[0] == 0xE9 {
			opcodeOffset = 0
		} else if site[1] == 0xE8 || site[1] == 0xE9 {
			opcodeOffset = 1
		}
		if opcodeOffset < 0 {
			if site[0] == 0xFF && (site[1] == 0x15 || site[1] == 0x25) {
				continue
			}
			return fmt.Errorf("unknown call site at 0x%X", addr)
		}
		target := uint32(addr) + uint32(opcodeOffset) + 5 + binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(site[opcodeOffset+1:])
		slot, ok := slots[target]
		if !ok {
			return fmt.Errorf("call site 0x%X target 0x%X is not in IAT", addr, target)
		}
		jump := site[opcodeOffset] == 0xE9
		site[0], site[1] = 0xFF, 0x15
		if jump {
			site[1] = 0x25
		}
		binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(site[2:], slot)
		if err := tm.WriteMemory(addr, site[:]); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	log.Goodf("Restored %d API call sites", len(sites))
	return nil
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) FinishUnpacking(oep uintptr) {
	for _, record := range tm.EFLs {
		if record.Address != 0 {
			tm.write(record.Address, record.Original)
		}
	}
	if tm.Err != nil {
		return
	}
	dump := dumper.NewDumper(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, tm.ProcessInfo.ProcessId, tm.Executable, tm.ImageBase, oep, false)
	iat, err := tm.DetermineIATAddress(oep, dump)
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return
	}
	log.Goodf("IAT: 0x%X", iat)
	if tm.ThemidaV3 {
		if err = tm.TraceImports(iat, dump); err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
			return
		}
	}
	if err = tm.FixupAPICallSites(iat); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return
	}
	if tm.IsVMOEP && tm.ThemidaV3 {
		if err = antidump.NewAntiDumpFixer(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, tm.ImageBase).RedirectOEP(oep, iat); err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
			return
		}
	}
	dump.IAT = iat
	header, err := dump.Process()
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return
	}
	out := dumper.ChangeFileExt(tm.Executable, "U"+filepath.Ext(tm.Executable))
	if err = dump.DumpToFile(out, header, tm.IsDLL); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return
	}
	out = dump.OutputFile
	if tm.CreateDataSections {
		patch, err := patcher.NewPatcher(out)
		if err == nil {
			err = patch.ProcessMkData()
		}
		if err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
			return
		}
	}
	tm.HideThreadEnd = true
	tm.Succeeded = true
	tm.ShouldTerminate = true
	log.Goodf("Operation completed successfully. Output saved to: %s", out)
}

```

`pkg/themida/themida32.go`:

```go
package themida

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"syscall"

	"unmida/pkg/debugger"
	"unmida/pkg/dumper"
	"unmida/pkg/log"
	"unmida/pkg/pe"
	"unmida/pkg/utils"
	"unmida/pkg/winapi"
)

type EFLRecord struct {
	Address  uintptr
	Original []byte
}

type TMDebugger32 struct {
	*TMCommon
	Wow64, Compressed, BaseAccessed, NewVer, AncientVer, ThemidaV2BySections                       bool
	BaseAccessCount, AllocMemCounter                                                               int
	Base1, RepEIP, NtQIP                                                                           uintptr
	CloseHandleAPI, AllocMemAPI, AllocHeapAPI, KiFastSystemCall, NtSIT, NtQIP64, VirtualProtectAPI uintptr
	CorExeMain, CmpImgBase, MagicJump, MagicJumpV1                                                 uintptr
	IJumper, MJ1, MJ2, MJ3, MJ4                                                                    uintptr
	EFLs                                                                                           [3]EFLRecord
	GuardStart, GuardEnd                                                                           uintptr
	GuardProtection                                                                                uint32
	GuardStepping                                                                                  bool
	TLSAddressesOfCallbacks                                                                        uintptr
	TLSCounter, TLSTotal                                                                           int
}

func NewDebugger32(executable, parameters string, createData bool) (*TMDebugger32, error) {
	tm := &TMDebugger32{TMCommon: &TMCommon{CreateDataSections: createData}}
	tm.DebuggerCore = debugger.NewDebuggerCore(executable, parameters, tm)
	return tm, nil
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) read32(addr uintptr) uint32 {
	var b [4]byte
	if !tm.RPM(addr, b[:]) {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("reading DWORD at 0x%X failed", addr))
		return 0
	}
	return binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(b[:])
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) api(module, name string) uintptr {
	h, err := winapi.GetModuleHandle(module)
	if err != nil {
		h, err = winapi.LoadLibrary(module)
	}
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return 0
	}
	address, err := winapi.GetProcAddress(h, name)
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("%s!%s: %w", module, name, err))
	}
	return address
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) bp(addr uintptr, kind debugger.HWBPType) {
	if addr == 0 {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("cannot set breakpoint at zero"))
		return
	}
	if err := tm.SetBreakpoint(addr, kind, true); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
	}
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) write(addr uintptr, data []byte) {
	if err := tm.WriteMemory(addr, data); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("patching 0x%X: %w", addr, err))
	}
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) OnDebugStart(hFile, hThread syscall.Handle) {
	if len(filepath.Base(tm.Executable)) >= 50 {
		log.Infof("Long filenames crash some Themida versions; recommend fewer than 50 characters")
	}
	tm.CloseHandleAPI = tm.api("kernel32.dll", "CloseHandle")
	tm.bp(tm.CloseHandleAPI, debugger.HWExecute)
	executable, err := os.Executable()
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return
	}
	injector := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(executable), "InjectorCLIx86.exe")
	if _, err := os.Stat(injector); err == nil {
		cmd := exec.Command(injector, fmt.Sprintf("pid:%d", tm.ProcessInfo.ProcessId), filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(executable), "HookLibraryx86.dll"), "nowait")
		cmd.Dir = filepath.Dir(executable)
		cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{HideWindow: true}
		if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
			return
		}
		go cmd.Wait()
		log.Goodf("Applying ScyllaHide")
	} else {
		tm.NtSIT = tm.api("ntdll.dll", "ZwSetInformationThread")
		tm.bp(tm.NtSIT, debugger.HWExecute)
		tm.Wow64, err = winapi.IsWow64Process(tm.ProcessInfo.Process)
		if err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
			return
		}
		if tm.Wow64 {
			tm.NtQIP64 = tm.api("ntdll.dll", "ZwQueryInformationProcess")
			tm.bp(tm.NtQIP64, debugger.HWExecute)
		} else {
			tm.KiFastSystemCall = tm.api("ntdll.dll", "KiFastSystemCall")
			if err := tm.SetSoftBP(tm.KiFastSystemCall); err != nil {
				tm.Fail(err)
				return
			}
			tm.NtQIP = uintptr(tm.read32(tm.api("ntdll.dll", "ZwQueryInformationProcess") + 1))
		}
	}
	tm.VirtualProtectAPI = tm.api("kernel32.dll", "VirtualProtect")
	tm.SleepAPI = tm.api("kernel32.dll", "Sleep")
	tm.LstrlenAPI = tm.api("kernel32.dll", "lstrlenA")
	data, err := os.ReadFile(tm.Executable)
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return
	}
	header, err := pe.ParsePEHeader(data)
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return
	}
	if err = tm.InitPEDetails(header); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return
	}
	n := len(tm.PESections)
	if n >= 2 {
		tm.ThemidaV2BySections = tm.PESections[n-1].VirtualSize == 0x1000 && tm.PESections[n-1].SizeOfRawData == 0x1000 && tm.PESections[n-2].VirtualSize == 0x1000
	}
	tm.BaseOfData = uintptr(header.BaseOfData)
	tm.Compressed = tm.PESections[0].VirtualSize != tm.PESections[0].SizeOfRawData
	tm.Base1 = uintptr(tm.PESections[0].VirtualSize)
	if n > 2 {
		nameAddr := tm.ImageBase + uintptr(header.LFANew) + 24 + uintptr(header.FileHeader.SizeOfOptionalHeader) + 2*40 + 1
		tm.write(nameAddr, []byte{'p'})
	}
	tls := header.DataDirectory[winapi.IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_TLS]
	if tls.Size >= 24 {
		var b [24]byte
		if tm.RPM(tm.ImageBase+uintptr(tls.VirtualAddress), b[:]) {
			index := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(b[8:12])
			callbacks := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(b[12:16])
			distance := int64(callbacks) - int64(index)
			if distance > 0 && distance <= 16 {
				tm.TLSTotal = int(distance / 4)
				tm.TLSAddressesOfCallbacks = uintptr(callbacks)
				log.Infof("Expecting up to %d TLS entries", tm.TLSTotal)
			}
		}
	}
	tm.AllocMemAPI = tm.api("ntdll.dll", "ZwAllocateVirtualMemory")
	tm.AllocHeapAPI = tm.api("ntdll.dll", "RtlAllocateHeap")
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) SelectThemidaSection(ip uintptr) error {
	for _, section := range tm.PESections {
		region := utils.MemoryRegion{Address: tm.ImageBase + uintptr(section.VirtualAddress), Size: uintptr(section.VirtualSize)}
		if region.Contains(ip) {
			tm.TMSectR = region
			tm.TMSect = make([]byte, region.Size)
			if !tm.RPM(region.Address, tm.TMSect) {
				return fmt.Errorf("reading Themida section failed")
			}
			tm.AncientVer = string(section.Name[:]) == "Themida "
			log.Infof("TMSect: 0x%X (%d bytes), ancient: %t", region.Address, region.Size, tm.AncientVer)
			return nil
		}
	}
	return fmt.Errorf("no Themida section for 0x%X", ip)
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) finderCheck(c *winapi.CONTEXT32) bool {
	var b [2]byte
	if tm.RPM(uintptr(c.Eip), b[:]) && b == [2]byte{0xF3, 0xA4} {
		return true
	}
	target := uint32(tm.ImageBase + 0x1000 + tm.Base1 - 4)
	return c.Eax == target || c.Ebx == target || c.Ecx == target || c.Edx == target || c.Esi == target || c.Edi == target
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) OnHardwareBreakpoint(hThread syscall.Handle, bpa uintptr, c *winapi.CONTEXT32, unused *winapi.CONTEXT64) {
	ip := uintptr(c.Eip)
	switch ip {
	case tm.CloseHandleAPI:
		ret := uintptr(tm.read32(uintptr(c.Esp)))
		if ret < tm.ImageBoundary {
			tm.ResetBreakpoint(ip)
			if tm.Compressed {
				tm.bp(tm.ImageBase+0x1000, debugger.HWAccess)
			} else {
				tm.bp(tm.AllocMemAPI, debugger.HWExecute)
			}
		}
	case tm.AllocMemAPI:
		frame := tm.read32(uintptr(c.Ebp))
		distance := int64(frame) - int64(c.Ebp)
		var ret uintptr
		if distance > -0x40 && distance < 0x40 {
			ret = uintptr(tm.read32(uintptr(frame) + 4))
		} else {
			ret = uintptr(tm.read32(uintptr(c.Ebp) + 4))
		}
		log.Infof("AllocMem called from 0x%X", ret)
		if ret > tm.ImageBase && ret < tm.ImageBoundary {
			tm.AllocMemCounter++
			required := 5
			if tm.Compressed {
				required = 4
			}
			if tm.AllocMemCounter == required {
				tm.ResetBreakpoint(tm.AllocMemAPI)
				if tm.ThemidaV3 {
					tm.InstallCodeSectionGuard(winapi.PAGE_NOACCESS)
				} else {
					if err := tm.SelectThemidaSection(ret); err != nil {
						tm.Fail(err)
						return
					}
					tm.TMIATFix2()
				}
			}
		}
	case tm.CmpImgBase:
		tm.ResetBreakpoint(ip)
		tm.MagicJump = tm.find("4B0F84????0000", ip)
		if tm.MagicJump == 0 {
			tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("magic jumps not found"))
			return
		}
		tm.bp(tm.MagicJump, debugger.HWExecute)
	case tm.MagicJump:
		tm.ResetBreakpoint(ip)
		tm.TMIATFix4()
	case tm.MJ1:
		tm.ResetBreakpoint(ip)
		tm.TMIATFix5()
	case tm.MagicJumpV1:
		tm.ResetBreakpoint(ip)
		tm.TMIATFixV1(ip)
	case tm.AllocHeapAPI:
		log.Infof("Special IAT fix unavailable; continuing with code section tracing")
		tm.ResetBreakpoint(ip)
		tm.SoftBPClear()
		tm.InstallCodeSectionGuard(winapi.PAGE_NOACCESS)
	case tm.VirtualProtectAPI:
		tm.InstallCodeSectionGuard(winapi.PAGE_NOACCESS)
	case tm.NtSIT:
		ret := tm.read32(uintptr(c.Esp))
		if uintptr(ret) < tm.ImageBoundary && tm.read32(uintptr(c.Esp)+8) == 17 {
			c.Esp += 20
			c.Eip = ret
			c.Eax = winapi.STATUS_SUCCESS
			c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_32 | winapi.CONTEXT_INTEGER_32
			if err := winapi.SetThreadContext32(hThread, c); err != nil {
				tm.Fail(err)
			}
			log.Goodf("Ignoring NtSetInformationThread(ThreadHideFromDebugger)")
		}
	case tm.NtQIP64:
		ret := tm.read32(uintptr(c.Esp))
		infoClass := tm.read32(uintptr(c.Esp) + 8)
		if infoClass == 7 || infoClass == 30 {
			result := uintptr(tm.read32(uintptr(c.Esp) + 12))
			tm.write(result, make([]byte, 4))
			c.Esp += 24
			c.Eip = ret
			c.Eax = winapi.STATUS_SUCCESS
			if infoClass == 30 {
				c.Eax = winapi.STATUS_PORT_NOT_SET
			}
			c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_32 | winapi.CONTEXT_INTEGER_32
			if err := winapi.SetThreadContext32(hThread, c); err != nil {
				tm.Fail(err)
			}
			log.Goodf("Faking process debug information (%d)", infoClass)
		}
	default:
		if bpa != tm.ImageBase+0x1000 {
			log.Infof("Accessed 0x%X from 0x%X", bpa, ip)
			return
		}
		tm.BaseAccessCount++
		if !tm.BaseAccessed {
			tm.ResetBreakpoint(bpa)
			tm.bp(bpa, debugger.HWWrite)
			tm.BaseAccessed = true
		} else if tm.finderCheck(c) {
			tm.ResetBreakpoint(bpa)
			tm.bp(tm.AllocMemAPI, debugger.HWExecute)
			tm.RepEIP = ip
		} else if tm.BaseAccessCount == 3 && !tm.ThemidaV2BySections {
			tm.ThemidaV3 = true
			log.Infof("Assuming Themida v3")
			if err := tm.SelectThemidaSection(ip); err != nil {
				tm.Fail(err)
				return
			}
			tm.ResetBreakpoint(bpa)
			tm.bp(tm.AllocMemAPI, debugger.HWExecute)
		}
	}
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) InstallCodeSectionGuard(protection uint32) {
	tm.GuardStart = tm.ImageBase + uintptr(tm.PESections[0].VirtualAddress)
	tm.GuardEnd = tm.ImageBase + tm.BaseOfData
	tm.GuardProtection = protection
	if tm.GuardEnd <= tm.GuardStart {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("invalid code section boundary"))
		return
	}
	if _, err := winapi.VirtualProtectEx(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, tm.GuardStart, tm.GuardEnd-tm.GuardStart, protection); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return
	}
	if !tm.ThemidaV3 && !tm.IsHWBreakpoint(tm.VirtualProtectAPI) {
		tm.bp(tm.VirtualProtectAPI, debugger.HWExecute)
	}
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) OnSinglestep(bpa uintptr) uint32 {
	if tm.GuardStepping {
		if _, err := winapi.VirtualProtectEx(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, tm.GuardStart, tm.GuardEnd-tm.GuardStart, tm.GuardProtection); err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
		}
		tm.GuardStepping = false
	}
	tm.EnableBreakpoints()
	return winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) OnAccessViolation(hThread syscall.Handle, exc *winapi.EXCEPTION_RECORD) uint32 {
	address := exc.ExceptionInformation[1]
	if tm.GuardStart == 0 || address < tm.GuardStart || address >= tm.GuardEnd {
		return winapi.DBG_EXCEPTION_NOT_HANDLED
	}
	if _, err := winapi.VirtualProtectEx(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, tm.GuardStart, tm.GuardEnd-tm.GuardStart, winapi.PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return winapi.DBG_EXCEPTION_NOT_HANDLED
	}
	var c winapi.CONTEXT32
	c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_32
	if err := winapi.GetThreadContext32(hThread, &c); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return winapi.DBG_EXCEPTION_NOT_HANDLED
	}
	if exc.ExceptionAddress > tm.GuardEnd {
		tm.GuardAddrs = append(tm.GuardAddrs, address)
		tm.GuardStepping = true
		c.EFlags |= 0x100
		if err := winapi.SetThreadContext32(hThread, &c); err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
		}
		return winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
	}
	if tm.TLSTotal > 0 && tm.TLSCounter < tm.TLSTotal {
		ret := uintptr(tm.read32(uintptr(c.Esp)))
		args := [3]uint32{tm.read32(uintptr(c.Esp) + 4), tm.read32(uintptr(c.Esp) + 8), tm.read32(uintptr(c.Esp) + 12)}
		if tm.TMSectR.Contains(ret) && !tm.IsTMExceptionHandler(ret) && args[0]&0xFFF == 0 && args[1] <= 3 {
			tm.TLSCounter++
			log.Goodf("TLS %d: 0x%X", tm.TLSCounter, exc.ExceptionAddress)
			c.Eip = uint32(ret)
			c.Esp += 16
			if err := winapi.SetThreadContext32(hThread, &c); err != nil {
				tm.Fail(err)
			}
			tm.InstallCodeSectionGuard(tm.GuardProtection)
			return winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
		}
	}
	oep := exc.ExceptionAddress
	oep = tm.RestoreStolenOEPForMSVC6(hThread, oep)
	if tm.IsDLL && tm.MajorLinkerVersion == 9 {
		oep = tm.RestoreStolenOEPForMSVC9DLL(oep)
	}
	tm.CheckVirtualizedOEP(oep)
	if !tm.AncientVer {
		ret := uintptr(tm.read32(uintptr(c.Esp)))
		if tm.TMSectR.Contains(ret) && !tm.IsTMExceptionHandler(ret) {
			oep = tm.TryFindCorrectOEP(oep)
		}
	}
	if tm.Err == nil {
		tm.FinishUnpacking(oep)
	}
	return winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) OnDLLLoad(name string, base uintptr) {
	if !strings.EqualFold(filepath.Base(name), "mscoree.dll") {
		return
	}
	h, err := winapi.LoadLibrary("mscoree.dll")
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return
	}
	defer winapi.FreeLibrary(h)
	if uintptr(h) != base {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("mscoree.dll loaded at a different base than target"))
		return
	}
	tm.CorExeMain, err = winapi.GetProcAddress(h, "_CorExeMain")
	if err == nil {
		err = tm.SetSoftBP(tm.CorExeMain)
	}
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
	}
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) OnUnsolicitedSoftwareBreakpoint(hThread syscall.Handle, bpa uintptr) {
	log.Infof("Unsolicited breakpoint at 0x%X", bpa)
}

func (tm *TMDebugger32) OnSoftwareBreakpoint(hThread syscall.Handle, bpa uintptr) debugger.SoftBPAction {
	var c winapi.CONTEXT32
	c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_FULL_32
	if err := winapi.GetThreadContext32(hThread, &c); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return debugger.SbpClearContinue
	}
	if !tm.Wow64 && bpa == tm.KiFastSystemCall {
		infoClass := tm.read32(uintptr(c.Esp) + 12)
		if uintptr(c.Eax) == tm.NtQIP && (infoClass == 7 || infoClass == 30) {
			ret := tm.read32(uintptr(c.Esp))
			result := uintptr(tm.read32(uintptr(c.Esp) + 16))
			tm.write(result, make([]byte, 4))
			c.Esp += 4
			c.Eip = ret
			c.Eax = winapi.STATUS_SUCCESS
			if infoClass == 30 {
				c.Eax = winapi.STATUS_PORT_NOT_SET
			}
		} else {
			c.Edx = c.Esp
			c.Eip = uint32(tm.KiFastSystemCall + 2)
		}
		if err := winapi.SetThreadContext32(hThread, &c); err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
		}
		return debugger.SbpKeepContinueNoStep
	}
	if bpa == tm.CorExeMain {
		out := dumper.ChangeFileExt(tm.Executable, "U"+filepath.Ext(tm.Executable))
		dotnet := dumper.NewDumperDotnet(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, tm.ImageBase)
		if err := dotnet.DumpToFile(out); err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
		} else {
			tm.Succeeded = true
			tm.ShouldTerminate = true
			tm.HideThreadEnd = true
			log.Goodf(".NET process dumped to %s", dotnet.OutputFile)
		}
		return debugger.SbpClearContinue
	}
	if !tm.NewVer {
		bases := tm.moduleBases()
		if c.Eax != bases[0] && c.Eax != bases[1] && c.Eax != bases[2] {
			if !tm.IsHWBreakpoint(tm.AllocHeapAPI) {
				tm.bp(tm.AllocHeapAPI, debugger.HWExecute)
			}
			return debugger.SbpKeepContinue
		}
		tm.SoftBPClear()
		tm.installOldIATPatch(uintptr(c.Eip), bases)
	} else {
		for i := range tm.EFLs {
			if tm.EFLs[i].Address == 0 {
				if tm.InstallEFLPatch(bpa, &c, &tm.EFLs[i]) {
					return debugger.SbpClearContinue
				}
				break
			}
		}
		tm.SoftBPClear()
	}
	protection := uint32(winapi.PAGE_READONLY)
	if tm.AncientVer {
		protection = winapi.PAGE_NOACCESS
	}
	tm.InstallCodeSectionGuard(protection)
	return debugger.SbpClearContinue
}

```

`pkg/themida/themida64.go`:

```go
package themida

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"syscall"

	"unmida/pkg/debugger"
	"unmida/pkg/dumper"
	"unmida/pkg/log"
	"unmida/pkg/pe"
	"unmida/pkg/utils"
	"unmida/pkg/winapi"
)

type TMDebugger64 struct {
	*TMCommon

	CloseHandleAPI  uintptr
	VirtualAllocAPI uintptr
	CorExeMain      uintptr

	GuardStart    uintptr
	GuardEnd      uintptr
	GuardStepping bool
	TMGuard       bool
	TraceMSVCOEP  bool

	MSVCInitCookie uintptr
	MSVCOEP        uintptr

	TLSCounter int
	TLSTotal   int
}

func NewDebugger64(executable, parameters string, createData bool) (*TMDebugger64, error) {
	appDir := filepath.Dir(mustExecutablePath())
	for _, name := range []string{"InjectorCLIx64.exe", "HookLibraryx64.dll", "scylla_hide.ini"} {
		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(appDir, name)); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("64-bit unpacking requires %s next to Unmida: %w", name, err)
		}
	}
	tm := &TMDebugger64{
		TMCommon: &TMCommon{
			CreateDataSections: createData,
			ThemidaV3:          true,
		},
	}
	tm.TMCommon.DebuggerCore = debugger.NewDebuggerCore(executable, parameters, tm)
	tm.Is64Bit = true
	return tm, nil
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) InImageBounds(addr uintptr) bool {
	return addr >= tm.ImageBase && addr < tm.ImageBoundary
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) SelectThemidaSection(addr uintptr) error {
	for _, sec := range tm.PESections {
		secStart := tm.ImageBase + uintptr(sec.VirtualAddress)
		secEnd := secStart + uintptr(sec.VirtualSize)
		if addr >= secStart && addr < secEnd {
			tm.TMSectR = utils.MemoryRegion{
				Address: secStart,
				Size:    uintptr(sec.VirtualSize),
			}
			tm.TMSect = make([]byte, sec.VirtualSize)
			if !tm.RPM(secStart, tm.TMSect) {
				tm.TMSect = nil
				return fmt.Errorf("RPM for Themida section failed")
			}
			log.Infof("TMSect: 0x%X (%d bytes)", tm.TMSectR.Address, tm.TMSectR.Size)
			return nil
		}
	}
	return fmt.Errorf("unable to find section for 0x%X", addr)
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) OnDebugStart(hFile syscall.Handle, hThread syscall.Handle) {
	appDir := filepath.Dir(mustExecutablePath())
	injectorPath := filepath.Join(appDir, "InjectorCLIx64.exe")
	hookLibPath := filepath.Join(appDir, "HookLibraryx64.dll")

	if _, err := os.Stat(injectorPath); err == nil {
		ts, _ := utils.GetPETimestamp(injectorPath)
		if ts < 0x6484FEF9 {
			tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("InjectorCLIx64 is older than the required fix"))
			return
		}
		log.Goodf("Applying ScyllaHide")
		cmd := exec.Command(injectorPath, fmt.Sprintf("pid:%d", tm.ProcessInfo.ProcessId), hookLibPath, "nowait")
		cmd.Dir = appDir
		cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{HideWindow: true}
		if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
			return
		}
		go cmd.Wait()
	} else {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("ScyllaHide is mandatory for Themida64 (InjectorCLIx64.exe not found)"))
		return
	}

	k32, _ := winapi.GetModuleHandle("kernel32.dll")
	tm.VirtualAllocAPI, _ = winapi.GetProcAddress(k32, "VirtualAlloc")
	tm.SleepAPI, _ = winapi.GetProcAddress(k32, "Sleep")
	tm.LstrlenAPI, _ = winapi.GetProcAddress(k32, "lstrlenA")

	tm.tmInit()
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) tmInit() {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(tm.Executable)
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("Reading executable failed: %v", err))
		return
	}
	peHeader, err := pe.ParsePEHeader(data)
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("Parsing PE header failed: %v", err))
		return
	}

	if err := tm.InitPEDetails(peHeader); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("%v", err))
		return
	}

	firstSec := peHeader.Sections[0]
	tm.BaseOfData = uintptr(firstSec.Header.VirtualAddress + peHeader.SizeOfCode)

	// PE Header Antidump fix
	if len(peHeader.Sections) > 2 && peHeader.Sections[2].Header.Name[1] == 'i' {
		sec2NameOff := int(peHeader.LFANew) + 24 + int(peHeader.FileHeader.SizeOfOptionalHeader) + 2*40 + 1
		targetAddr := tm.ImageBase + uintptr(sec2NameOff)
		winapi.VirtualProtectEx(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, targetAddr, 1, winapi.PAGE_READWRITE)
		tm.WriteByte(targetAddr, 'p')
	}

	secName := firstSec.Header.SectionName()
	if secName == ".text" {
		log.Goodf("Text section not encrypted/compressed, installing page guard")
		tm.InstallCodeSectionGuard()
	} else {
		k32, _ := winapi.GetModuleHandle("kernel32.dll")
		tm.CloseHandleAPI, _ = winapi.GetProcAddress(k32, "CloseHandle")
		if err := tm.SetBreakpoint(tm.CloseHandleAPI, debugger.HWExecute, true); err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
		}
	}

	tlsDir := peHeader.DataDirectory[winapi.IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_TLS]
	if tlsDir.Size > 0 {
		var tlsBuf [40]byte
		if tm.RPM(tm.ImageBase+uintptr(tlsDir.VirtualAddress), tlsBuf[:]) {
			addrOfCallbacks := uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(tlsBuf[24:32]))
			tlsDist := int64(tm.ImageBase+uintptr(tlsDir.VirtualAddress)) - int64(addrOfCallbacks)
			if tlsDist > 0 && tlsDist <= int64(8*(4+1)) {
				tm.TLSTotal = int(tlsDist/8) - 1
				log.Infof("[MSVC] Expecting %d TLS entries", tm.TLSTotal)
			}
		}
	}
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) InstallCodeSectionGuard() {
	tm.GuardStart = tm.ImageBase + uintptr(tm.PESections[0].VirtualAddress)
	tm.GuardEnd = tm.ImageBase + tm.BaseOfData
	if tm.GuardEnd <= tm.GuardStart {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("invalid 64-bit code boundary"))
		return
	}
	if _, err := winapi.VirtualProtectEx(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, tm.GuardStart, tm.GuardEnd-tm.GuardStart, winapi.PAGE_NOACCESS); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
	}
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) IsGuardedAddress(addr uintptr) bool {
	if tm.GuardStart == 0 {
		return false
	}
	return addr >= tm.GuardStart && addr < tm.GuardEnd
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) OnAccessViolation(hThread syscall.Handle, excRec *winapi.EXCEPTION_RECORD) uint32 {
	faultAddr := excRec.ExceptionInformation[1]
	if tm.IsGuardedAddress(faultAddr) {
		return tm.ProcessGuardedAccess(hThread, excRec)
	}
	return winapi.DBG_EXCEPTION_NOT_HANDLED
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) ProcessGuardedAccess(hThread syscall.Handle, excRec *winapi.EXCEPTION_RECORD) uint32 {
	flag := byte(excRec.ExceptionInformation[0])
	addr := excRec.ExceptionInformation[1]
	pc := excRec.ExceptionAddress

	log.Infof("[Guard] %s 0x%X", utils.AccessViolationFlagToStr(flag), addr)

	if _, err := winapi.VirtualProtectEx(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, tm.GuardStart, tm.GuardEnd-tm.GuardStart, winapi.PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return winapi.DBG_EXCEPTION_NOT_HANDLED
	}

	if tm.TMGuard {
		tm.TMGuard = false
		tm.InstallCodeSectionGuard()
	} else if !tm.InImageBounds(pc) {
		tm.GuardStepping = true
	} else if pc > tm.GuardEnd {
		if tm.TMSectR.Address == 0 {
			if err := tm.SelectThemidaSection(pc); err != nil {
				tm.Fail(err)
				return winapi.DBG_EXCEPTION_NOT_HANDLED
			}
		}
		tm.GuardAddrs = append(tm.GuardAddrs, addr)
		tm.GuardStepping = true
	} else if flag == 8 && tm.TLSTotal > 0 && tm.TLSCounter < tm.TLSTotal {
		tm.TLSCounter++
		log.Goodf("TLS %d: 0x%08X", tm.TLSCounter, pc)
		tm.GuardStart = tm.TMSectR.Address
		tm.GuardEnd = tm.ImageBoundary
		tm.TMGuard = true
		if _, err := winapi.VirtualProtectEx(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, tm.GuardStart, tm.GuardEnd-tm.GuardStart, winapi.PAGE_READWRITE); err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
			return winapi.DBG_EXCEPTION_NOT_HANDLED
		}
	} else if tm.TraceMSVCOEP {
		tm.WriteMSVCOEP(pc)
		if tm.Err == nil {
			tm.FinishUnpacking(tm.MSVCOEP)
		}
	} else {
		oep := pc
		tm.CheckVirtualizedOEP(oep)

		var c winapi.CONTEXT64
		c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_64
		if err := winapi.GetThreadContext64(hThread, &c); err == nil {
			var retBuf [8]byte
			tm.RPM(uintptr(c.Rsp), retBuf[:])
			retAddr := uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(retBuf[:]))

			if tm.TMSectR.Contains(retAddr) {
				log.Infof("Return address points into Themida section: 0x%09X", retAddr)
				oep = tm.TryFindCorrectOEP(oep)

				if tm.TraceMSVCOEP {
					tm.MSVCOEP = oep
					c.Rip = uint64(retAddr)
					c.Rsp += 8
					winapi.SetThreadContext64(hThread, &c)
					tm.InstallCodeSectionGuard()
					return winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
				}
			} else {
				log.Goodf("OEP: 0x%08X", oep)
			}
		} else {
			tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("GetThreadContext64 failed for further OEP check: %v", err))
		}

		if tm.Err == nil {
			tm.FinishUnpacking(oep)
		}
	}

	if tm.GuardStepping {
		var c winapi.CONTEXT64
		c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_64
		if err := winapi.GetThreadContext64(hThread, &c); err == nil {
			c.EFlags |= 0x100
			winapi.SetThreadContext64(hThread, &c)
		}
	}

	return winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) TryFindCorrectOEP(hitAddr uintptr) uintptr {
	if tm.MajorLinkerVersion != 9 && tm.MajorLinkerVersion != 10 &&
		tm.MajorLinkerVersion != 11 && tm.MajorLinkerVersion != 12 && tm.MajorLinkerVersion != 14 {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("Don't know what to do about OEP for this compiler. Your target likely won't run."))
		return hitAddr
	}

	textLen := int(tm.BaseOfData - uintptr(tm.PESections[0].VirtualAddress))
	textBuf := make([]byte, textLen)
	textStart := tm.ImageBase + uintptr(tm.PESections[0].VirtualAddress)

	if tm.RPM(textStart, textBuf) {
		scanFor := uint32(hitAddr - textStart)
		for i := 0; i <= textLen-10; i++ {
			if textBuf[i] == 0xE8 && textBuf[i+5] == 0xE9 {
				rel := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(textBuf[i+1 : i+5])
				if rel+uint32(i)+5 == scanFor {
					oep := textStart + uintptr(i)
					log.Goodf("Found suitable real OEP 0x%09X", oep)
					return oep
				}
			}
		}
	}

	if len(tm.GuardAddrs) >= 2 && tm.GuardAddrs[len(tm.GuardAddrs)-1] == tm.GuardAddrs[len(tm.GuardAddrs)-2]+1 {
		tm.MSVCInitCookie = hitAddr
		tm.TraceMSVCOEP = true
		return tm.GuardAddrs[len(tm.GuardAddrs)-2]
	}

	tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("real OEP not found"))
	return hitAddr
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) WriteMSVCOEP(crtStartup uintptr) {
	// sub rsp, 28h
	// call msvcInitCookie
	// add rsp, 28h
	// jmp crtStartup
	subRsp := uint32(0x28EC8348)
	callRel := int32(tm.MSVCInitCookie - (tm.MSVCOEP + 4) - 5)
	addRsp := uint32(0x28C48348)
	jmpRel := int32(crtStartup - (tm.MSVCOEP + 4 + 5 + 4) - 5)

	buf := new(bytes.Buffer)
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, subRsp)
	buf.WriteByte(0xE8)
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, callRel)
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, addRsp)
	buf.WriteByte(0xE9)
	binary.Write(buf, binary.LittleEndian, jmpRel)

	if err := tm.WriteMemory(tm.MSVCOEP, buf.Bytes()); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(err)
		return
	}

	log.Goodf("Virtualized MSVC9+ OEP restored: 0x%X", tm.MSVCOEP)
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) FinishUnpacking(oep uintptr) {
	dmp := dumper.NewDumper(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, tm.ProcessInfo.ProcessId, tm.Executable, tm.ImageBase, oep, tm.Is64Bit)

	iat, err := tm.DetermineIATAddress(oep, dmp)
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("DetermineIATAddress error: %v", err))
		return
	}
	log.Goodf("IAT: 0x%08X", iat)

	if err := tm.TraceImports(iat, dmp); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("TraceImports error: %v", err))
		return
	}

	dmp.IAT = iat
	peHdr, err := dmp.Process()
	if err != nil {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("Dumper Process error: %v", err))
		return
	}

	outFN := dumper.ChangeFileExt(tm.Executable, "U"+filepath.Ext(tm.Executable))
	if err := dmp.DumpToFile(outFN, peHdr, tm.IsDLL); err != nil {
		tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("DumpToFile error: %v", err))
		return
	}

	outFN = dmp.OutputFile
	tm.Succeeded = true
	tm.HideThreadEnd = true
	winapi.TerminateProcess(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, 0)
	tm.ShouldTerminate = true

	log.Goodf("Operation completed successfully. Output saved to: %s", outFN)
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) OnHardwareBreakpoint(hThread syscall.Handle, bpa uintptr, c32 *winapi.CONTEXT32, c64 *winapi.CONTEXT64) {
	rip := uintptr(c64.Rip)

	if rip == tm.CloseHandleAPI {
		var buf [8]byte
		tm.RPM(uintptr(c64.Rsp), buf[:])
		retAddr := uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(buf[:]))
		log.Infof("CloseHandle called from 0x%X", retAddr)

		if tm.InImageBounds(retAddr) {
			tm.ResetBreakpoint(tm.CloseHandleAPI)
			if err := tm.SetBreakpoint(tm.ImageBase+0x1000, debugger.HWWrite, true); err != nil {
				tm.Fail(err)
			}
		}
	} else if rip == tm.VirtualAllocAPI {
		var buf [8]byte
		tm.RPM(uintptr(c64.Rsp), buf[:])
		retAddr := uintptr(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(buf[:]))
		log.Infof("AllocMem called from 0x%X", retAddr)

		if tm.InImageBounds(retAddr) {
			tm.ResetBreakpoint(tm.VirtualAllocAPI)
			tm.InstallCodeSectionGuard()
		}
	} else if bpa == tm.ImageBase+0x1000 {
		log.Goodf("Wrote to .text base from 0x%X", rip)
		if tm.TMSectR.Address == 0 {
			if err := tm.SelectThemidaSection(rip); err != nil {
				tm.Fail(err)
				return
			}
		}
		tm.ResetBreakpoint(tm.ImageBase + 0x1000)
		if err := tm.SetBreakpoint(tm.VirtualAllocAPI, debugger.HWExecute, true); err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
		}
	} else {
		log.Infof("Accessed 0x%X from 0x%X", bpa, rip)
	}
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) OnSoftwareBreakpoint(hThread syscall.Handle, bpa uintptr) debugger.SoftBPAction {
	if bpa == tm.CorExeMain {
		dDotnet := dumper.NewDumperDotnet(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, tm.ImageBase)
		outFN := dumper.ChangeFileExt(tm.Executable, "U"+filepath.Ext(tm.Executable))
		if err := dDotnet.DumpToFile(outFN); err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
			return debugger.SbpClearContinue
		}
		log.Goodf(".NET process dumped to %s", dDotnet.OutputFile)

		tm.Succeeded = true
		tm.HideThreadEnd = true
		winapi.TerminateProcess(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, 0)
		tm.ShouldTerminate = true
		return debugger.SbpClearContinue
	}

	tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("Unexpected SoftBP at 0x%X", bpa))
	return debugger.SbpClearContinue
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) OnSinglestep(bpa uintptr) uint32 {
	if tm.GuardStepping {
		if _, err := winapi.VirtualProtectEx(tm.ProcessInfo.Process, tm.GuardStart, tm.GuardEnd-tm.GuardStart, winapi.PAGE_NOACCESS); err != nil {
			tm.Fail(err)
		}
		tm.GuardStepping = false
		return winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
	}
	tm.EnableBreakpoints()
	return winapi.DBG_CONTINUE
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) OnDLLLoad(fileName string, baseAddress uintptr) {
	if strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(fileName), "mscoree.dll") {
		log.Infof("This might be a .NET program - setting _CorExeMain BP")
		hCorEE, err := winapi.LoadLibrary("mscoree.dll")
		if err == nil {
			defer winapi.FreeLibrary(hCorEE)
		}
		if err == nil && uintptr(hCorEE) == baseAddress {
			tm.CorExeMain, err = winapi.GetProcAddress(hCorEE, "_CorExeMain")
			if err == nil {
				err = tm.SetSoftBP(tm.CorExeMain)
			}
			if err != nil {
				tm.Fail(err)
			}
		} else {
			tm.Fail(fmt.Errorf("mscoree.dll was loaded at different base than in target!"))
		}
	}
}

func (tm *TMDebugger64) OnUnsolicitedSoftwareBreakpoint(hThread syscall.Handle, bpa uintptr) {
	log.Infof("Unsolicited int32/int3 at 0x%X", bpa)
}

func mustExecutablePath() string {
	path, err := os.Executable()
	if err != nil {
		return os.Args[0]
	}
	return path
}

```

`pkg/tracer/tracer.go`:

```go
package tracer

import (
	"fmt"
	"unmida/pkg/winapi"
	"syscall"
)

type PredicateFunc func(tracer *Tracer, c32 *winapi.CONTEXT32, c64 *winapi.CONTEXT64) bool

type Tracer struct {
	ProcessID, ThreadID uint32
	ThreadHandle        syscall.Handle
	Is64Bit             bool
	Predicate           PredicateFunc
	Counter, Limit      uint32
	LimitReached        bool
	StartAddress        uintptr
	Pending             bool
}

func NewTracer(processID, threadID uint32, threadHandle syscall.Handle, is64Bit bool, predicate PredicateFunc) *Tracer {
	return &Tracer{ProcessID: processID, ThreadID: threadID, ThreadHandle: threadHandle, Is64Bit: is64Bit, Predicate: predicate}
}

func (t *Tracer) Trace(address uintptr, limit uint32) error {
	t.Counter, t.Limit, t.StartAddress = 0, limit, address
	t.LimitReached, t.Pending = false, true
	if t.Is64Bit {
		var c winapi.CONTEXT64
		c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_64
		if err := winapi.GetThreadContext64(t.ThreadHandle, &c); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		c.Rip = uint64(address)
		c.EFlags |= 0x100
		if err := winapi.SetThreadContext64(t.ThreadHandle, &c); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	} else {
		var c winapi.CONTEXT32
		c.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_32
		if err := winapi.GetThreadContext32(t.ThreadHandle, &c); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		c.Eip = uint32(address)
		c.EFlags |= 0x100
		if err := winapi.SetThreadContext32(t.ThreadHandle, &c); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	if err := winapi.ContinueDebugEvent(t.ProcessID, t.ThreadID, winapi.DBG_CONTINUE); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	t.Pending = false
	suspended := make(map[uint32]syscall.Handle)
	defer func() {
		for _, h := range suspended {
			winapi.ResumeThread(h)
			winapi.CloseHandle(h)
		}
	}()
	for {
		var event winapi.DEBUG_EVENT
		if err := winapi.WaitForDebugEvent(&event, 0xFFFFFFFF); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if event.DwDebugEventCode == winapi.EXIT_PROCESS_DEBUG_EVENT {
			winapi.ContinueDebugEvent(event.DwProcessId, event.DwThreadId, winapi.DBG_CONTINUE)
			return fmt.Errorf("target exited during import tracing")
		}
		if event.DwThreadId != t.ThreadID {
			switch event.DwDebugEventCode {
			case winapi.CREATE_THREAD_DEBUG_EVENT:
				winapi.CloseHandle(event.CreateThread().HThread)
			case winapi.LOAD_DLL_DEBUG_EVENT:
				winapi.CloseHandle(event.LoadDll().HFile)
			}
			if _, ok := suspended[event.DwThreadId]; !ok && event.DwDebugEventCode != winapi.EXIT_THREAD_DEBUG_EVENT {
				h, err := winapi.OpenThread(winapi.THREAD_SUSPEND_RESUME, false, event.DwThreadId)
				if err != nil {
					winapi.ContinueDebugEvent(event.DwProcessId, event.DwThreadId, winapi.DBG_CONTINUE)
					return err
				}
				if _, err = winapi.SuspendThread(h); err != nil {
					winapi.CloseHandle(h)
					winapi.ContinueDebugEvent(event.DwProcessId, event.DwThreadId, winapi.DBG_CONTINUE)
					return err
				}
				suspended[event.DwThreadId] = h
			}
			status := uint32(winapi.DBG_CONTINUE)
			if event.DwDebugEventCode == winapi.EXCEPTION_DEBUG_EVENT {
				status = winapi.DBG_EXCEPTION_NOT_HANDLED
			}
			if err := winapi.ContinueDebugEvent(event.DwProcessId, event.DwThreadId, status); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			continue
		}
		t.Pending = true
		if event.DwDebugEventCode == winapi.EXCEPTION_DEBUG_EVENT {
			exc := event.Exception().ExceptionRecord
			if exc.ExceptionCode != winapi.EXCEPTION_SINGLE_STEP {
				return fmt.Errorf("exception 0x%08X at 0x%X during import trace", exc.ExceptionCode, exc.ExceptionAddress)
			}
			stop, err := t.onSingleStep()
			if err != nil || stop {
				return err
			}
		} else {
			switch event.DwDebugEventCode {
			case winapi.LOAD_DLL_DEBUG_EVENT:
				winapi.CloseHandle(event.LoadDll().HFile)
			case winapi.EXIT_THREAD_DEBUG_EVENT:
				return fmt.Errorf("traced thread exited")
			}
		}
		if err := winapi.ContinueDebugEvent(event.DwProcessId, event.DwThreadId, winapi.DBG_CONTINUE); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		t.Pending = false
	}
}

func (t *Tracer) onSingleStep() (bool, error) {
	t.Counter++
	var c32 winapi.CONTEXT32
	var c64 winapi.CONTEXT64
	if t.Is64Bit {
		c64.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_64 | winapi.CONTEXT_INTEGER_64
		if err := winapi.GetThreadContext64(t.ThreadHandle, &c64); err != nil {
			return true, err
		}
	} else {
		c32.ContextFlags = winapi.CONTEXT_CONTROL_32 | winapi.CONTEXT_INTEGER_32
		if err := winapi.GetThreadContext32(t.ThreadHandle, &c32); err != nil {
			return true, err
		}
	}
	if t.Limit != 0 && t.Counter > t.Limit {
		t.LimitReached = true
	}
	stop := t.LimitReached
	if !stop && t.Predicate != nil {
		if t.Is64Bit {
			stop = t.Predicate(t, nil, &c64)
		} else {
			stop = t.Predicate(t, &c32, nil)
		}
	}
	if t.Is64Bit {
		c64.EFlags &^= 0x100
		if !stop {
			c64.EFlags |= 0x100
		}
		if err := winapi.SetThreadContext64(t.ThreadHandle, &c64); err != nil {
			return true, err
		}
	} else {
		c32.EFlags &^= 0x100
		if !stop {
			c32.EFlags |= 0x100
		}
		if err := winapi.SetThreadContext32(t.ThreadHandle, &c32); err != nil {
			return true, err
		}
	}
	if t.LimitReached {
		return true, fmt.Errorf("import trace exceeded %d instructions", t.Limit)
	}
	return stop, nil
}
```

`pkg/utils/utils.go`:

```go
package utils

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/hex"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"strings"
	"syscall"
	"unsafe"
)

var (
	ntdll         = syscall.NewLazyDLL("ntdll.dll")
	procRtlGetVer = ntdll.NewProc("RtlGetVersion")
)

type RTL_OSVERSIONINFOW struct {
	OSVersionInfoSize uint32
	MajorVersion      uint32
	MinorVersion      uint32
	BuildNumber       uint32
	PlatformId        uint32
	CSDVersion        [128]uint16
}

// MemoryRegion represents a memory range [Address, Address+Size).
type MemoryRegion struct {
	Address uintptr
	Size    uintptr
}

func (m MemoryRegion) Contains(addr uintptr) bool {
	return addr >= m.Address && addr < m.Address+m.Size
}

func AccessViolationFlagToStr(flag byte) string {
	switch flag {
	case 0:
		return "Read"
	case 1:
		return "Write"
	case 8:
		return "Execute"
	default:
		return fmt.Sprintf("%d", flag)
	}
}

type patternElem struct {
	val        byte
	isWildcard bool
}

// ParsePattern parses a hex pattern where '?' represents wildcard nibbles.
func parsePattern(pattern string) ([]patternElem, error) {
	clean := strings.ReplaceAll(pattern, " ", "")
	if len(clean)%2 != 0 {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid pattern length: %d", len(clean))
	}
	elems := make([]patternElem, len(clean)/2)
	for i := 0; i < len(clean); i += 2 {
		pair := clean[i : i+2]
		if pair == "??" {
			elems[i/2] = patternElem{isWildcard: true}
		} else {
			b, err := hex.DecodeString(pair)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid hex in pattern '%s': %w", pair, err)
			}
			elems[i/2] = patternElem{val: b[0], isWildcard: false}
		}
	}
	return elems, nil
}

// FindDynamic searches for a pattern containing wildcards '?' in buf.
// Returns the offset in buf, or -1 if not found.
func FindDynamic(pattern string, buf []byte) int {
	elems, err := parsePattern(pattern)
	if err != nil || len(elems) == 0 || len(buf) < len(elems) {
		return -1
	}

	max := len(buf) - len(elems)
	for i := 0; i <= max; i++ {
		match := true
		for j := 0; j < len(elems); j++ {
			if !elems[j].isWildcard && buf[i+j] != elems[j].val {
				match = false
				break
			}
		}
		if match {
			return i
		}
	}
	return -1
}

// FindStatic searches for an exact hex pattern in buf.
// Returns the offset in buf, or -1 if not found.
func FindStatic(pattern string, buf []byte) int {
	clean := strings.ReplaceAll(pattern, " ", "")
	b, err := hex.DecodeString(clean)
	if err != nil || len(b) == 0 || len(buf) < len(b) {
		return -1
	}

	max := len(buf) - len(b)
	for i := 0; i <= max; i++ {
		match := true
		for j := 0; j < len(b); j++ {
			if buf[i+j] != b[j] {
				match = false
				break
			}
		}
		if match {
			return i
		}
	}
	return -1
}

// GetWindowsBuildNumber returns the Windows build number using RtlGetVersion.
func GetWindowsBuildNumber() uint32 {
	var info RTL_OSVERSIONINFOW
	info.OSVersionInfoSize = uint32(unsafe.Sizeof(info))
	r, _, _ := procRtlGetVer.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&info)))
	if r == 0 {
		return info.BuildNumber
	}
	return 0
}

// GetPETimestamp returns the TimeDateStamp from the PE header of the given file.
func GetPETimestamp(filename string) (uint32, error) {
	f, err := os.Open(filename)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	defer f.Close()

	var header [0x1000]byte
	n, err := f.Read(header[:])
	if err != nil || n < 0x40 {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("unable to read PE header: %w", err)
	}

	// DOS header magic "MZ"
	if header[0] != 'M' || header[1] != 'Z' {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("not a valid PE DOS header")
	}

	lfanew := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(header[0x3C:0x40])
	if lfanew > 0xF00 || int(lfanew)+24 > n {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("invalid lfanew offset: 0x%X", lfanew)
	}

	// NT signature "PE\0\0"
	ntSig := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(header[lfanew : lfanew+4])
	if ntSig != 0x00004550 {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("invalid PE signature: 0x%X", ntSig)
	}

	// FileHeader is at lfanew + 4, TimeDateStamp is at offset +4 inside FileHeader (i.e. lfanew + 8)
	ts := binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(header[lfanew+8 : lfanew+12])
	return ts, nil
}
```

```go
package winapi

import (
	"fmt"
	"runtime"
	"syscall"
	"unsafe"
)

var (
	modKernel32 = syscall.NewLazyDLL("kernel32.dll")
	modNtdll    = syscall.NewLazyDLL("ntdll.dll")

	procWaitForDebugEvent        = modKernel32.NewProc("WaitForDebugEvent")
	procContinueDebugEvent       = modKernel32.NewProc("ContinueDebugEvent")
	procDebugActiveProcess       = modKernel32.NewProc("DebugActiveProcess")
	procDebugActiveProcessStop   = modKernel32.NewProc("DebugActiveProcessStop")
	procCreateProcessW           = modKernel32.NewProc("CreateProcessW")
	procOpenProcess              = modKernel32.NewProc("OpenProcess")
	procOpenThread               = modKernel32.NewProc("OpenThread")
	procSuspendThread            = modKernel32.NewProc("SuspendThread")
	procResumeThread             = modKernel32.NewProc("ResumeThread")
	procGetThreadContext         = modKernel32.NewProc("GetThreadContext")
	procSetThreadContext         = modKernel32.NewProc("SetThreadContext")
	procWow64GetThreadContext    = modKernel32.NewProc("Wow64GetThreadContext")
	procWow64SetThreadContext    = modKernel32.NewProc("Wow64SetThreadContext")
	procReadProcessMemory        = modKernel32.NewProc("ReadProcessMemory")
	procWriteProcessMemory       = modKernel32.NewProc("WriteProcessMemory")
	procVirtualAllocEx           = modKernel32.NewProc("VirtualAllocEx")
	procVirtualProtectEx         = modKernel32.NewProc("VirtualProtectEx")
	procVirtualQueryEx           = modKernel32.NewProc("VirtualQueryEx")
	procFlushInstructionCache    = modKernel32.NewProc("FlushInstructionCache")
	procTerminateProcess         = modKernel32.NewProc("TerminateProcess")
	procCloseHandle              = modKernel32.NewProc("CloseHandle")
	procCreateToolhelp32Snapshot = modKernel32.NewProc("CreateToolhelp32Snapshot")
	procModule32FirstW           = modKernel32.NewProc("Module32FirstW")
	procModule32NextW            = modKernel32.NewProc("Module32NextW")
	procIsWow64Process           = modKernel32.NewProc("IsWow64Process")
	procGetProcAddress           = modKernel32.NewProc("GetProcAddress")
	procGetModuleHandleW         = modKernel32.NewProc("GetModuleHandleW")
	procLoadLibraryW             = modKernel32.NewProc("LoadLibraryW")
	procLoadLibraryExW           = modKernel32.NewProc("LoadLibraryExW")
	procFreeLibrary              = modKernel32.NewProc("FreeLibrary")

	procNtQueryInformationProcess = modNtdll.NewProc("NtQueryInformationProcess")
	procNtQueryInformationThread  = modNtdll.NewProc("NtQueryInformationThread")

func WaitForDebugEvent(event *DEBUG_EVENT, timeout uint32) error {
	r, _, err := procWaitForDebugEvent.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(event)), uintptr(timeout))
	if r == 0 {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func ContinueDebugEvent(processID, threadID, continueStatus uint32) error {
	r, _, err := procContinueDebugEvent.Call(uintptr(processID), uintptr(threadID), uintptr(continueStatus))
	if r == 0 {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func DebugActiveProcess(processID uint32) error {
	r, _, err := procDebugActiveProcess.Call(uintptr(processID))
	if r == 0 {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func DebugActiveProcessStop(processID uint32) error {
	r, _, err := procDebugActiveProcessStop.Call(uintptr(processID))
	if r == 0 {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func CreateProcess(appName, cmdLine string, currentDir string, flags uint32, si *syscall.StartupInfo, pi *syscall.ProcessInformation) error {
	var pApp, pCmd, pDir *uint16
	var err error
	if appName != "" {
		pApp, err = syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(appName)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	if cmdLine != "" {
		pCmd, err = syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(cmdLine)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	if currentDir != "" {
		pDir, err = syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(currentDir)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	r, _, err := procCreateProcessW.Call(
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(pApp)),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(pCmd)),
		0, 0,
		0, // bInheritHandles = FALSE
		uintptr(flags),
		0,
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(pDir)),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(si)),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(pi)),
	)
	if r == 0 {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func OpenProcess(desiredAccess uint32, inheritHandle bool, processId uint32) (syscall.Handle, error) {
	var inherit uintptr
	if inheritHandle {
		inherit = 1
	}
	r, _, err := procOpenProcess.Call(uintptr(desiredAccess), inherit, uintptr(processId))
	if r == 0 {
		return 0, err
	}
	return syscall.Handle(r), nil
}

func OpenThread(desiredAccess uint32, inheritHandle bool, threadId uint32) (syscall.Handle, error) {
	var inherit uintptr
	if inheritHandle {
		inherit = 1
	}
	r, _, err := procOpenThread.Call(uintptr(desiredAccess), inherit, uintptr(threadId))
	if r == 0 {
		return 0, err
	}
	return syscall.Handle(r), nil
}

func SuspendThread(hThread syscall.Handle) (uint32, error) {
	r, _, err := procSuspendThread.Call(uintptr(hThread))
	if int32(r) == -1 {
		return 0, err
	}
	return uint32(r), nil
}

func ResumeThread(hThread syscall.Handle) (uint32, error) {
	r, _, err := procResumeThread.Call(uintptr(hThread))
	if int32(r) == -1 {
		return 0, err
	}
	return uint32(r), nil
}

func GetThreadContext64(hThread syscall.Handle, ctx *CONTEXT64) error {
	storage := make([]byte, int(unsafe.Sizeof(*ctx))+15)
	offset := (16 - uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&storage[0]))%16) % 16
	aligned := (*CONTEXT64)(unsafe.Pointer(&storage[offset]))
	*aligned = *ctx
	r, _, err := procGetThreadContext.Call(uintptr(hThread), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(aligned)))
	*ctx = *aligned
	runtime.KeepAlive(storage)
	if r == 0 {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func SetThreadContext64(hThread syscall.Handle, ctx *CONTEXT64) error {
	storage := make([]byte, int(unsafe.Sizeof(*ctx))+15)
	offset := (16 - uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&storage[0]))%16) % 16
	aligned := (*CONTEXT64)(unsafe.Pointer(&storage[offset]))
	*aligned = *ctx
	r, _, err := procSetThreadContext.Call(uintptr(hThread), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(aligned)))
	runtime.KeepAlive(storage)
	if r == 0 {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func GetThreadContext32(hThread syscall.Handle, ctx *CONTEXT32) error {
	// If running as 32-bit process, use standard GetThreadContext; if 64-bit host, use Wow64GetThreadContext
	var r uintptr
	var err error
	if unsafe.Sizeof(uintptr(0)) == 8 {
		r, _, err = procWow64GetThreadContext.Call(uintptr(hThread), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(ctx)))
	} else {
		r, _, err = procGetThreadContext.Call(uintptr(hThread), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(ctx)))
	}
	if r == 0 {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func SetThreadContext32(hThread syscall.Handle, ctx *CONTEXT32) error {
	var r uintptr
	var err error
	if unsafe.Sizeof(uintptr(0)) == 8 {
		r, _, err = procWow64SetThreadContext.Call(uintptr(hThread), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(ctx)))
	} else {
		r, _, err = procSetThreadContext.Call(uintptr(hThread), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(ctx)))
	}
	if r == 0 {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func ReadProcessMemory(hProcess syscall.Handle, baseAddr uintptr, buf []byte) (int, error) {
	if len(buf) == 0 {
		return 0, nil
	}
	var read uintptr
	r, _, err := procReadProcessMemory.Call(
		uintptr(hProcess),
		baseAddr,
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&buf[0])),
		uintptr(len(buf)),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&read)),
	)
	if r == 0 {
		return int(read), err
	}
	if read != uintptr(len(buf)) {
		return int(read), fmt.Errorf("short process read: %d of %d bytes", read, len(buf))
	}
	return int(read), nil
}

func WriteProcessMemory(hProcess syscall.Handle, baseAddr uintptr, buf []byte) (int, error) {
	if len(buf) == 0 {
		return 0, nil
	}
	var written uintptr
	r, _, err := procWriteProcessMemory.Call(
		uintptr(hProcess),
		baseAddr,
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&buf[0])),
		uintptr(len(buf)),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&written)),
	)
	if r == 0 {
		return int(written), err
	}
	if written != uintptr(len(buf)) {
		return int(written), fmt.Errorf("short process write: %d of %d bytes", written, len(buf))
	}
	return int(written), nil
}

func VirtualAllocEx(hProcess syscall.Handle, addr uintptr, size uintptr, allocType, protect uint32) (uintptr, error) {
	r, _, err := procVirtualAllocEx.Call(uintptr(hProcess), addr, size, uintptr(allocType), uintptr(protect))
	if r == 0 {
		return 0, err
	}
	return r, nil
}

func VirtualProtectEx(hProcess syscall.Handle, addr uintptr, size uintptr, newProtect uint32) (uint32, error) {
	var oldProtect uint32
	r, _, err := procVirtualProtectEx.Call(
		uintptr(hProcess),
		addr,
		size,
		uintptr(newProtect),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&oldProtect)),
	)
	if r == 0 {
		return 0, err
	}
	return oldProtect, nil
}

func VirtualQueryEx(hProcess syscall.Handle, addr uintptr, mbi *MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION) (int, error) {
	r, _, err := procVirtualQueryEx.Call(
		uintptr(hProcess),
		addr,
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(mbi)),
		uintptr(unsafe.Sizeof(*mbi)),
	)
	if r == 0 {
		return 0, err
	}
	return int(r), nil
}

func FlushInstructionCache(hProcess syscall.Handle, addr uintptr, size uintptr) error {
	r, _, err := procFlushInstructionCache.Call(uintptr(hProcess), addr, size)
	if r == 0 {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

	if r == 0 {

	}
	return nil

	return syscall.CloseHandle(h)
}

func CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(flags uint32, processID uint32) (syscall.Handle, error) {
	r, _, err := procCreateToolhelp32Snapshot.Call(uintptr(flags), uintptr(processID))
	if syscall.Handle(r) == syscall.InvalidHandle {
		return 0, err
	}
	return syscall.Handle(r), nil
}

func Module32First(hSnapshot syscall.Handle, me *MODULEENTRY32W) error {
	me.DwSize = uint32(unsafe.Sizeof(*me))
	r, _, err := procModule32FirstW.Call(uintptr(hSnapshot), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(me)))
	if r == 0 {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

	me.DwSize = uint32(unsafe.Sizeof(*me))
	r, _, err := procModule32NextW.Call(uintptr(hSnapshot), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(me)))
	if r == 0 {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func IsWow64Process(hProcess syscall.Handle) (bool, error) {
	var isWow64 int32
	r, _, err := procIsWow64Process.Call(uintptr(hProcess), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&isWow64)))
	if r == 0 {
		return false, err
	}
	return isWow64 != 0, nil
}

func GetModuleHandle(name string) (syscall.Handle, error) {
	if name == "" {
		r, _, err := procGetModuleHandleW.Call(0)
		if r == 0 {
			return 0, err
		}
		return syscall.Handle(r), nil
	}
	pName, err := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(name)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	r, _, err := procGetModuleHandleW.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(pName)))
	if r == 0 {
		return 0, err
	}
	return syscall.Handle(r), nil
}

func LoadLibrary(name string) (syscall.Handle, error) {
	pName, err := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(name)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	r, _, err := procLoadLibraryW.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(pName)))
	if r == 0 {
		return 0, err
	}
	return syscall.Handle(r), nil
}

func LoadLibraryEx(name string, flags uint32) (syscall.Handle, error) {
	pName, err := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(name)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	r, _, err := procLoadLibraryExW.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(pName)), 0, uintptr(flags))
	if r == 0 {
		return 0, err
	}
	return syscall.Handle(r), nil
}

func FreeLibrary(hModule syscall.Handle) error {
	r, _, err := procFreeLibrary.Call(uintptr(hModule))
	if r == 0 {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func GetProcAddress(hModule syscall.Handle, procName string) (uintptr, error) {
	pName, err := syscall.BytePtrFromString(procName)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	r, _, err := procGetProcAddress.Call(uintptr(hModule), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(pName)))
	if r == 0 {
		return 0, err
	}
	return r, nil
}

func NtQueryInformationProcess(hProcess syscall.Handle, infoClass uint32, info unsafe.Pointer, infoLen uint32, retLen *uint32) error {
	r, _, _ := procNtQueryInformationProcess.Call(
		uintptr(hProcess),
		uintptr(infoClass),
		uintptr(info),
		uintptr(infoLen),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(retLen)),
	)
	if r != 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("NtQueryInformationProcess failed: 0x%08X", r)
	}
	return nil
}

func DebugModuleFileName(file, process syscall.Handle, base uintptr) string {
	buffer := make([]uint16, 32768)
	if file != 0 && file != syscall.InvalidHandle {
		length, _, _ := modKernel32.NewProc("GetFinalPathNameByHandleW").Call(uintptr(file), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&buffer[0])), uintptr(len(buffer)), 0)
		if length > 0 && length < uintptr(len(buffer)) {
			return syscall.UTF16ToString(buffer[:length])
		}
	}
	length, _, _ := syscall.NewLazyDLL("psapi.dll").NewProc("GetMappedFileNameW").Call(uintptr(process), base, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&buffer[0])), uintptr(len(buffer)))
	if length > 0 && length < uintptr(len(buffer)) {
		return syscall.UTF16ToString(buffer[:length])
	}
	return ""
}
```

```go
	DwDebugInfoFileOffset uint32
	NNumberOfBytesToMap   uint32
	LpImageName           uintptr
	FUnicode              uint16
}

type UNLOAD_DLL_DEBUG_INFO struct {
	LpBaseOfDll uintptr
}

type OUTPUT_DEBUG_STRING_INFO struct {
	LpDebugStringData  uintptr
	FUnicode           uint16
	NDebugStringLength uint16
}

type RIP_INFO struct {
	DwError uint32
	DwType  uint32
}

type DEBUG_EVENT struct {
	DwDebugEventCode uint32
	DwProcessId      uint32
	DwThreadId       uint32
	_                [0]uintptr
	U                [160]byte
}

func (de *DEBUG_EVENT) Exception() *EXCEPTION_DEBUG_INFO {
	return (*EXCEPTION_DEBUG_INFO)(unsafe.Pointer(&de.U[0]))
}

func (de *DEBUG_EVENT) CreateThread() *CREATE_THREAD_DEBUG_INFO {
	return (*CREATE_THREAD_DEBUG_INFO)(unsafe.Pointer(&de.U[0]))
}

func (de *DEBUG_EVENT) CreateProcessInfo() *CREATE_PROCESS_DEBUG_INFO {
	return (*CREATE_PROCESS_DEBUG_INFO)(unsafe.Pointer(&de.U[0]))
}

func (de *DEBUG_EVENT) ExitThread() *EXIT_THREAD_DEBUG_INFO {
	return (*EXIT_THREAD_DEBUG_INFO)(unsafe.Pointer(&de.U[0]))
}

func (de *DEBUG_EVENT) ExitProcess() *EXIT_PROCESS_DEBUG_INFO {
	return (*EXIT_PROCESS_DEBUG_INFO)(unsafe.Pointer(&de.U[0]))
}

func (de *DEBUG_EVENT) LoadDll() *LOAD_DLL_DEBUG_INFO {
	return (*LOAD_DLL_DEBUG_INFO)(unsafe.Pointer(&de.U[0]))
}

func (de *DEBUG_EVENT) UnloadDll() *UNLOAD_DLL_DEBUG_INFO {
	return (*UNLOAD_DLL_DEBUG_INFO)(unsafe.Pointer(&de.U[0]))
}

func (de *DEBUG_EVENT) DebugString() *OUTPUT_DEBUG_STRING_INFO {
	return (*OUTPUT_DEBUG_STRING_INFO)(unsafe.Pointer(&de.U[0]))
}

func (de *DEBUG_EVENT) RipInfo() *RIP_INFO {
	return (*RIP_INFO)(unsafe.Pointer(&de.U[0]))
}

type PROCESS_BASIC_INFORMATION struct {
	ExitStatus                   uint32
	PebBaseAddress               uintptr
	AffinityMask                 uintptr
	BasePriority                 int32
	UniqueProcessId              uintptr
	InheritedFromUniqueProcessId uintptr
}

type MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION struct {
	BaseAddress       uintptr
	AllocationBase    uintptr
	AllocationProtect uint32
	RegionSize        uintptr
	State             uint32
	Protect           uint32
	Type              uint32
}

type MODULEENTRY32W struct {
	DwSize        uint32
	Th32ModuleID  uint32
	Th32ProcessID uint32
	GlblcntUsage  uint32
	ProccntUsage  uint32
	ModBaseAddr   uintptr
	ModBaseSize   uint32
	HModule       syscall.Handle
	SzModule      [256]uint16
	SzExePath     [260]uint16
}
```
